===== [10/66] AI_Music/app/src/AudioEditRunner.cs  (1420 bytes) =====
using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;

namespace AIMusicGenerator
{
    internal static class AudioEditRunner
    {
        public static int Run(ProcessStartInfo startInfo, Action<string> log)
        {
            if (!File.Exists(Paths.AudioEditTool))
            {
                log("=== 找不到音訊工具: " + Paths.AudioEditTool + " ===");
                return 2;
            }
            try
            {
                using (Process process = new Process())
                {
                    process.StartInfo = startInfo;
                    process.OutputDataReceived += delegate(object sender, DataReceivedEventArgs e)
                    {
                        if (e.Data != null) log(e.Data);
                    };
                    process.ErrorDataReceived += delegate(object sender, DataReceivedEventArgs e)
                    {
                        if (e.Data != null) log("[audio] " + e.Data);
                    };
                    process.Start();
                    process.BeginOutputReadLine();
                    process.BeginErrorReadLine();
                    process.WaitForExit();
                    return process.ExitCode;
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                log("=== 音訊工具啟動失敗: " + ex.Message + " ===");
                return 1;
            }
        }
    }
}


===== [11/66] AI_Music/app/src/AudioTools.cs  (4638 bytes) =====
using System;
using System.IO;
using NAudio.Wave;
using TagLib;

namespace AIMusicGenerator
{
    internal static class AudioTools
    {
        public static WaveStream OpenReader(string path)
        {
            try
            {
                return new AudioFileReader(path);
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
                return new MediaFoundationReader(path);
            }
        }

        public static float[] BuildPeaks(string path, int buckets, out double seconds)
        {
            using (WaveStream reader = OpenReader(path))
            {
                ISampleProvider provider = reader.ToSampleProvider();
                int channels = Math.Max(1, provider.WaveFormat.Channels);
                long totalFrames = Math.Max(1L,
                    (long)Math.Round(reader.TotalTime.TotalSeconds * provider.WaveFormat.SampleRate));
                float[] peaks = new float[buckets];
                float[] buffer = new float[16384];
                long frame = 0;
                int read;
                while ((read = provider.Read(buffer, 0, buffer.Length)) > 0)
                {
                    int frames = read / channels;
                    for (int i = 0; i < frames; i++)
                    {
                        int index = (int)((frame + i) * buckets / totalFrames);
                        if (index >= buckets) index = buckets - 1;
                        for (int c = 0; c < channels; c++)
                        {
                            float value = Math.Abs(buffer[i * channels + c]);
                            if (value > peaks[index]) peaks[index] = value;
                        }
                    }
                    frame += frames;
                }
                seconds = reader.TotalTime.TotalSeconds;
                return peaks;
            }
        }

        public static void WriteTags(string path, string prompt, long seed, string bpm, string key, string format, string coverPath)
        {
            using (TagLib.File file = TagLib.File.Create(path))
            {
                string title = (prompt ?? "").Trim().Replace("\r", " ").Replace("\n", " ");
                if (title.Length > 200) title = title.Substring(0, 200);
                file.Tag.Title = title;
                file.Tag.Performers = new string[] { "AI Music Maker" };
                file.Tag.Comment = "seed=" + seed + "; bpm=" + bpm + "; key=" + key + "; format=" + format;
                bool hasCover = false;
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(coverPath) && System.IO.File.Exists(coverPath))
                {
                    try
                    {
                        byte[] bytes = System.IO.File.ReadAllBytes(coverPath);
                        TagLib.Picture picture = new TagLib.Picture();
                        picture.Data = new TagLib.ByteVector(bytes);
                        picture.Type = TagLib.PictureType.FrontCover;
                        picture.MimeType = coverPath.EndsWith(".png", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                            ? "image/png" : "image/jpeg";
                        picture.Description = "Cover";
                        file.Tag.Pictures = new TagLib.IPicture[] { picture };
                        hasCover = true;
                    }
                    catch (Exception)
                    {
                        hasCover = false;
                    }
                }
                try
                {
                    file.Save();
                }
                catch (Exception)
                {
                    if (hasCover)
                    {
                        file.Tag.Pictures = new TagLib.IPicture[0];
                        file.Save();
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        throw;
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        public static string ReadComment(string path)
        {
            try
            {
                using (TagLib.File file = TagLib.File.Create(path))
                {
                    string title = file.Tag.Title ?? "";
                    string comment = file.Tag.Comment ?? "";
                    int pictures = file.Tag.Pictures == null ? 0 : file.Tag.Pictures.Length;
                    if (title.Length > 60) title = title.Substring(0, 60);
                    return (title.Length > 0 ? title + "  |  " : "") + comment + "  |  pictures=" + pictures;
                }
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
                return "";
            }
        }
    }
}


===== [12/66] AI_Music/app/src/AutoRunner.cs  (2005 bytes) =====
using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

namespace AIMusicGenerator
{
    internal static class AutoRunner
    {
        public static int Run(string configPath)
        {
            string logDir = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "AIMusicGenerator");
            Directory.CreateDirectory(logDir);
            string logPath = Path.Combine(logDir, "auto_" + DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss") + ".log");
            int exitCode = -1;
            object gate = new object();
            using (StreamWriter writer = new StreamWriter(logPath, false, new UTF8Encoding(false)))
            {
                writer.AutoFlush = true;
                using (Process process = new Process())
                {
                    process.StartInfo = Paths.BuildStartInfo(Path.GetFullPath(configPath));
                    process.OutputDataReceived += delegate(object sender, DataReceivedEventArgs e)
                    {
                        if (e.Data == null) return;
                        lock (gate) { writer.WriteLine(StripAnsi(e.Data)); }
                    };
                    process.ErrorDataReceived += delegate(object sender, DataReceivedEventArgs e)
                    {
                        if (e.Data == null) return;
                        lock (gate) { writer.WriteLine("[stderr] " + StripAnsi(e.Data)); }
                    };
                    process.Start();
                    process.BeginOutputReadLine();
                    process.BeginErrorReadLine();
                    process.WaitForExit();
                    exitCode = process.ExitCode;
                }
                writer.WriteLine("[auto] exit=" + exitCode);
                writer.WriteLine("[auto] log=" + logPath);
            }
            return exitCode;
        }

        private static string StripAnsi(string text)
        {
            return Regex.Replace(text, "\\x1B\\[[0-9;]*m", "");
        }
    }
}


===== [13/66] AI_Music/app/src/GpuLease.cs  (2045 bytes) =====
using System;
using System.Diagnostics;

namespace AIMusicGenerator
{
    internal sealed class GpuLease : IDisposable
    {
        private readonly Process process;

        private GpuLease(Process process)
        {
            this.process = process;
        }

        public static GpuLease Acquire(string source, int waitSeconds, out string error)
        {
            error = "";
            Process process = new Process();
            try
            {
                process.StartInfo = Paths.BuildGpuHoldStartInfo(source, waitSeconds);
                process.EnableRaisingEvents = false;
                process.Start();
                string line = process.StandardOutput.ReadLine();
                if (line != null && line.StartsWith("[locked]"))
                    return new GpuLease(process);
                error = line == null ? "no response" : line.Trim();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                error = ex.Message;
            }
            try
            {
                if (!process.HasExited) process.Kill();
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
            }
            process.Dispose();
            return null;
        }

        public void Dispose()
        {
            try
            {
                if (!process.HasExited)
                {
                    process.StandardInput.WriteLine("quit");
                    process.StandardInput.Flush();
                    if (!process.WaitForExit(3000))
                    {
                        process.Kill();
                        process.WaitForExit(3000);
                    }
                }
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
                try
                {
                    if (!process.HasExited) process.Kill();
                }
                catch (Exception)
                {
                }
            }
            process.Dispose();
        }
    }
}


===== [14/66] AI_Music/app/src/IconRenderer.cs  (15521 bytes) =====
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Drawing.Drawing2D;
using System.Globalization;

namespace AIMusicGenerator
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Draws Lucide icons (24x24 stroke icons, see LucideIcons.cs) as vectors, so they stay
    /// sharp at any DPI and follow the theme colour.
    /// </summary>
    internal static class IconRenderer
    {
        private static readonly Dictionary<string, GraphicsPath> Cache = new Dictionary<string, GraphicsPath>();
        private static readonly object Gate = new object();

        public static bool Has(string name)
        {
            return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(name) && LucideIcons.Paths.ContainsKey(name);
        }

        public static void Draw(Graphics g, string name, RectangleF bounds, Color color)
        {
            Draw(g, name, bounds, color, 2F);
        }

        public static void Draw(Graphics g, string name, RectangleF bounds, Color color, float strokeWidth)
        {
            GraphicsPath source = GetPath(name);
            if (source == null || bounds.Width <= 0 || bounds.Height <= 0) return;
            float size = Math.Min(bounds.Width, bounds.Height);
            float scale = size / 24F;
            using (GraphicsPath path = (GraphicsPath)source.Clone())
            using (Matrix matrix = new Matrix())
            {
                matrix.Translate(bounds.X + (bounds.Width - size) / 2F, bounds.Y + (bounds.Height - size) / 2F);
                matrix.Scale(scale, scale);
                path.Transform(matrix);
                SmoothingMode oldMode = g.SmoothingMode;
                PixelOffsetMode oldOffset = g.PixelOffsetMode;
                g.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
                g.PixelOffsetMode = PixelOffsetMode.HighQuality;
                using (Pen pen = new Pen(color, Math.Max(1F, strokeWidth * scale)))
                {
                    pen.StartCap = LineCap.Round;
                    pen.EndCap = LineCap.Round;
                    pen.LineJoin = LineJoin.Round;
                    g.DrawPath(pen, path);
                }
                g.SmoothingMode = oldMode;
                g.PixelOffsetMode = oldOffset;
            }
        }

        public static Bitmap ToBitmap(string name, int size, Color color)
        {
            Bitmap bitmap = new Bitmap(size, size, System.Drawing.Imaging.PixelFormat.Format32bppArgb);
            using (Graphics g = Graphics.FromImage(bitmap))
            {
                g.Clear(Color.Transparent);
                Draw(g, name, new RectangleF(0, 0, size, size), color);
            }
            return bitmap;
        }

        public static GraphicsPath GetPath(string name)
        {
            if (!Has(name)) return null;
            lock (Gate)
            {
                GraphicsPath path;
                if (Cache.TryGetValue(name, out path)) return path;
                path = SvgPathParser.Parse(LucideIcons.Paths[name]);
                Cache[name] = path;
                return path;
            }
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Minimal SVG path-data parser (M L H V C S Q T A Z, absolute and relative).</summary>
    internal static class SvgPathParser
    {
        public static GraphicsPath Parse(string data)
        {
            GraphicsPath path = new GraphicsPath(FillMode.Winding);
            Scanner s = new Scanner(data);
            PointF current = PointF.Empty;
            PointF start = PointF.Empty;
            PointF lastControl = PointF.Empty;
            char lastCommand = ' ';
            bool open = false;
            char command = ' ';
            while (true)
            {
                s.SkipSeparators();
                if (s.AtEnd) break;
                if (s.PeekIsElementBreak())
                {
                    // '|' separates SVG elements: each one starts from the origin again,
                    // so a leading relative "m" is measured from (0,0) as in the SVG spec.
                    s.ReadChar();
                    current = start = lastControl = PointF.Empty;
                    lastCommand = command = ' ';
                    open = false;
                    continue;
                }
                if (s.PeekIsCommand()) command = s.ReadChar();
                else if (command == ' ') throw new FormatException("path data must start with a command");
                else if (command == 'M') command = 'L';
                else if (command == 'm') command = 'l';

                bool rel = char.IsLower(command);
                char upper = char.ToUpperInvariant(command);
                switch (upper)
                {
                    case 'M':
                    {
                        PointF p = s.ReadPoint();
                        if (rel && lastCommand != ' ') p = Add(current, p);
                        path.StartFigure();
                        open = false;
                        current = start = p;
                        break;
                    }
                    case 'L':
                    {
                        PointF p = s.ReadPoint();
                        if (rel) p = Add(current, p);
                        path.AddLine(current, p);
                        open = true;
                        current = p;
                        break;
                    }
                    case 'H':
                    {
                        float x = s.ReadNumber();
                        PointF p = new PointF(rel ? current.X + x : x, current.Y);
                        path.AddLine(current, p);
                        open = true;
                        current = p;
                        break;
                    }
                    case 'V':
                    {
                        float y = s.ReadNumber();
                        PointF p = new PointF(current.X, rel ? current.Y + y : y);
                        path.AddLine(current, p);
                        open = true;
                        current = p;
                        break;
                    }
                    case 'C':
                    {
                        PointF c1 = s.ReadPoint(), c2 = s.ReadPoint(), p = s.ReadPoint();
                        if (rel) { c1 = Add(current, c1); c2 = Add(current, c2); p = Add(current, p); }
                        path.AddBezier(current, c1, c2, p);
                        open = true;
                        lastControl = c2;
                        current = p;
                        break;
                    }
                    case 'S':
                    {
                        PointF c2 = s.ReadPoint(), p = s.ReadPoint();
                        if (rel) { c2 = Add(current, c2); p = Add(current, p); }
                        char lc = char.ToUpperInvariant(lastCommand);
                        PointF c1 = (lc == 'C' || lc == 'S') ? Reflect(lastControl, current) : current;
                        path.AddBezier(current, c1, c2, p);
                        open = true;
                        lastControl = c2;
                        current = p;
                        break;
                    }
                    case 'Q':
                    {
                        PointF q = s.ReadPoint(), p = s.ReadPoint();
                        if (rel) { q = Add(current, q); p = Add(current, p); }
                        AddQuadratic(path, current, q, p);
                        open = true;
                        lastControl = q;
                        current = p;
                        break;
                    }
                    case 'T':
                    {
                        PointF p = s.ReadPoint();
                        if (rel) p = Add(current, p);
                        char lc = char.ToUpperInvariant(lastCommand);
                        PointF q = (lc == 'Q' || lc == 'T') ? Reflect(lastControl, current) : current;
                        AddQuadratic(path, current, q, p);
                        open = true;
                        lastControl = q;
                        current = p;
                        break;
                    }
                    case 'A':
                    {
                        float rx = s.ReadNumber(), ry = s.ReadNumber(), angle = s.ReadNumber();
                        bool large = s.ReadFlag(), sweep = s.ReadFlag();
                        PointF p = s.ReadPoint();
                        if (rel) p = Add(current, p);
                        AddArc(path, current, rx, ry, angle, large, sweep, p);
                        open = true;
                        current = p;
                        break;
                    }
                    case 'Z':
                    {
                        if (open) path.CloseFigure();
                        open = false;
                        current = start;
                        break;
                    }
                    default:
                        throw new FormatException("unsupported path command: " + command);
                }
                lastCommand = command;
            }
            return path;
        }

        private static PointF Add(PointF a, PointF b) { return new PointF(a.X + b.X, a.Y + b.Y); }

        private static PointF Reflect(PointF control, PointF around)
        {
            return new PointF(2 * around.X - control.X, 2 * around.Y - control.Y);
        }

        private static void AddQuadratic(GraphicsPath path, PointF p0, PointF q, PointF p)
        {
            PointF c1 = new PointF(p0.X + 2F / 3F * (q.X - p0.X), p0.Y + 2F / 3F * (q.Y - p0.Y));
            PointF c2 = new PointF(p.X + 2F / 3F * (q.X - p.X), p.Y + 2F / 3F * (q.Y - p.Y));
            path.AddBezier(p0, c1, c2, p);
        }

        // SVG endpoint arc -> cubic beziers (SVG 1.1 implementation notes, F.6.5).
        private static void AddArc(GraphicsPath path, PointF p0, float rxIn, float ryIn, float angleDeg,
            bool large, bool sweep, PointF p1)
        {
            if (p0 == p1) return;
            double rx = Math.Abs(rxIn), ry = Math.Abs(ryIn);
            if (rx < 1e-6 || ry < 1e-6) { path.AddLine(p0, p1); return; }
            double phi = angleDeg * Math.PI / 180.0;
            double cos = Math.Cos(phi), sin = Math.Sin(phi);
            double dx = (p0.X - p1.X) / 2.0, dy = (p0.Y - p1.Y) / 2.0;
            double x1 = cos * dx + sin * dy, y1 = -sin * dx + cos * dy;
            double lambda = (x1 * x1) / (rx * rx) + (y1 * y1) / (ry * ry);
            if (lambda > 1) { double k = Math.Sqrt(lambda); rx *= k; ry *= k; }
            double num = rx * rx * ry * ry - rx * rx * y1 * y1 - ry * ry * x1 * x1;
            double den = rx * rx * y1 * y1 + ry * ry * x1 * x1;
            double coef = den == 0 ? 0 : Math.Sqrt(Math.Max(0, num / den));
            if (large == sweep) coef = -coef;
            double cx1 = coef * rx * y1 / ry, cy1 = -coef * ry * x1 / rx;
            double cx = cos * cx1 - sin * cy1 + (p0.X + p1.X) / 2.0;
            double cy = sin * cx1 + cos * cy1 + (p0.Y + p1.Y) / 2.0;
            double theta1 = Angle(1, 0, (x1 - cx1) / rx, (y1 - cy1) / ry);
            double delta = Angle((x1 - cx1) / rx, (y1 - cy1) / ry, (-x1 - cx1) / rx, (-y1 - cy1) / ry);
            if (!sweep && delta > 0) delta -= 2 * Math.PI;
            else if (sweep && delta < 0) delta += 2 * Math.PI;

            int segments = (int)Math.Ceiling(Math.Abs(delta) / (Math.PI / 2) - 1e-9);
            if (segments < 1) segments = 1;
            double step = delta / segments;
            double t = 4.0 / 3.0 * Math.Tan(step / 4);
            PointF from = p0;
            double a = theta1;
            for (int i = 0; i < segments; i++)
            {
                double b = a + step;
                double cosA = Math.Cos(a), sinA = Math.Sin(a), cosB = Math.Cos(b), sinB = Math.Sin(b);
                PointF c1 = Map(cx, cy, rx, ry, cos, sin, cosA - t * sinA, sinA + t * cosA);
                PointF c2 = Map(cx, cy, rx, ry, cos, sin, cosB + t * sinB, sinB - t * cosB);
                PointF to = i == segments - 1 ? p1 : Map(cx, cy, rx, ry, cos, sin, cosB, sinB);
                path.AddBezier(from, c1, c2, to);
                from = to;
                a = b;
            }
        }

        private static PointF Map(double cx, double cy, double rx, double ry, double cos, double sin, double x, double y)
        {
            x *= rx; y *= ry;
            return new PointF((float)(cos * x - sin * y + cx), (float)(sin * x + cos * y + cy));
        }

        private static double Angle(double ux, double uy, double vx, double vy)
        {
            return Math.Atan2(ux * vy - uy * vx, ux * vx + uy * vy);
        }

        private sealed class Scanner
        {
            private readonly string text;
            private int pos;

            public Scanner(string text) { this.text = text ?? ""; }

            public bool AtEnd { get { return pos >= text.Length; } }

            public void SkipSeparators()
            {
                while (pos < text.Length && (char.IsWhiteSpace(text[pos]) || text[pos] == ',')) pos++;
            }

            public bool PeekIsElementBreak()
            {
                return pos < text.Length && text[pos] == '|';
            }

            public bool PeekIsCommand()
            {
                if (pos >= text.Length) return false;
                char c = text[pos];
                return char.IsLetter(c) && c != 'e' && c != 'E';
            }

            public char ReadChar() { return text[pos++]; }

            public bool ReadFlag()
            {
                SkipSeparators();
                if (pos >= text.Length) throw new FormatException("missing arc flag");
                char c = text[pos++];
                if (c != '0' && c != '1') throw new FormatException("invalid arc flag");
                return c == '1';
            }

            public PointF ReadPoint()
            {
                float x = ReadNumber();
                float y = ReadNumber();
                return new PointF(x, y);
            }

            public float ReadNumber()
            {
                SkipSeparators();
                int begin = pos;
                if (pos < text.Length && (text[pos] == '-' || text[pos] == '+')) pos++;
                bool dot = false;
                while (pos < text.Length)
                {
                    char c = text[pos];
                    if (char.IsDigit(c)) { pos++; continue; }
                    if (c == '.' && !dot) { dot = true; pos++; continue; }
                    break;
                }
                if (pos < text.Length && (text[pos] == 'e' || text[pos] == 'E'))
                {
                    pos++;
                    if (pos < text.Length && (text[pos] == '-' || text[pos] == '+')) pos++;
                    while (pos < text.Length && char.IsDigit(text[pos])) pos++;
                }
                if (pos == begin) throw new FormatException("number expected at " + begin);
                return float.Parse(text.Substring(begin, pos - begin), NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            }
        }
    }
}


===== [15/66] AI_Music/app/src/LinkAnalyzer.cs  (14033 bytes) =====
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Net;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using System.Web.Script.Serialization;

namespace AIMusicGenerator
{
    internal sealed class LinkInfo
    {
        public string Source = "";
        public string Title = "";
        public string Artist = "";
        public string Album = "";
        public string Year = "";
        public string Description = "";
    }

    internal sealed class PromptSuggestion
    {
        public string Genre = "";
        public string Bpm = "";
        public string Prompt = "";
        public string Negative = "";
    }

    internal static class LinkAnalyzer
    {
        private static readonly JavaScriptSerializer Serializer = new JavaScriptSerializer();

        static LinkAnalyzer()
        {
            try
            {
                ServicePointManager.SecurityProtocol |= SecurityProtocolType.Tls12;
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
            }
        }

        public static LinkInfo Analyze(string url)
        {
            Uri uri;
            if (!Uri.TryCreate(url.Trim(), UriKind.Absolute, out uri))
                throw new ArgumentException("請輸入完整的 http/https 連結。");
            string host = uri.Host.ToLowerInvariant();
            if (host.Contains("youtube.com") || host.Contains("youtu.be"))
                return AnalyzeYouTube(url.Trim());
            if (host.Contains("spotify.com"))
                return AnalyzeSpotify(url.Trim());
            return AnalyzeGeneric(url.Trim());
        }

        private static LinkInfo AnalyzeYouTube(string url)
        {
            string endpoint = "https://www.youtube.com/oembed?url=" + Uri.EscapeDataString(url) + "&format=json";
            Dictionary<string, object> data = ParseJson(Fetch(endpoint));
            string title = GetString(data, "title");
            string author = GetString(data, "author_name");
            if (author.EndsWith(" - Topic", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                author = author.Substring(0, author.Length - 8).Trim();
            LinkInfo info = new LinkInfo();
            info.Source = "YouTube";
            info.Title = CleanTitle(title);
            info.Artist = author;
            if (info.Artist.Length > 0)
            {
                string prefix = info.Artist + " - ";
                if (info.Title.StartsWith(prefix, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    info.Title = info.Title.Substring(prefix.Length).Trim();
            }
            info.Description = title;
            return info;
        }

        private static LinkInfo AnalyzeSpotify(string url)
        {
            LinkInfo info = new LinkInfo();
            info.Source = "Spotify";
            try
            {
                string endpoint = "https://open.spotify.com/oembed?url=" + Uri.EscapeDataString(url);
                Dictionary<string, object> data = ParseJson(Fetch(endpoint));
                info.Title = GetString(data, "title");
                info.Description = info.Title;
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
            }
            try
            {
                string html = Fetch(url);
                Match desc = Regex.Match(html,
                    "<meta[^>]+property=\"og:description\"[^>]+content=\"([^\"]*)\"",
                    RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
                if (desc.Success)
                {
                    string value = WebUtility.HtmlDecode(desc.Groups[1].Value);
                    string[] parts = value.Split(new string[] { " · " }, StringSplitOptions.None);
                    if (parts.Length >= 1) info.Artist = parts[0].Trim();
                    if (parts.Length >= 2) info.Album = parts[1].Trim();
                    if (parts.Length >= 2) info.Year = parts[parts.Length - 1].Trim();
                    info.Description = value;
                }
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
            }
            if (info.Title.Length == 0)
            {
                try
                {
                    string html = Fetch(url);
                    Match title = Regex.Match(html, "<title>([^<]*)</title>", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
                    if (title.Success)
                    {
                        string pageTitle = WebUtility.HtmlDecode(title.Groups[1].Value).Trim();
                        int marker = pageTitle.IndexOf(" - song", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                        if (marker > 0) info.Title = pageTitle.Substring(0, marker).Trim();
                        else info.Title = pageTitle;
                        int by = pageTitle.IndexOf(" by ", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                        if (info.Artist.Length == 0 && by > 0)
                        {
                            string tail = pageTitle.Substring(by + 4);
                            int bar = tail.IndexOf('|');
                            info.Artist = (bar > 0 ? tail.Substring(0, bar) : tail).Trim();
                        }
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception)
                {
                }
            }
            return info;
        }

        private static LinkInfo AnalyzeGeneric(string url)
        {
            LinkInfo info = new LinkInfo();
            info.Source = "Web";
            string html = Fetch(url);
            Match title = Regex.Match(html, "<meta[^>]+property=\"og:title\"[^>]+content=\"([^\"]*)\"",
                RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
            if (!title.Success)
                title = Regex.Match(html, "<title>([^<]*)</title>", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
            if (title.Success) info.Title = WebUtility.HtmlDecode(title.Groups[1].Value).Trim();
            Match desc = Regex.Match(html, "<meta[^>]+property=\"og:description\"[^>]+content=\"([^\"]*)\"",
                RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
            if (desc.Success) info.Description = WebUtility.HtmlDecode(desc.Groups[1].Value).Trim();
            return info;
        }

        private static bool ContainsKeyword(string text, string keyword)
        {
            if (Regex.IsMatch(keyword, "^[a-z0-9]+$"))
                return Regex.IsMatch(text, "\\b" + Regex.Escape(keyword) + "\\b");
            return text.Contains(keyword);
        }

        public static PromptSuggestion Suggest(LinkInfo info, bool instrumental, bool chinese = false)
        {
            string haystack = (info.Title + " " + info.Artist + " " + info.Album + " " + info.Description)
                .ToLowerInvariant();
            string titleArtist = (info.Title + " " + info.Artist).ToLowerInvariant();

            List<Dictionary<string, object>> genres = LoadGenres();
            Dictionary<string, object> best = null;
            int bestScore = 0;
            foreach (Dictionary<string, object> genre in genres)
            {
                int score = 0;
                object keywordsValue;
                if (genre.TryGetValue("keywords", out keywordsValue) && keywordsValue is IEnumerable)
                {
                    foreach (object keywordObject in (IEnumerable)keywordsValue)
                    {
                        string keyword = Convert.ToString(keywordObject).ToLowerInvariant();
                        if (keyword.Length == 0) continue;
                        if (ContainsKeyword(titleArtist, keyword)) score += 3;
                        else if (ContainsKeyword(haystack, keyword)) score += 1;
                    }
                }
                if (score > bestScore)
                {
                    bestScore = score;
                    best = genre;
                }
            }
            if (best == null)
            {
                foreach (Dictionary<string, object> genre in genres)
                {
                    if (GetString(genre, "name") == "pop")
                    {
                        best = genre;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
            if (best == null && genres.Count > 0) best = genres[0];

            PromptSuggestion suggestion = new PromptSuggestion();
            if (best == null)
            {
                suggestion.Genre = "instrumental";
                suggestion.Prompt = "Instrumental music with a clear mood and production style. ";
                suggestion.Negative = "low quality, clipping, muddy mix";
                return suggestion;
            }

            suggestion.Genre = chinese ? GetString(best, "name_zh") : GetString(best, "name");
            if (suggestion.Genre.Length == 0) suggestion.Genre = GetString(best, "name");
            suggestion.Bpm = GetString(best, "bpm");
            string prompt = (chinese ? GetString(best, "prompt_zh") : GetString(best, "prompt")).Trim();
            if (chinese)
            {
                if (prompt.Length > 0 && !prompt.EndsWith("。")) prompt += "。";
                prompt += " ";
                if (info.Artist.Length > 0 || info.Title.Length > 0)
                {
                    prompt += "風格參考：" + info.Artist;
                    if (info.Title.Length > 0) prompt += " - " + info.Title;
                    prompt += "（僅參考風格，不是翻唱，也不複製旋律）。";
                }
                if (instrumental)
                    prompt += "純音樂。完全無人聲、無歌唱、無饒舌、無朗讀、無合唱、無哼唱、無人聲切片。";
                prompt += "高品質製作，編曲完整，動態自然。";
            }
            else
            {
                if (prompt.Length > 0 && !prompt.EndsWith(".")) prompt += ".";
                prompt += " ";
                if (info.Artist.Length > 0 || info.Title.Length > 0)
                {
                    prompt += "Style reference: " + info.Artist;
                    if (info.Title.Length > 0) prompt += " - " + info.Title;
                    prompt += ". Original composition, not a cover and not a melody copy. ";
                }
                if (instrumental)
                    prompt += "Instrumental only. Absolutely no vocals, no singing, no rap, no spoken words, no choir, no humming, no vocal chops. ";
                prompt += "High-quality production, cohesive arrangement, natural dynamics.";
            }
            suggestion.Prompt = prompt;
            string negative = (chinese ? GetString(best, "negative_zh") : GetString(best, "negative")).Trim().TrimEnd(',');
            string lowerNegative = negative.ToLowerInvariant();
            if (chinese)
            {
                if (!negative.Contains("低品質")) negative += "，低品質、爆音、混音混濁";
                if (instrumental && !negative.Contains("人聲")) negative += "、人聲、歌唱、饒舌、朗讀";
            }
            else
            {
                if (!lowerNegative.Contains("low quality")) negative += ", low quality, clipping, muddy mix";
                if (instrumental && !lowerNegative.Contains("vocal")) negative += ", vocals, singing, rap, spoken words";
            }
            suggestion.Negative = negative.TrimStart(',', ' ');
            return suggestion;
        }

        private static List<Dictionary<string, object>> LoadGenres()
        {
            string path = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, "genres.json");
            if (System.IO.File.Exists(path))
            {
                try
                {
                    List<Dictionary<string, object>> parsed =
                        Serializer.Deserialize<List<Dictionary<string, object>>>(
                            System.IO.File.ReadAllText(path, Encoding.UTF8));
                    if (parsed != null && parsed.Count > 0) return parsed;
                }
                catch (Exception)
                {
                }
            }
            return new List<Dictionary<string, object>>();
        }

        private static string Fetch(string url)
        {
            HttpWebRequest request = (HttpWebRequest)WebRequest.Create(url);
            request.UserAgent = "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)";
            request.Accept = "application/json,text/html,*/*";
            request.Timeout = 20000;
            request.ReadWriteTimeout = 20000;
            using (HttpWebResponse response = (HttpWebResponse)request.GetResponse())
            using (StreamReader reader = new StreamReader(response.GetResponseStream(), Encoding.UTF8))
            {
                return reader.ReadToEnd();
            }
        }

        private static Dictionary<string, object> ParseJson(string json)
        {
            Dictionary<string, object> data =
                Serializer.Deserialize<Dictionary<string, object>>(json);
            if (data == null) throw new InvalidOperationException("無法解析 API 回應。");
            return data;
        }

        private static string GetString(Dictionary<string, object> data, string key)
        {
            object value;
            return data.TryGetValue(key, out value) && value != null ? Convert.ToString(value) : "";
        }

        private static string CleanTitle(string title)
        {
            string cleaned = Regex.Replace(title,
                @"\s*[\(\[](official|lyrics?|audio|video|visuali[sz]er|remaster(ed)?|4k|hd|mv)[^\)\]]*[\)\]]",
                "", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
            cleaned = Regex.Replace(cleaned, @"\s+", " ").Trim();
            return cleaned.Length > 0 ? cleaned : title.Trim();
        }
    }
}


===== [16/66] AI_Music/app/src/LinkPromptDialog.cs  (7393 bytes) =====
using System;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Threading;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace AIMusicGenerator
{
    internal sealed class LinkPromptDialog : Form
    {
        private readonly TextBox txtUrl = new TextBox();
        private readonly ModernButton btnParse = new ModernButton();
        private readonly ComboBox comboOutputLanguage = new ThemedComboBox();
        private LinkInfo lastInfo;
        private readonly Label lblStatus = new Label();
        private readonly Label lblMeta = new Label();
        private readonly TextBox txtPreview = new TextBox();
        private readonly ModernButton btnApply = new ModernButton();
        private readonly ModernButton btnCancel = new ModernButton();

        public string SuggestedPrompt { get; private set; }
        private string suggestionPrompt = "";

        public LinkPromptDialog()
        {
            UiBuild.DialogParts parts = UiBuild.Dialog(this, "參考連結 → 提示詞",
                "貼上 YouTube／Spotify 連結；只讀取 metadata，不下載音訊", "link", 760, 580, false);
            BuildUi(parts);
            ThemeManager.ApplyDialog(this);
        }

        private void BuildUi(UiBuild.DialogParts parts)
        {
            TableLayoutPanel body = parts.Body;
            body.ColumnCount = 2;
            body.ColumnStyles.Clear();
            body.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
            body.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100F));

            txtUrl.Text = "https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=";
            UiBuild.Button(btnParse, "解析", "sparkles", ButtonKind.Primary);
            btnParse.Click += OnParse;
            UiBuild.AddWide(body, "連結", UiBuild.Fill(UiBuild.TextField(txtUrl), btnParse));

            comboOutputLanguage.Items.AddRange(new object[] { "English", "繁體中文" });
            comboOutputLanguage.SelectedIndex = 0;
            comboOutputLanguage.SelectedIndexChanged += delegate { RefreshLanguage(); };
            UiBuild.AddWide(body, "輸出語言", UiBuild.Row(UiBuild.ComboFixed(comboOutputLanguage, Dpi.S(160)),
                UiBuild.Unit("建議提示詞使用英文，模型理解較好")));

            lblStatus.AutoSize = true;
            lblStatus.Tag = "accent";
            lblStatus.Font = ThemeManager.HeadingFont;
            lblStatus.Margin = new Padding(0, Dpi.S(12), 0, Dpi.S(2));
            UiBuild.AddFull(body, lblStatus, new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
            lblMeta.AutoSize = true;
            lblMeta.Tag = "muted";
            lblMeta.MaximumSize = new Size(Dpi.S(700), 0);
            lblMeta.Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, Dpi.S(6));
            UiBuild.AddFull(body, lblMeta, new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));

            txtPreview.ReadOnly = true;
            txtPreview.ScrollBars = ScrollBars.Vertical;
            UiBuild.AddFull(body, UiBuild.TextArea(txtPreview, ThemeManager.MonoFont), new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100F));

            UiBuild.Button(btnApply, "套用提示詞", "check", ButtonKind.Primary);
            btnApply.Enabled = false;
            btnApply.Click += delegate
            {
                SuggestedPrompt = suggestionPrompt.Length > 0 ? suggestionPrompt : txtPreview.Text.Trim();
                if (SuggestedPrompt.Length == 0) return;
                DialogResult = DialogResult.OK;
                Close();
            };
            UiBuild.Button(btnCancel, "取消", "", ButtonKind.Secondary);
            btnCancel.Click += delegate { DialogResult = DialogResult.Cancel; Close(); };
            UiBuild.AddFooterButton(parts.Footer, btnApply);
            UiBuild.AddFooterButton(parts.Footer, btnCancel);
            CancelButton = btnCancel;
        }

        private void RefreshLanguage()
        {
            if (lastInfo == null || !btnApply.Enabled) return;
            bool chinese = comboOutputLanguage.SelectedIndex == 1;
            ApplySuggestion(LinkAnalyzer.Suggest(lastInfo, true, chinese), chinese);
        }

        private void ApplySuggestion(PromptSuggestion result, bool chinese)
        {
            suggestionPrompt = result.Prompt;
            txtPreview.Text = result.Prompt + "\r\n\r\n[建議 BPM] " + result.Bpm +
                              "\r\n[負向提示，僅供參考] " + result.Negative;
            lblStatus.Text = "推測曲風: " + result.Genre + "（僅供參考，可手動修改）";
            btnApply.Enabled = true;
        }

        private void OnParse(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            string url = txtUrl.Text.Trim();
            if (url.Length == 0)
            {
                lblStatus.Text = "請先貼上連結。";
                return;
            }
            btnParse.Enabled = false;
            btnApply.Enabled = false;
            lblStatus.Text = "讀取 metadata 中...";
            lblMeta.Text = "";
            txtPreview.Clear();

            Thread worker = new Thread(delegate()
            {
                try
                {
                    LinkInfo info = LinkAnalyzer.Analyze(url);
                    bool chinese = comboOutputLanguage.SelectedIndex == 1;
                    PromptSuggestion result = LinkAnalyzer.Suggest(info, true, chinese);
                    try
                    {
                        BeginInvoke(new Action(delegate()
                        {
                            lastInfo = info;
                            string meta = info.Source + " | " + info.Artist;
                            if (info.Title.Length > 0) meta += " - " + info.Title;
                            if (info.Album.Length > 0) meta += "\r\n專輯: " + info.Album;
                            if (info.Year.Length > 0) meta += "  年份: " + info.Year;
                            lblMeta.Text = meta;
                            txtPreview.Text = result.Prompt + "\r\n\r\n[建議 BPM] " + result.Bpm +
                                              "\r\n[負向提示，僅供參考] " + result.Negative;
                            lblStatus.Text = "推測曲風: " + result.Genre + "（僅供參考，可手動修改）";
                            suggestionPrompt = result.Prompt;
                            btnApply.Enabled = true;
                            btnParse.Enabled = true;
                        }));
                    }
                    catch (InvalidOperationException)
                    {
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    try
                    {
                        BeginInvoke(new Action(delegate()
                        {
                            lblStatus.Text = "解析失敗: " + ex.Message;
                            btnParse.Enabled = true;
                        }));
                    }
                    catch (InvalidOperationException)
                    {
                    }
                }
            });
            worker.IsBackground = true;
            worker.Start();
        }

        protected override void OnShown(EventArgs e)
        {
            base.OnShown(e);
            txtUrl.Focus();
            txtUrl.SelectAll();
        }
    }
}
