        private void EnqueueLog(string text)
        {
            bool progress = text.IndexOf('\r') >= 0;
            if (progress)
            {
                string[] parts = text.Split('\r');
                string last = "";
                for (int i = parts.Length - 1; i >= 0; i--)
                {
                    if (parts[i].Trim().Length > 0)
                    {
                        last = parts[i];
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if (last.Length == 0) return;
                text = last;
            }
            lock (logGate)
            {
                if (logQueue.Count > 4000)
                {
                    logQueue.Clear();
                    logQueue.Enqueue("[...日誌過多，已丟棄...]");
                }
                logQueue.Enqueue((progress ? "\u0001" : "") + text);
            }
        }

        private void AppendLog(string text)
        {
            EnqueueLog(text);
        }

        private void OnLogTimer(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            List<string> items = new List<string>();
            lock (logGate)
            {
                while (logQueue.Count > 0 && items.Count < 200)
                    items.Add(logQueue.Dequeue());
            }
            if (items.Count == 0) return;
            txtLog.SuspendLayout();
            foreach (string item in items)
            {
                bool progress = item.Length > 0 && item[0] == '\u0001';
                AppendLogLine(progress ? item.Substring(1) : item, progress);
            }
            txtLog.ResumeLayout();
            txtLog.SelectionStart = txtLog.TextLength;
            txtLog.ScrollToCaret();
        }

        private void AppendLogLine(string text, bool progress)
        {
            if (txtLog.TextLength > 500000)
            {
                string current = txtLog.Text;
                int start = Math.Max(0, current.Length - 250000);
                int newline = current.IndexOf('\n', start);
                if (newline >= 0) start = newline + 1;
                txtLog.Text = "[...舊日誌已截斷...]" + Environment.NewLine + current.Substring(start);
                lastLogWasProgress = false;
            }
            if (progress && lastLogWasProgress)
            {
                string current = txtLog.Text;
                int end = current.Length;
                while (end > 0 && (current[end - 1] == '\n' || current[end - 1] == '\r')) end--;
                int cut = current.LastIndexOf('\n', Math.Max(0, end - 1));
                txtLog.Text = cut >= 0 ? current.Substring(0, cut + 1) : "";
            }
            txtLog.AppendText(text + Environment.NewLine);
            lastLogWasProgress = progress;
        }

        private void LoadLimitsAsync()
        {
            Thread worker = new Thread(delegate()
            {
                try
                {
                    ProcessStartInfo psi = Paths.BuildLimitsStartInfo();
                    using (Process process = new Process())
                    {
                        process.StartInfo = psi;
                        process.ErrorDataReceived += delegate(object sender, DataReceivedEventArgs e) { };
                        process.OutputDataReceived += delegate(object sender, DataReceivedEventArgs e)
                        {
                            if (e.Data == null || !e.Data.StartsWith("[limits]")) return;
                            string[] tokens = e.Data.Substring(8).Trim().Split(' ');
                            foreach (string token in tokens)
                            {
                                if (!token.StartsWith("max_duration_with_lm=")) continue;
                                int value;
                                if (int.TryParse(token.Substring("max_duration_with_lm=".Length), out value) && value > 0)
                                {
                                    int captured = value;
                                    try
                                    {
                                        BeginInvoke(new Action(delegate()
                                        {
                                            maxDurationWithLm = captured;
                                            ApplyDurationLimit();
                                        }));
                                    }
                                    catch (InvalidOperationException)
                                    {
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        };
                        process.Start();
                        process.BeginOutputReadLine();
                        process.BeginErrorReadLine();
                        process.WaitForExit();
                        process.WaitForExit();
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception)
                {
                }
            });
            worker.IsBackground = true;
            worker.Start();
        }

        private void CleanupTempConfigs()
        {
            try
            {
                if (!Directory.Exists(Paths.TempDir)) return;
                DateTime cutoff = DateTime.Now.AddDays(-7);
                foreach (FileInfo file in new DirectoryInfo(Paths.TempDir).GetFiles("config_*.json"))
                {
                    if (file.LastWriteTime < cutoff)
                    {
                        try { file.Delete(); }
                        catch (Exception) { }
                    }
                }
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
            }
        }

        private void MigrateLegacySettings()
        {
            try
            {
                if (File.Exists(Paths.SettingsFile)) return;
                if (!File.Exists(Paths.LegacySettingsFile)) return;
                Directory.CreateDirectory(Paths.SettingsDir);
                File.Move(Paths.LegacySettingsFile, Paths.SettingsFile);
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
            }
        }

        private void LoadSettings()
        {
            MigrateLegacySettings();
            Dictionary<string, object> s = new Dictionary<string, object>();
            try
            {
                if (File.Exists(Paths.SettingsFile))
                    s = serializer.Deserialize<Dictionary<string, object>>(
                        File.ReadAllText(Paths.SettingsFile, Encoding.UTF8));
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
                s = new Dictionary<string, object>();
            }
            txtCaption.Text = GetString(s, "caption", DefaultCaption);
            txtLyrics.Text = GetString(s, "lyrics", "[Instrumental]");
            txtCover.Text = GetString(s, "cover_prompt", "");
            txtVideo.Text = GetString(s, "video_prompt", "");
            txtOutDir.Text = GetString(s, "output_dir", Paths.Generated);
            txtSeed.Text = GetString(s, "seed", "");
            ThemeManager.SetTheme(GetBool(s, "theme_dark", true));
            ApplyTheme();
            savedSplitRatio = (double)Clamp(GetDecimal(s, "layout_split_ratio", 0.5m), 0.2m, 0.8m);
            negativePrompt = GetString(s, "lm_negative_prompt", "");
            fadeInValue = Clamp(GetDecimal(s, "fade_in_duration", 0m), 0m, 30m);
            fadeOutValue = Clamp(GetDecimal(s, "fade_out_duration", 0m), 0m, 30m);
            shiftValue = Clamp(GetDecimal(s, "shift", 1.0m), 0.5m, 10m);
            mp3BitrateValue = GetString(s, "mp3_bitrate", "128k");
            mp3SampleRateValue = (int)Clamp(GetDecimal(s, "mp3_sample_rate", 48000m), 44100m, 48000m);
            sdEnabled = GetBool(s, "sd_enabled", false);
            sdUrl = GetString(s, "sd_url", "http://127.0.0.1:7860");
            sdPath = GetString(s, "sd_path", @"G:\stable-diffusion-webui");
            sdModel = GetString(s, "sd_model", "");
            sdSteps = (int)Clamp(GetDecimal(s, "sd_steps", 20m), 8m, 40m);
            sdSize = (int)Clamp(GetDecimal(s, "sd_size", 512m), 512m, 768m);
            coverPath = GetString(s, "cover_path", Paths.CoverFile);
            if (!File.Exists(coverPath)) coverPath = "";
            chkInstrumental.Checked = GetBool(s, "instrumental", true);
            chkThinking.Checked = GetBool(s, "thinking", true);
            chkKeepModel.Checked = GetBool(s, "keep_model_on_cancel", false);
            chkAutoVideo.Checked = GetBool(s, "auto_video", true);
            chkAutoBpm.Checked = GetBool(s, "auto_bpm", true);
            SelectCombo(comboKey, GetString(s, "keyscale", ""));
            SelectCombo(comboSignature, GetString(s, "timesignature", ""));
            SelectCombo(comboLanguage, GetString(s, "vocal_language", "unknown"));
            SelectCombo(comboFormat, GetString(s, "audio_format", "wav"));
            SelectCombo(comboOffload, GetString(s, "offload_mode", "auto"));
            numBpm.Value = Clamp(GetDecimal(s, "bpm", 78m), numBpm.Minimum, numBpm.Maximum);
            numDuration.Value = Clamp(GetDecimal(s, "duration", 60m), 10m, 7200m);
            SetDurationFromSeconds((int)numDuration.Value);
            numSteps.Value = Clamp(GetDecimal(s, "inference_steps", 8m), numSteps.Minimum, numSteps.Maximum);
            numGuidance.Value = Clamp(GetDecimal(s, "guidance_scale", 1.0m),
                numGuidance.Minimum, numGuidance.Maximum);
            ApplyDurationLimit();
            numBpm.Enabled = !chkAutoBpm.Checked;
        }

        private void SaveSettings()
        {
            try
            {
                Directory.CreateDirectory(Paths.SettingsDir);
                Dictionary<string, object> s = new Dictionary<string, object>();
                s["caption"] = txtCaption.Text;
                s["lyrics"] = txtLyrics.Text;
                s["cover_prompt"] = txtCover.Text;
                s["video_prompt"] = txtVideo.Text;
                s["output_dir"] = txtOutDir.Text;
                s["seed"] = txtSeed.Text;
                s["theme_dark"] = ThemeManager.Dark;
                s["layout_split_ratio"] = CurrentSplitRatio();
                s["lm_negative_prompt"] = negativePrompt;
                s["fade_in_duration"] = (double)fadeInValue;
                s["fade_out_duration"] = (double)fadeOutValue;
                s["shift"] = (double)shiftValue;
                s["mp3_bitrate"] = mp3BitrateValue;
                s["mp3_sample_rate"] = mp3SampleRateValue;
                s["sd_enabled"] = sdEnabled;
                s["sd_url"] = sdUrl;
                s["sd_path"] = sdPath;
                s["sd_model"] = sdModel;
                s["sd_steps"] = sdSteps;
                s["sd_size"] = sdSize;
                s["cover_path"] = coverPath;
                s["bpm"] = (int)numBpm.Value;
                s["auto_bpm"] = chkAutoBpm.Checked;
                s["duration"] = (double)numDuration.Value;
                s["inference_steps"] = (int)numSteps.Value;
                s["guidance_scale"] = (double)numGuidance.Value;
                s["keyscale"] = comboKey.SelectedIndex <= 0 ? "" : Convert.ToString(comboKey.SelectedItem);
                s["timesignature"] = comboSignature.SelectedIndex <= 0 ? "" : Convert.ToString(comboSignature.SelectedItem);
                s["vocal_language"] = Convert.ToString(comboLanguage.SelectedItem);
                s["audio_format"] = Convert.ToString(comboFormat.SelectedItem);
                s["offload_mode"] = Convert.ToString(comboOffload.SelectedItem);
                s["instrumental"] = chkInstrumental.Checked;
                s["thinking"] = chkThinking.Checked;
                s["keep_model_on_cancel"] = chkKeepModel.Checked;
                s["auto_video"] = chkAutoVideo.Checked;
                File.WriteAllText(Paths.SettingsFile, serializer.Serialize(s), new UTF8Encoding(false));
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
                // settings are optional
            }
        }

        private static void SelectCombo(ComboBox combo, string value)
        {
            int index = combo.FindStringExact(value);
            if (index < 0 && string.IsNullOrEmpty(value)) index = 0;
            combo.SelectedIndex = index >= 0 ? index : 0;
        }

        private static string AsciiJson(string text)
        {
            StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder(text.Length);
            foreach (char ch in text)
            {
                if (ch <= 0x7F) builder.Append(ch);
                else builder.Append("\\u").Append(((int)ch).ToString("x4"));
            }
            return builder.ToString();
        }

        private static decimal Clamp(decimal value, decimal min, decimal max)
        {
            if (value < min) return min;
            if (value > max) return max;
            return value;
        }

        private static string GetString(Dictionary<string, object> s, string key, string fallback)
        {
            object value;
            if (s.TryGetValue(key, out value) && value != null) return Convert.ToString(value);
            return fallback;
        }

        private static bool GetBool(Dictionary<string, object> s, string key, bool fallback)
        {
            object value;
            if (s.TryGetValue(key, out value) && value != null)
            {
                try { return Convert.ToBoolean(value); }
                catch (Exception) { }
            }
            return fallback;
        }

        private static decimal GetDecimal(Dictionary<string, object> s, string key, decimal fallback)
        {
            object value;
            if (s.TryGetValue(key, out value) && value != null)
            {
                try { return Convert.ToDecimal(value); }
                catch (Exception) { }
            }
            return fallback;
        }


        internal void StartSingleUiTest()
        {
            Shown += delegate
            {
                WindowState = FormWindowState.Minimized;
                uiTestTimer = new WinTimer();
                uiTestTimer.Interval = 1500;
                uiTestTimer.Tick += OnSingleUiTestTick;
                uiTestTimer.Start();
            };
        }

        private void OnSingleUiTestTick(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            uiTestTicks++;
            try
            {
                if (uiTestState == 0)
                {
                    uiTestState = 1;
                    AppendLog("=== UI TEST SINGLE: generate ===");
                    OnGenerate(this, EventArgs.Empty);
                    return;
                }
                if (uiTestState == 1)
                {
                    string cover = lastOutputFile.Length > 0
                        ? Path.ChangeExtension(lastOutputFile, ".cover.png") : "";
                    if (cover.Length > 0 && File.Exists(cover))
                    {
                        FinishUiTest(0);
                        return;
                    }
                    if (lblStatus.Text.Contains("SD 封面失敗") ||
                        lblStatus.Text.Contains("失敗 (exit") ||
                        lblStatus.Text.Contains("錯誤:"))
                    {
                        FinishUiTest(2);
                        return;
                    }
                    if (uiTestTicks > 900) FinishUiTest(3);
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                AppendLog("UI TEST SINGLE error: " + ex.Message);
                FinishUiTest(4);
            }
        }

        internal string PlayerTest(string path)
        {
            LoadAudio(path);
            audioOutput.Volume = 0f;
            audioOutput.Play();
            System.Threading.Thread.Sleep(900);
            string result = "state=" + audioOutput.PlaybackState +
                            " position=" + audioStream.CurrentTime.TotalSeconds.ToString("F2") +
                            " duration=" + audioStream.TotalTime.TotalSeconds.ToString("F2");
            DisposeAudio();
            return result;
        }

        internal string AutoVideoTest(string path)
        {
            string output = Path.ChangeExtension(path, ".mp4");
            try
            {
                if (File.Exists(output)) File.Delete(output);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return "delete_failed " + ex.Message;
            }
            chkAutoVideo.Checked = true;
            QueueAutoVideo(path);
            DateTime deadline = DateTime.Now.AddMinutes(5);
            while (DateTime.Now < deadline)
            {
                if (File.Exists(output))
                {
                    try
                    {
                        using (FileStream stream = File.Open(output, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read, FileShare.None))
                        {
                            return "ok output=" + output + " bytes=" + stream.Length;
                        }
                    }
                    catch (IOException)
                    {
                    }
                }
                System.Threading.Thread.Sleep(200);
            }
            return "timeout output=" + output;
        }

        internal string StyleReport()
        {
            return string.Format("multiline={0} wordwrap={1} width={2} client={3} font={4} textlen={5} lines={6} capH={7} logH={8}",
                txtCaption.Multiline, txtCaption.WordWrap, txtCaption.Width, txtCaption.ClientSize.Width,
                txtCaption.Font.Height, txtCaption.TextLength, txtCaption.Lines.Length, txtCaption.Height, txtLog.Height) +
                string.Format(" scroll={0}", txtCaption.ScrollBars) +
                string.Format(" size={0} client={1} scale={2} dims={3} current={4}", Size, ClientSize, AutoScaleMode, AutoScaleDimensions, CurrentAutoScaleDimensions);
        }

        internal void StartUiTest(bool batch)
        {
            uiTestBatch = batch;
            Shown += delegate
            {
                WindowState = FormWindowState.Minimized;
                if (batch)
                {
                    txtCaption.Text = "Warm lo-fi piano instrumental, no vocals.\r\nMellow acoustic guitar instrumental, no vocals.";
                    chkBatch.Checked = true;
                }
                uiTestTimer = new WinTimer();
                uiTestTimer.Interval = 1000;
                uiTestTimer.Tick += OnUiTestTick;
                uiTestTimer.Start();
            };
        }

        private void OnUiTestTick(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            uiTestTicks++;
            try
            {
                if (uiTestState == 0)
                {
                    uiTestState = 1;
                    uiTestTicks = 0;
                    AppendLog("=== UI TEST: generate ===");
                    OnGenerate(this, EventArgs.Empty);
                }
                else if (uiTestState == 1)
                {
                    if (uiTestBatch)
                    {
                        string uiStatus = lblStatus.Text;
                        if (uiStatus.Contains("批次完成 ") || uiStatus.Contains("批次完成：") ||
                            uiStatus.Contains("批次 SD 封面完成"))
                        {
                            FinishUiTest(0);
                            return;
                        }
                        if (uiTestTicks > 1800 || uiStatus.Contains("失敗")) { FinishUiTest(2); return; }
                        return;
                    }
                    if (lblStatus.Text.Contains("完成:"))
                    {
                        uiTestState = 2;
                        uiTestTicks = 0;
                        AppendLog("=== UI TEST: first generation complete ===");
                        OnGenerate(this, EventArgs.Empty);
                    }
                    else if (uiTestTicks > 900 || lblStatus.Text.Contains("失敗"))
                    {
                        FinishUiTest(2);
                    }
                }
                else if (uiTestState == 2)
                {
                    if (uiTestTicks >= 5)
                    {
                        uiTestState = 3;
                        uiTestTicks = 0;
                        AppendLog("=== UI TEST: cancel ===");
                        OnCancel(this, EventArgs.Empty);
                    }
                }
                else if (uiTestState == 3)
                {
                    if (lblStatus.Text.Contains("已取消"))
                    {
                        FinishUiTest(0);
                    }
                    else if (uiTestTicks > 60)
                    {
                        FinishUiTest(3);
                    }
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                AppendLog("UI TEST error: " + ex.Message);
                FinishUiTest(4);
            }
        }

        private void FinishUiTest(int code)
        {
            UiTestResult = code;
            try
            {
                uiTestTimer.Stop();
                Directory.CreateDirectory(Paths.TempDir);
                string body = "result=" + code + Environment.NewLine +
                              "status=" + lblStatus.Text + Environment.NewLine +
                              "log=" + txtLog.Text;
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Paths.TempDir, "ui_test_result.txt"),
                    body, new UTF8Encoding(false));
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
            }
            Close();
        }
        private void OnFormClosing(object sender, FormClosingEventArgs e)
        {
            closing = true;
            if (albumProcess != null && !albumProcess.HasExited)
                ProcessSupervisor.StopTree(albumProcess, 5000);
            if (telegramProcess != null && !telegramProcess.HasExited)
                ProcessSupervisor.StopTree(telegramProcess, 5000);
            SdCoverClient.Stop();
            DisposeAudio();
            finishTimer.Stop();
            logTimer.Stop();
            daemonIdleTimer.Stop();
            elapsedTimer.Stop();
            SaveSettings();
            Process running = daemon;
            daemon = null;
            if (running != null)
            {
                try
                {
                if (!running.HasExited)
                {
                    try
                    {
                        running.StandardInput.WriteLine("quit");
                        running.StandardInput.Flush();
                    }
                    catch (Exception)
                    {
                    }
                    ProcessSupervisor.StopTree(running, 3000);
                }
                }
                catch (Exception)
                {
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

        private void ConfigureInputs()
        {
            txtCaption.WordWrap = true;

            txtVideo.DetectUrls = false;
            txtVideo.ScrollBars = RichTextBoxScrollBars.Vertical;

            numHours.Minimum = 0;
            numHours.Maximum = 2;
            numHours.Value = 0;
            numHours.ValueChanged += OnDurationPartsChanged;
            numMinutes.Minimum = 0;
            numMinutes.Maximum = 59;
            numMinutes.Value = 1;
            numMinutes.ValueChanged += OnDurationPartsChanged;
            numSeconds.Minimum = 0;
            numSeconds.Maximum = 59;
            numSeconds.Value = 0;
            numSeconds.ValueChanged += OnDurationPartsChanged;
            numDuration.Minimum = 10;
            numDuration.Maximum = 7200;
            numDuration.Value = 60;

            numSteps.Minimum = 1;
            numSteps.Maximum = 100;
            numSteps.Value = 8;

            numGuidance.DecimalPlaces = 2;
            numGuidance.Increment = 0.1m;
            numGuidance.Minimum = 0.1m;
            numGuidance.Maximum = 20m;
            numGuidance.Value = 1.0m;

            txtSeed.Font = ThemeManager.UiFont;
            txtSeed.HandleCreated += delegate
            {
                SendMessage(txtSeed.Handle, EM_SETCUEBANNER, (IntPtr)1, "空白 = 隨機");
            };
            txtOutDir.Font = ThemeManager.UiFont;

            chkInstrumental.Text = "純音樂（無人聲）";
            chkInstrumental.Checked = true;
            chkThinking.Text = "LM 思考（較慢，編排較完整）";
            chkThinking.Checked = true;
            chkThinking.CheckedChanged += OnThinkingChanged;
            chkKeepModel.Text = "取消時保留模型（背景跑完）";
            chkAutoVideo.Text = "完成後自動轉 MP4 影片";
            chkBatch.Text = "批次";

            foreach (StyleTemplate template in StyleTemplates.All) comboStyleTemplate.Items.Add(template);
            if (comboStyleTemplate.Items.Count > 0) comboStyleTemplate.SelectedIndex = 0;

            comboKey.Items.AddRange(new object[]
            {
                "自動", "C Major", "C Minor", "C# Major", "C# Minor",
                "D Major", "D Minor", "D# Major", "D# Minor",
                "E Major", "E Minor", "F Major", "F Minor",
                "F# Major", "F# Minor", "G Major", "G Minor",
                "G# Major", "G# Minor", "A Major", "A Minor",
                "A# Major", "A# Minor", "B Major", "B Minor"
            });
            comboKey.SelectedIndex = 0;
            comboKey.MaxDropDownItems = 14;

            comboSignature.Items.AddRange(new object[] { "自動", "4", "3", "6" });
            comboSignature.SelectedIndex = 0;

            comboLanguage.Items.AddRange(new object[]
            {
                "unknown", "en", "zh", "yue", "ja", "ko", "es", "fr",
                "de", "it", "pt", "ru", "ar", "hi", "th", "vi", "id", "tr"
            });
            comboLanguage.SelectedIndex = 0;
            comboLanguage.MaxDropDownItems = 12;

            comboFormat.Items.AddRange(new object[] { "wav", "flac", "mp3" });
            comboFormat.SelectedIndex = 0;

            comboOffload.Items.AddRange(new object[] { "auto", "save_vram" });
            comboOffload.SelectedIndex = 0;

            comboTheme.Items.AddRange(new object[] { "Mocha 深色", "Latte 淺色" });
            comboTheme.SelectedIndex = 0;

            txtLog.Multiline = true;
            txtLog.ReadOnly = true;
            txtLog.ScrollBars = ScrollBars.Vertical;
            txtLog.WordWrap = true;
            txtLog.Font = ThemeManager.MonoFont;
            txtLog.Tag = "log";

            lstHistory.IntegralHeight = false;
            lstHistory.DrawMode = DrawMode.OwnerDrawFixed;
            lstHistory.ItemHeight = S(50);
            lstHistory.Font = ThemeManager.UiFont;
            lstHistory.DrawItem += OnHistoryDrawItem;
            lstHistory.SelectedIndexChanged += OnHistorySelected;
            lstHistory.DoubleClick += delegate(object sender, EventArgs e) { PlaySelected(); };

            seekBar.Minimum = 0;
            seekBar.Maximum = 1000;
            seekBar.Scroll += OnSeekScroll;
            seekBar.MouseDown += delegate(object sender, MouseEventArgs e) { scrubbing = true; };
            seekBar.MouseUp += delegate(object sender, MouseEventArgs e) { scrubbing = false; SeekTo(seekBar.Value / 1000.0); };
            waveform.SeekRequested += OnWaveformSeek;

            lblStatus.Text = "待命 · 首次載入模型約需 1–5 分鐘，載入後會保持常駐";
            lblStatus.AutoEllipsis = true;
            lblStatus.Tag = "title";
            lblStatus.Font = ThemeManager.HeadingFont;
            toolTip.SetToolTip(editorTabs, "切換音樂提示詞、歌詞、封面提示詞、影片提示詞");

            lblTime.Text = "00:00 / 00:00";

            lblTime.TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleRight;
            lblNowPlaying.Text = "尚未載入音檔";

            SetupButton(btnGenerate, "生成音樂", "sparkles", ButtonKind.Primary, OnGenerate);

        private static void SetupButton(ModernButton button, string text, string icon, ButtonKind kind, EventHandler handler)
        {
            button.Text = text;
            button.IconName = icon;
            button.Kind = kind;
            button.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            if (handler != null) button.Click += handler;
            button.Size = button.GetPreferredSize(Size.Empty);
        }

        private Control BuildHeader()
        {
            TableLayoutPanel header = new TableLayoutPanel();
            header.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            header.Margin = Dpi.S(0, 0, 0, 6);
            header.ColumnCount = 3;
            header.RowCount = 1;
            header.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Absolute, S(54)));
            header.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100F));
            header.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
            header.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100F));

            LogoTile logo = new LogoTile();
            logo.Anchor = AnchorStyles.Left;
            logo.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            header.Controls.Add(logo, 0, 0);

            TableLayoutPanel titles = new TableLayoutPanel();
            titles.Anchor = AnchorStyles.Left;
            titles.AutoSize = true;
            titles.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            titles.ColumnCount = 1;
            titles.RowCount = 2;
            Label title = new Label();
            title.Text = "AI Music Maker";
            title.Font = ThemeManager.TitleFont;
            title.Tag = "title";
            title.AutoSize = true;
            title.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            Label subtitle = new Label();
            subtitle.Text = "ACE-Step 1.5 · 本機離線生成";
            subtitle.Font = ThemeManager.SmallFont;
            subtitle.Tag = "muted";
            subtitle.AutoSize = true;
            subtitle.Margin = Dpi.S(2, 0, 0, 0);
            titles.Controls.Add(title, 0, 0);
            titles.Controls.Add(subtitle, 0, 1);
            header.Controls.Add(titles, 1, 0);

            FlowLayoutPanel actions = new FlowLayoutPanel();
            actions.AutoSize = true;
            actions.WrapContents = false;
            actions.Anchor = AnchorStyles.Right;
            actions.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            foreach (ModernButton button in new ModernButton[] { btnLink, btnAlbum, btnMediaTools, btnSd, btnAdvanced, btnTelegram })
            {
                button.Margin = Dpi.S(2, 0, 0, 0);
                actions.Controls.Add(button);
            }
            Panel divider = new Panel();
            divider.Size = new Size(Math.Max(1, S(1)), S(22));
            divider.Margin = Dpi.S(10, 8, 8, 0);
            divider.Paint += delegate(object sender, PaintEventArgs e) { e.Graphics.Clear(ThemeManager.BorderStrong); };
            actions.Controls.Add(divider);
            btnTheme.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            actions.Controls.Add(btnTheme);
            header.Controls.Add(actions, 2, 0);
            return header;
        }

        private Control BuildMainArea()
        {
            mainSplit.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            mainSplit.Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal;
            mainSplit.Margin = new Padding(0, 0, Gap, 0);
            mainSplit.SplitterWidth = Gap;
            mainSplit.TabStop = false;

            // top: one editor card that switches between prompt and lyrics
            editorCard = UiBuild.NewCard("創作", "wand-sparkles", PromptHint);
            editorCard.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            Panel editors = new Panel();
            editors.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            editors.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            promptHost = UiBuild.Host(txtCaption, Dpi.S(12, 10, 6, 8));
            lyricsHost = UiBuild.Host(txtLyrics, Dpi.S(12, 10, 6, 8));
            coverHost = UiBuild.Host(txtCover, Dpi.S(12, 10, 6, 8));
            videoHost = UiBuild.Host(txtVideo, Dpi.S(12, 10, 6, 8));
            lyricsHost.Visible = false;
            coverHost.Visible = false;
            videoHost.Visible = false;
            editors.Controls.Add(videoHost);
            editors.Controls.Add(coverHost);
            editors.Controls.Add(lyricsHost);
            editors.Controls.Add(promptHost);
            editorCard.Controls.Add(editors);
            editorTabs.SelectedIndexChanged += OnEditorTabChanged;
            comboStyleTemplate.Width = S(168);
            UiBuild.AddHeaderTools(editorCard, editorTabs, chkBatch, comboStyleTemplate, btnApplyTemplate, btnExpandPrompt);
            mainSplit.Panel1.Controls.Add(editorCard);

            // bottom: action bar, player, history + log
            TableLayoutPanel lower = new TableLayoutPanel();
            lower.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            lower.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            lower.ColumnCount = 2;
            lower.RowCount = 3;
            lower.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 42F));
            lower.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 58F));
            lower.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Absolute, ActionHeight + Gap));
            lower.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Absolute, PlayerHeight + Gap));
            lower.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100F));

            Control action = BuildActionCard();
            lower.Controls.Add(action, 0, 0);
            lower.SetColumnSpan(action, 2);
            Control player = BuildPlayerCard();
            lower.Controls.Add(player, 0, 1);
            lower.SetColumnSpan(player, 2);

            CardPanel historyCard = UiBuild.NewCard("歷史輸出", "list-music", "雙擊播放");
            historyCard.Margin = new Padding(0, 0, Gap / 2, 0);
            InputHost historyHost = UiBuild.Host(lstHistory, Dpi.S(4, 4, 2, 4));
            historyHost.Cursor = Cursors.Default;
            historyCard.Controls.Add(historyHost);
            UiBuild.AddHeaderTools(historyCard, btnPlay);
            lower.Controls.Add(historyCard, 0, 2);

            CardPanel logCard = UiBuild.NewCard("執行日誌", "scroll-text", "");
            logCard.Margin = new Padding(Gap / 2, 0, 0, 0);
            InputHost logHost = UiBuild.Host(txtLog, Dpi.S(10, 8, 2, 6));
            logHost.Tag = "log";
            logCard.Controls.Add(logHost);
            lower.Controls.Add(logCard, 1, 2);

            mainSplit.Panel2.Controls.Add(lower);
            return mainSplit;
        }

        private Control BuildSidebar()
        {
            TableLayoutPanel side = new TableLayoutPanel();
            side.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            side.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            side.ColumnCount = 1;
            side.RowCount = 2;
            side.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100F));
            side.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100F));
            side.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Absolute, S(150)));

            CardPanel paramsCard = UiBuild.NewCard("生成參數", "sliders-horizontal", "");
            paramsCard.Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, Gap);
            paramsCard.Padding = new Padding(S(16), CardPanel.HeaderHeight, S(4), S(10));
            Panel scroller = new Panel();
            scroller.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            scroller.AutoScroll = true;
            scroller.Margin = Padding.Empty;

            // 4 columns: caption | field | caption | field
            TableLayoutPanel grid = new TableLayoutPanel();
            grid.AutoSize = true;
            grid.AutoSizeMode = AutoSizeMode.GrowAndShrink;
            grid.Dock = DockStyle.Top;
            grid.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            grid.Padding = new Padding(0, 0, S(12), S(4));
            grid.ColumnCount = 4;
            grid.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
            grid.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 50F));
            grid.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
            grid.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 50F));

            UiBuild.AddSection(grid, "節奏與長度", false);
            UiBuild.AddWide(grid, "BPM", UiBuild.Row(UiBuild.Stepper(numBpm, S(120)), chkAutoBpm));
            // 時／分／秒 needs the full card width: caption on its own line, fields below.
            UiBuild.AddFull(grid, UiBuild.Caption("時長"), new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
            UiBuild.AddFull(grid, UiBuild.Row(
                UiBuild.Stepper(numHours, S(80)), UiBuild.Unit("時"),
                UiBuild.Stepper(numMinutes, S(84)), UiBuild.Unit("分"),
                UiBuild.Stepper(numSeconds, S(84)), UiBuild.Unit("秒")), new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
            UiBuild.AddSection(grid, "取樣", true);
            UiBuild.AddPair(grid, "步數", UiBuild.Stepper(numSteps, 0), "Guidance", UiBuild.Stepper(numGuidance, 0));
            InputHost seedHost = UiBuild.Host(txtSeed, UiBuild.TextPadding(txtSeed));
            seedHost.Height = S(36);
            UiBuild.AddWide(grid, "種子", UiBuild.Fill(seedHost, btnRandomSeed));
            UiBuild.AddSection(grid, "音樂", true);
            UiBuild.AddPair(grid, "調性", UiBuild.Combo(comboKey), "拍號", UiBuild.Combo(comboSignature));
            UiBuild.AddPair(grid, "語言", UiBuild.Combo(comboLanguage), "格式", UiBuild.Combo(comboFormat));
            UiBuild.AddSection(grid, "模型", true);
            UiBuild.AddWide(grid, "Offload", UiBuild.Row(UiBuild.ComboFixed(comboOffload, S(130)), UiBuild.Unit("下次載入模型時生效")));
            UiBuild.AddSection(grid, "選項", true);
            foreach (ToggleSwitch toggle in new ToggleSwitch[] { chkInstrumental, chkThinking, chkAutoVideo, chkKeepModel })
            {
                toggle.Margin = new Padding(0, S(1), 0, S(1));
                int row = grid.RowCount++;
                grid.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
                grid.Controls.Add(toggle, 0, row);
                grid.SetColumnSpan(toggle, 4);
            }
            scroller.Controls.Add(grid);
            paramsCard.Controls.Add(scroller);
            side.Controls.Add(paramsCard, 0, 0);

            CardPanel output = UiBuild.NewCard("輸出", "folder", "");
            output.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            TableLayoutPanel outGrid = new TableLayoutPanel();
            outGrid.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            outGrid.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            outGrid.ColumnCount = 2;
            outGrid.RowCount = 2;
            outGrid.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 50F));
            outGrid.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 50F));
            outGrid.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Absolute, S(44)));
            outGrid.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100F));
            InputHost dirHost = UiBuild.Host(txtOutDir, UiBuild.TextPadding(txtOutDir));
            Control dirRow = UiBuild.Fill(dirHost, btnBrowse);
            outGrid.Controls.Add(dirRow, 0, 0);
            outGrid.SetColumnSpan(dirRow, 2);
            btnOpen.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            btnOpen.Margin = new Padding(0, S(4), S(5), 0);
            btnCover.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            btnCover.Margin = new Padding(S(5), S(4), 0, 0);
            outGrid.Controls.Add(btnOpen, 0, 1);
            outGrid.Controls.Add(btnCover, 1, 1);
            output.Controls.Add(outGrid);
            side.Controls.Add(output, 0, 1);
            return side;
        }

        private void OnEditorTabChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            int tab = editorTabs.SelectedIndex;
            bool lyrics = tab == 1;
            bool cover = tab == 2;
            bool video = tab == 3;
            promptHost.Visible = tab == 0;
            lyricsHost.Visible = lyrics;
            coverHost.Visible = cover;
            videoHost.Visible = video;
            if (lyrics) editorCard.Subtitle = LyricsHint;
            else if (cover) editorCard.Subtitle = CoverHint;
            else if (video) editorCard.Subtitle = VideoHint;
            else editorCard.Subtitle = PromptHint;
            // template / batch / expand only act on the music prompt
            chkBatch.Visible = tab == 0;
            comboStyleTemplate.Visible = tab == 0;
            btnApplyTemplate.Visible = tab == 0;
            btnExpandPrompt.Visible = tab == 0;
            Control target = tab == 1 ? (Control)txtLyrics
                : tab == 2 ? (Control)txtCover
                : tab == 3 ? (Control)txtVideo
                : txtCaption;
            target.Focus();
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------------ theme & window chrome

        private void TryLoadIcon()
        {
            try
            {
                string iconPath = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, "app.ico");
                if (File.Exists(iconPath)) Icon = new Icon(iconPath);
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
            }
        }

        [DllImport("dwmapi.dll")]
        private static extern int DwmSetWindowAttribute(IntPtr hwnd, int attr, ref int value, int size);

        protected override void OnHandleCreated(EventArgs e)
        {
            base.OnHandleCreated(e);
            ApplyTitleBarTheme();
        }

        private void ApplyTitleBarTheme()
        {
            if (!IsHandleCreated) return;
            try
            {
                int enabled = ThemeManager.Dark ? 1 : 0;
                DwmSetWindowAttribute(Handle, 20, ref enabled, sizeof(int));
                DwmSetWindowAttribute(Handle, 19, ref enabled, sizeof(int));
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
            }
        }

        private void ApplyTheme()
        {
            ThemeManager.Apply(this);
            ThemeManager.StylePrimary(btnGenerate);
            ThemeManager.StylePrimary(btnPlayPause);
            btnCancel.Kind = ButtonKind.DangerOutline;
            btnTheme.IconName = ThemeManager.Dark ? "sun" : "moon";
            mainSplit.BackColor = ThemeManager.FormBack;
            waveform.ThemeDark = ThemeManager.Dark;
            comboTheme.SelectedIndex = ThemeManager.Dark ? 0 : 1;
            ApplyTitleBarTheme();
            ThemeManager.ApplyNativeScrollBars(this);
            Invalidate(true);
        }

        private void OnToggleTheme(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            ThemeManager.SetTheme(!ThemeManager.Dark);
            ApplyTheme();
            SaveSettings();
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------------ split ratio

        private void ApplySplitRatio()
        {
            int height = mainSplit.Height;
            if (height < S(200)) return;
            int lowerMin = ActionHeight + PlayerHeight + Gap * 2 + S(120);
            mainSplit.Panel1MinSize = Math.Min(S(240), Math.Max(S(120), height - lowerMin - Gap));
            mainSplit.Panel2MinSize = Math.Max(S(100), Math.Min(lowerMin, height - mainSplit.Panel1MinSize - Gap));
            int distance = (int)Math.Round(height * savedSplitRatio);
            int max = height - mainSplit.Panel2MinSize - mainSplit.SplitterWidth;
            distance = Math.Max(mainSplit.Panel1MinSize, Math.Min(max, distance));
            try { mainSplit.SplitterDistance = distance; } catch (Exception) { }
            splitApplied = true;
        }

        private double CurrentSplitRatio()
        {
            if (!splitApplied || mainSplit.Height <= 0) return savedSplitRatio;
            return Math.Round(mainSplit.SplitterDistance / (double)mainSplit.Height, 3);
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------------ player & history drawing

        private void UpdatePlaybackUi()
        {
            bool playing = audioOutput != null && audioOutput.PlaybackState == NAudio.Wave.PlaybackState.Playing;
            btnPlayPause.Text = playing ? "暫停" : "播放";
            btnPlayPause.IconName = playing ? "pause" : "play";
        }

        private void SetNowPlaying(string path)
        {
            lblNowPlaying.Text = string.IsNullOrEmpty(path) ? "尚未載入音檔" : Path.GetFileName(path);
        }

        private void OnHistoryDrawItem(object sender, DrawItemEventArgs e)
        {
            if (e.Index < 0) return;
            Graphics g = e.Graphics;
            bool selected = (e.State & DrawItemState.Selected) != 0;
            Color back = ThemeManager.InputBack;
            using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(back)) g.FillRectangle(brush, e.Bounds);
            Rectangle row = new Rectangle(e.Bounds.X + S(2), e.Bounds.Y + S(2), e.Bounds.Width - S(4), e.Bounds.Height - S(4));
            g.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
            if (selected)
            {
                using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath path = Shapes.RoundedRect(row, S(8)))
                using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ThemeManager.Blend(back, ThemeManager.Accent, ThemeManager.Dark ? 0.22 : 0.14)))
                    g.FillPath(brush, path);
            }
            int tileSize = S(32);
            Rectangle tile = new Rectangle(row.X + S(8), row.Y + (row.Height - tileSize) / 2, tileSize, tileSize);
            using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath path = Shapes.RoundedRect(tile, S(8)))
            using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ThemeManager.Blend(back, ThemeManager.Accent, selected ? 0.45 : 0.16)))
                g.FillPath(brush, path);
            int icon = S(16);
            IconRenderer.Draw(g, "music", new RectangleF(tile.X + (tileSize - icon) / 2F, tile.Y + (tileSize - icon) / 2F, icon, icon),
                selected ? ThemeManager.Text : ThemeManager.Accent);

            string text = lstHistory.Items[e.Index].ToString();
            string[] parts = text.Split(new string[] { "  |  " }, StringSplitOptions.None);
            string name = parts[0];
            string detail = parts.Length > 1 ? string.Join("  ·  ", parts, 1, parts.Length - 1) : "";
            int textX = tile.Right + S(10);
            int lineHeight = row.Height / 2;
            Rectangle nameRect = new Rectangle(textX, row.Y + S(2), row.Right - textX - S(8), lineHeight);
            TextRenderer.DrawText(g, name, ThemeManager.UiFont, nameRect, ThemeManager.Text,
                TextFormatFlags.Left | TextFormatFlags.Bottom | TextFormatFlags.EndEllipsis | TextFormatFlags.NoPrefix);
            if (detail.Length > 0)
            {
                Rectangle detailRect = new Rectangle(textX, row.Y + lineHeight + S(1), row.Right - textX - S(8), lineHeight - S(2));
                TextRenderer.DrawText(g, detail, ThemeManager.SmallFont, detailRect, ThemeManager.Muted,
                    TextFormatFlags.Left | TextFormatFlags.Top | TextFormatFlags.EndEllipsis | TextFormatFlags.NoPrefix);
            }
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------------ self tests

        internal string UiSelfTest()
        {
            StringBuilder report = new StringBuilder();
            try
            {
                Size = Dpi.S(1440, 900);
                PerformLayout();
                ThemeManager.SetTheme(false);
                ApplyTheme();
                report.AppendLine("light_form=" + BackColor.ToArgb() +
                                  " card=" + ThemeManager.CardBack.ToArgb() +
                                  " input=" + txtCaption.BackColor.ToArgb());
                ThemeManager.SetTheme(true);
                ApplyTheme();
                report.AppendLine("dark_form=" + BackColor.ToArgb() +
                                  " card=" + ThemeManager.CardBack.ToArgb() +
                                  " input=" + txtCaption.BackColor.ToArgb());
                report.AppendLine("dpi_factor=" + Dpi.Factor);
                int missing = 0;
                foreach (string name in new string[] { "sparkles", "x", "play", "pause", "square", "folder", "folder-open",
                    "link", "disc-3", "wrench", "image", "settings-2", "send", "sun", "moon", "dices", "music",
                    "list-music", "scroll-text", "sliders-horizontal", "wand-sparkles", "maximize-2", "audio-lines",
                    "audio-waveform" })
                {
                    if (IconRenderer.GetPath(name) == null) { missing++; report.AppendLine("icon_missing=" + name); }
                }
                report.AppendLine("icons_missing=" + missing);
                int oldSeconds = (int)numDuration.Value;
                SetDurationFromSeconds(7200);
                report.AppendLine("duration_max=" + numHours.Value + "h" + numMinutes.Value +
                                  "m" + numSeconds.Value + "s total=" + numDuration.Value);
                SetDurationFromSeconds(30);
                report.AppendLine("duration_30s=" + numHours.Value + "h" + numMinutes.Value +
                                  "m" + numSeconds.Value + "s total=" + numDuration.Value);
                SetDurationFromSeconds(oldSeconds);
                report.AppendLine("style_templates=" + comboStyleTemplate.Items.Count);
                comboStyleTemplate.SelectedIndex = 0;
                OnApplyStyleTemplate(this, EventArgs.Empty);
                report.AppendLine("template_apply=" + numBpm.Value + "|" +
                                  Convert.ToString(comboKey.SelectedItem) + "|" + txtCaption.TextLength + " chars");
                report.AppendLine("editor_tabs=" + editorTabs.Count +
                                  " cover_chars=" + txtCover.TextLength +
                                  " video_style=" + VideoStyleFromPrompt("ambient rain") +
                                  " cover_override=" + (CoverPromptFor("missing.mp3").Length > 0));
                report.AppendLine("auto_video=" + chkAutoVideo.Text + " checked=" + chkAutoVideo.Checked);
                using (MediaToolsDialog tools = new MediaToolsDialog(Paths.Generated, null))
                {
                    tools.CreateControl();
                    report.AppendLine("media_tools_controls=" + tools.Controls.Count);
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                report.AppendLine("error=" + ex);
            }
            return report.ToString();
        }

        /// <summary>Fills the window with demo content for --ui-screenshot.</summary>
        internal void PrepareScreenshot(bool dark, string demoDir)
        {
            string size = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("AIMUSIC_SHOT_SIZE");
            string screen = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("AIMUSIC_SHOT_SCREEN");
            WindowState = FormWindowState.Normal;
            StartPosition = FormStartPosition.Manual;
            Rectangle area = Screen.PrimaryScreen.WorkingArea;
            int index;
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(screen) && int.TryParse(screen, out index) && index < Screen.AllScreens.Length)
                area = Screen.AllScreens[index].WorkingArea;
            Size target = new Size(Math.Min(area.Width, S(1440)), Math.Min(area.Height, S(900)));
            if (size == "max") target = area.Size;
            Location = area.Location;
            Size = target;
            ThemeManager.SetTheme(dark);
            ApplyTheme();
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(demoDir))
            {
                txtOutDir.Text = demoDir;
                RefreshHistory();
                if (lstHistory.Items.Count > 1) lstHistory.SelectedIndex = 1;
            }
            txtCaption.Text = "Warm lo-fi hip hop, dusty boom bap drums, mellow Rhodes piano, jazzy guitar licks, " +
                              "upright bass, vinyl crackle and tape hiss, relaxed late-night study atmosphere, " +
                              "instrumental only, no vocals.";
            float[] peaks = new float[1024];
            Random random = new Random(7);
            for (int i = 0; i < peaks.Length; i++)
            {
                double envelope = 0.35 + 0.45 * Math.Abs(Math.Sin(i / 90.0)) + 0.15 * Math.Sin(i / 13.0);
                peaks[i] = (float)Math.Max(0.05, Math.Min(1.0, envelope * (0.7 + 0.3 * random.NextDouble())));
            }
            waveform.Peaks = peaks;
            waveform.Position = 0.37;
            seekBar.Value = 370;
            lblNowPlaying.Text = "ace_20261117_021553_seed1706262108.mp3";
            lblTime.Text = "02:57 / 08:00";
            lblStatus.Text = "生成音樂中（480s, 8 steps）... 42%  [已耗時 03:12]";
            bar.Style = ProgressBarStyle.Blocks;
            bar.Value = 42;
            btnCancel.Enabled = true;
            AppendLog("=== 啟動模型常駐程序（載入後保持，offload=auto）===");
            AppendLog("[gpu] NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3060 - VRAM free 10.84/12.00 GB");
            AppendLog("[stage] 載入 DiT 主模型 (acestep-v15-turbo)...");
            AppendLog("[info] DiT 載入 41.2s ok=True");
            AppendLog("=== 模型就緒 ===");
            AppendLog("[stage] 生成音樂中（480s, 8 steps）...");
        }
    }
}

            public FileItem(string path)

                Path = path;
            }

            public override string ToString()
            {
                return System.IO.Path.GetFileName(Path);
            }
        }

        private readonly string outputDir;

        {

            this.onChanged = onChanged;
            UiBuild.DialogParts parts = UiBuild.Dialog(this, "媒體工具",
                "淡入淡出、音量正規化、合併與預覽；處理結果另存新檔，不覆蓋原檔", "wrench", 1000, 700, true);

            numFadeIn.DecimalPlaces = 1;

        private static void AddAction(TableLayoutPanel grid, ModernButton button, string text, string icon,
            ButtonKind kind, EventHandler handler)
        {
            UiBuild.Button(button, text, icon, kind);
            button.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            button.Height = Dpi.S(38);
            button.Margin = new Padding(0, Dpi.S(4), 0, Dpi.S(4));
            button.Click += handler;
            UiBuild.AddFull(grid, button, new RowStyle(SizeType.Absolute, Dpi.S(46)));
        }

        private void OnDrawFile(object sender, DrawItemEventArgs e)
        {
            if (e.Index < 0) return;
            Graphics g = e.Graphics;
            bool selected = (e.State & DrawItemState.Selected) != 0;
            Color back = ThemeManager.InputBack;
            using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(back)) g.FillRectangle(brush, e.Bounds);
            Rectangle row = new Rectangle(e.Bounds.X + Dpi.S(2), e.Bounds.Y + Dpi.S(1), e.Bounds.Width - Dpi.S(4), e.Bounds.Height - Dpi.S(2));
            if (selected)
            {
                g.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
                using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath path = Shapes.RoundedRect(row, Dpi.S(6)))
                using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ThemeManager.Blend(back, ThemeManager.Accent, ThemeManager.Dark ? 0.24 : 0.14)))
                    g.FillPath(brush, path);
            }
            int icon = Dpi.S(16);
            IconRenderer.Draw(g, "music", new RectangleF(row.X + Dpi.S(8), row.Y + (row.Height - icon) / 2F, icon, icon),
                selected ? ThemeManager.Text : ThemeManager.Accent);
            Rectangle text = new Rectangle(row.X + Dpi.S(32), row.Y, row.Width - Dpi.S(36), row.Height);
            TextRenderer.DrawText(g, lstFiles.Items[e.Index].ToString(), lstFiles.Font, text, ThemeManager.Text,
                TextFormatFlags.Left | TextFormatFlags.VerticalCenter | TextFormatFlags.EndEllipsis | TextFormatFlags.NoPrefix);
        }

        private void RefreshFiles()
        {
            string selected = "";
            if (lstFiles.SelectedItem is FileItem)
                selected = ((FileItem)lstFiles.SelectedItem).Path;
            lstFiles.BeginUpdate();
            lstFiles.Items.Clear();
            try
            {
                if (Directory.Exists(outputDir))
                {
                    List<FileInfo> files = new List<FileInfo>();
                    foreach (FileInfo file in new DirectoryInfo(outputDir).GetFiles())
                    {
                        string ext = file.Extension.ToLowerInvariant();
                        if (ext == ".mp3" || ext == ".wav" || ext == ".flac")
                            files.Add(file);
                    }
                    files.Sort(delegate(FileInfo a, FileInfo b)
                    {
                        return b.LastWriteTime.CompareTo(a.LastWriteTime);
                    });
                    if (sortAscending) files.Reverse();
                    foreach (FileInfo file in files)
                        lstFiles.Items.Add(new FileItem(file.FullName));
                }
            }
            finally
            {
                lstFiles.EndUpdate();
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < lstFiles.Items.Count; i++)
            {
                FileItem item = lstFiles.Items[i] as FileItem;
                if (item != null && item.Path == selected)
                {
                    lstFiles.SelectedIndex = i;
                    break;
                }
            }
            lblStatus.Text = lstFiles.Items.Count + " 個音檔";
        }

        private List<string> SelectedPaths()
        {
            List<string> result = new List<string>();
            List<int> indices = new List<int>();
            foreach (int index in lstFiles.SelectedIndices) indices.Add(index);
            indices.Sort();
            foreach (int index in indices)
            {
                FileItem item = lstFiles.Items[index] as FileItem;
                if (item != null) result.Add(item.Path);
            }
            return result;
        }

        private void OnFade(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (busy) return;
            List<string> files = SelectedPaths();
            if (files.Count == 0)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(this, "請先選取至少一個音檔。", "媒體工具",
                    MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                return;
            }
            double fadeIn = (double)numFadeIn.Value;
            double fadeOut = (double)numFadeOut.Value;
            if (fadeIn <= 0 && fadeOut <= 0)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(this, "淡入與淡出不可同時為 0。", "媒體工具",
                    MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                return;
            }
            SetBusy(true);
            Log("=== 套用淡入淡出 ===");
            Thread worker = new Thread(delegate()
            {
                int failed = 0;
                foreach (string input in files)
                {
                    string output = Path.Combine(outputDir,
                        Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(input) + "_fade" + Path.GetExtension(input));
                    output = UniqueOutput(output);
                    Log("淡入淡出: " + Path.GetFileName(input));
                    int code = AudioEditRunner.Run(
                        Paths.BuildAudioFadeStartInfo(input, fadeIn, fadeOut, output), Log);
                    if (code != 0) failed++;
                }
                PostUi(delegate()
                {
                    SetBusy(false);
                    lblStatus.Text = failed == 0 ? "淡入淡出完成" : "淡入淡出完成，失敗 " + failed;
                    RefreshFiles();
                    if (onChanged != null) onChanged();
                });
            });
            worker.IsBackground = true;
            worker.Start();
        }

        private void OnNormalize(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (busy) return;
            List<string> files = SelectedPaths();
            if (files.Count == 0)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(this, "請先選取至少一個音檔。", "媒體工具",
                    MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                return;
            }
            SetBusy(true);
            Log("=== 音量正規化 -14 LUFS ===");
            Thread worker = new Thread(delegate()
            {
                int code = AudioEditRunner.Run(
                    Paths.BuildAudioNormalizeStartInfo(files, outputDir), Log);
                PostUi(delegate()
                {
                    SetBusy(false);
                    lblStatus.Text = code == 0 ? "音量正規化完成" : "音量正規化失敗";
                    RefreshFiles();
                    if (onChanged != null) onChanged();
                });
            });
            worker.IsBackground = true;
            worker.Start();
        }

        private void OnMerge(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (busy) return;
            List<string> files = SelectedPaths();
            if (files.Count < 2)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(this, "合併需要選取至少兩個音檔。", "媒體工具",
                    MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                return;
            }
            string output = Path.Combine(outputDir,
                "merged_" + DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss") + ".mp3");
            SetBusy(true);
            Log("=== 合併 " + files.Count + " 個音檔 ===");
            Thread worker = new Thread(delegate()
            {
                int code = AudioEditRunner.Run(
                    Paths.BuildAudioMergeStartInfo(files, output), Log);
                PostUi(delegate()
                {
                    SetBusy(false);
                    lblStatus.Text = code == 0
                        ? "合併完成: " + Path.GetFileName(output)
                        : "合併失敗";
                    RefreshFiles();
                    if (onChanged != null) onChanged();
                });
            });
            worker.IsBackground = true;
            worker.Start();
        }

        private void OnPreview(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (busy) return;
            List<string> files = SelectedPaths();
            if (files.Count != 1)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(this, "預覽請選取一個音檔。", "媒體工具",
                    MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                return;
            }
            string audio = files[0];
            string cover = Path.ChangeExtension(audio, ".cover.png");
            if (!File.Exists(cover)) cover = Paths.CoverFile;
            string video = Path.ChangeExtension(audio, ".mp4");
            using (PreviewDialog dialog = new PreviewDialog(audio, cover, video))
                dialog.ShowDialog(this);
        }

        private void SetBusy(bool value)
        {
            busy = value;
            lstFiles.Enabled = !value;
            numFadeIn.Enabled = !value;
            numFadeOut.Enabled = !value;
            btnFade.Enabled = !value;
            btnMerge.Enabled = !value;
            btnNormalize.Enabled = !value;
            btnPreview.Enabled = !value;
            btnRefresh.Enabled = !value;
            btnSort.Enabled = !value;
        }

        private static string UniqueOutput(string path)
        {
            if (!File.Exists(path)) return path;
            return Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(path),
                Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(path) + "_" +
                DateTime.Now.ToString("HHmmss") + Path.GetExtension(path));
        }

        private void Log(string text)
        {
            if (IsDisposed) return;
            if (InvokeRequired)
            {
                try { BeginInvoke(new Action<string>(Log), text); }
                catch (InvalidOperationException) { }
                return;
            }
            txtLog.AppendText(text + Environment.NewLine);
        }

        private void PostUi(Action action)
        {
            if (IsDisposed) return;
            try { BeginInvoke(action); }
            catch (InvalidOperationException) { }
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text;

namespace AIMusicGenerator
{
    internal static class Paths
    {
        private static string cachedWorkspace;

        public static string Workspace
        {
            get
            {
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cachedWorkspace)) return cachedWorkspace;
                string dir = AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory.TrimEnd('\\');
                for (int i = 0; i < 6 && dir != null; i++)
                {
                    if (File.Exists(Path.Combine(dir, "AI_Music.venv", "Scripts", "python.exe")))
                    {
                        cachedWorkspace = dir;
                        return cachedWorkspace;
                    }
                    DirectoryInfo parent = Directory.GetParent(dir);
                    if (parent == null) break;
                    dir = parent.FullName;
                }
                cachedWorkspace = @"G:\AIMUSIC";
                return cachedWorkspace;
            }
        }

        public static string PythonExe
        {
            get { return Path.Combine(Workspace, "AI_Music.venv", "Scripts", "python.exe"); }
        }

        public static string Backend
        {
            get { return Path.Combine(Workspace, "AI_Music", "app", "generate_backend.py"); }
        }

        public static string VideoTool
        {
            get { return Path.Combine(Workspace, "AI_Music", "scripts", "music_to_video.py"); }
        }

        public static string AudioEditTool
        {
            get { return Path.Combine(Workspace, "AI_Music", "scripts", "audio_edit.py"); }
        }

        public static string GpuHoldTool
        {
            get { return Path.Combine(Workspace, "AI_Music", "scripts", "gpu_lock_hold.py"); }
        }

        public static string FfmpegExe
        {
            get { return Path.Combine(Workspace, "AI_Music", "tools", "ffmpeg", "bin", "ffmpeg.exe"); }
        }

        public static string Generated
        {
            get { return Path.Combine(Workspace, "AI_Music", "generated"); }
        }

        public static string SettingsDir
        {
            get
            {
                return Path.Combine(
                    Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.ApplicationData),
                    "AIMusicGenerator");
            }
        }

        public static string SettingsFile
        {
            get { return Path.Combine(SettingsDir, "settings.json"); }
        }

        public static string LegacySettingsFile
        {
            get { return Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, "settings.json"); }
        }

        public static string TempDir
        {
            get { return Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "AIMusicGenerator"); }
        }

        public static string CoverFile
        {
            get { return Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, "cover.png"); }
        }

        public static string Quote(string value)
        {
            StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();
            builder.Append('"');
            int backslashes = 0;
            foreach (char ch in value)
            {
                if (ch == '\\')
                {
                    backslashes++;
                    continue;
                }
                if (ch == '"')
                {
                    builder.Append('\\', backslashes * 2 + 1);
                    builder.Append('"');
                    backslashes = 0;
                    continue;
                }
                if (backslashes > 0)
                {
                    builder.Append('\\', backslashes);
                    backslashes = 0;
                }
                builder.Append(ch);
            }
            if (backslashes > 0) builder.Append('\\', backslashes * 2);
            builder.Append('"');
            return builder.ToString();
        }

        public static ProcessStartInfo BuildStartInfo(string configPath)
        {
            return CreatePythonStartInfo(
                "-u " + Quote(Backend) + " --config " + Quote(configPath), false);
        }

        public static ProcessStartInfo BuildVideoStartInfo(string audioPath, string imagePath)
        {
            return BuildVideoStartInfo(audioPath, imagePath, null, 0);
        }

        public static ProcessStartInfo BuildVideoStartInfo(string audioPath, string imagePath,
            string style, int fps)
        {
            string arguments = "-u " + Quote(VideoTool) + " " + Quote(audioPath);
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(imagePath)) arguments += " --image " + Quote(imagePath);
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(style)) arguments += " --style " + style;
            if (fps > 0) arguments += " --fps " + fps.ToString(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            return CreatePythonStartInfo(arguments, false);
        }

        public static ProcessStartInfo BuildGpuHoldStartInfo(string source, int waitSeconds)
        {
            return CreatePythonStartInfo(
                "-u " + Quote(GpuHoldTool) + " " + Quote(source) + " " +
                waitSeconds.ToString(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), true);
        }

        public static ProcessStartInfo BuildAudioFadeStartInfo(string inputPath,
            double fadeIn, double fadeOut, string outputPath)
        {
            string arguments = "-u " + Quote(AudioEditTool) + " fade " + Quote(inputPath) +
                " --fade-in " + fadeIn.ToString("0.0", System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) +
                " --fade-out " + fadeOut.ToString("0.0", System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) +
                " --out " + Quote(outputPath) + " --overwrite";
            return CreatePythonStartInfo(arguments, false);
        }

        public static ProcessStartInfo BuildAudioMergeStartInfo(IEnumerable<string> inputs,
            string outputPath)
        {
            StringBuilder arguments = new StringBuilder();
            arguments.Append("-u ").Append(Quote(AudioEditTool)).Append(" merge");
            foreach (string input in inputs)
                arguments.Append(" ").Append(Quote(input));
            arguments.Append(" --out ").Append(Quote(outputPath)).Append(" --overwrite");
            return CreatePythonStartInfo(arguments.ToString(), false);
        }

        public static ProcessStartInfo BuildAudioNormalizeStartInfo(IEnumerable<string> inputs,
            string outputDir)
        {
            StringBuilder arguments = new StringBuilder();
            arguments.Append("-u ").Append(Quote(AudioEditTool)).Append(" normalize");
            foreach (string input in inputs)
                arguments.Append(" ").Append(Quote(input));
            arguments.Append(" --outdir ").Append(Quote(outputDir)).Append(" --overwrite");
            return CreatePythonStartInfo(arguments.ToString(), false);
        }

        public static ProcessStartInfo BuildDaemonStartInfo(string offloadMode)
        {
            return CreatePythonStartInfo(
                "-u " + Quote(Backend) + " --daemon --offload-mode " + Quote(offloadMode) +
                " --gpu-wait 20", true);
        }

        public static ProcessStartInfo BuildLimitsStartInfo()
        {
            return CreatePythonStartInfo("-u " + Quote(Backend) + " --limits", false);
        }

        private static ProcessStartInfo CreatePythonStartInfo(string arguments, bool redirectInput)
        {
            ProcessStartInfo psi = new ProcessStartInfo(PythonExe);
            psi.Arguments = arguments;
            psi.UseShellExecute = false;
            psi.CreateNoWindow = true;
            psi.RedirectStandardOutput = true;
            psi.RedirectStandardError = true;
            psi.RedirectStandardInput = redirectInput;
            psi.StandardOutputEncoding = Encoding.UTF8;
            psi.StandardErrorEncoding = Encoding.UTF8;
            psi.EnvironmentVariables["PYTHONIOENCODING"] = "utf-8";
            psi.WorkingDirectory = Workspace;
            return psi;
        }

        [DllImport("shell32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
        private static extern IntPtr CommandLineToArgvW(
            [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPWStr)] string lpCmdLine, out int pNumArgs);

        [DllImport("kernel32.dll")]
        private static extern IntPtr LocalFree(IntPtr hMem);

        public static bool SelfTestQuoting(out string details)
        {
            string[] cases = { @"D:\", @"D:\a b\", "D:\\a\"b" };
            details = "";
            foreach (string value in cases)
            {
                string commandLine = "\"prog\" " + Quote(value);
                int argc;
                IntPtr argv = CommandLineToArgvW(commandLine, out argc);
                if (argv == IntPtr.Zero)
                {
                    details = "CommandLineToArgvW failed for " + value;
                    return false;
                }
                string parsed = argc >= 2 ? Marshal.PtrToStringUni(Marshal.ReadIntPtr(argv, IntPtr.Size)) : null;
                LocalFree(argv);
                if (argc != 2 || parsed != value)
                {
                    details = "quote round-trip failed: input=<" + value + "> argc=" + argc +
                              " parsed=<" + parsed + ">";
                    return false;
                }
            }
            details = "quote round-trip OK (" + cases.Length + " cases)";
            return true;
        }
    }
}

using NAudio.Wave;

namespace AIMusicGenerator

    internal sealed class PreviewDialog : Form

            info.ColumnCount = 2;
            info.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100F));
            info.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));

            lblTitle.TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleLeft;
            lblTitle.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            lblTime.Text = "00:00 / 00:00";
            lblTime.Font = ThemeManager.MonoFont;
            lblTime.AutoSize = true;
            lblTime.Anchor = AnchorStyles.Right;
            lblTime.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            info.Controls.Add(lblTitle, 0, 0);
            info.Controls.Add(lblTime, 1, 0);
            grid.Controls.Add(info, 1, 0);

            seek.Minimum = 0;

            seek.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            seek.Margin = new Padding(0, Dpi.S(4), 0, Dpi.S(4));
            seek.Scroll += delegate
            {
                if (reader == null || scrubbing) return;
                try { reader.CurrentTime = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(reader.TotalTime.TotalSeconds * seek.Value / 1000.0); }
                catch (Exception) { }
            };
            seek.MouseDown += delegate { scrubbing = true; };
            seek.MouseUp += delegate
            {
                scrubbing = false;
                if (reader == null) return;
                try { reader.CurrentTime = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(reader.TotalTime.TotalSeconds * seek.Value / 1000.0); }
                catch (Exception) { }
            };
            grid.Controls.Add(seek, 1, 1);
            player.Controls.Add(grid);
            UiBuild.AddFull(body, player, new RowStyle(SizeType.Absolute, Dpi.S(96)));

            UiBuild.Button(btnVideo, "開啟 MP4", "video", ButtonKind.Secondary);
            btnVideo.Enabled = videoPath.Length > 0 && File.Exists(videoPath);
            btnVideo.Click += delegate
            {
                if (videoPath.Length > 0 && File.Exists(videoPath))
                    Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo(videoPath) { UseShellExecute = true });
            };
            ModernButton close = UiBuild.Button(new ModernButton(), "關閉", "", ButtonKind.Secondary);
            close.Click += delegate { Close(); };
            UiBuild.AddFooterButton(parts.Footer, close);
            UiBuild.AddFooterButton(parts.Footer, btnVideo);
            CancelButton = close;

            ThemeManager.ApplyDialog(this);

            try
            {
                reader = AudioTools.OpenReader(audioPath);
                output.Init(reader);
                timer.Interval = 200;
                timer.Tick += OnTimer;
                timer.Start();
                output.Play();
                SetPlayButton(true);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                lblTitle.Text = "無法載入音訊: " + ex.Message;
                btnPlay.Enabled = false;
            }
            FormClosed += delegate { DisposeAudio(); };
        }

        private void SetPlayButton(bool playing)
        {
            btnPlay.Text = playing ? "暫停" : "播放";
            btnPlay.IconName = playing ? "pause" : "play";
        }

        private void OnPlayPause(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (reader == null) return;
            if (output.PlaybackState == PlaybackState.Playing)
            {
                output.Pause();
                SetPlayButton(false);
            }
            else
            {
                output.Play();
                SetPlayButton(true);
            }
        }

        private void OnTimer(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (reader == null || scrubbing) return;
            double total = reader.TotalTime.TotalSeconds;
            double current = reader.CurrentTime.TotalSeconds;
            if (total > 0)
                seek.Value = Math.Max(0, Math.Min(1000, (int)(current / total * 1000)));
            lblTime.Text = FormatTime(reader.CurrentTime) + " / " + FormatTime(reader.TotalTime);
        }

        private static string FormatTime(TimeSpan value)
        {
            return ((int)value.TotalMinutes).ToString("00") + ":" + value.Seconds.ToString("00");
        }

        private void DisposeAudio()
        {
            timer.Stop();
            try { output.Stop(); } catch (Exception) { }
            try { output.Dispose(); } catch (Exception) { }
            try { if (reader != null) reader.Dispose(); } catch (Exception) { }
        }
    }
}


===== [23/66] AI_Music/app/src/ProcessSupervisor.cs  (1175 bytes) =====
using System;
using System.Diagnostics;

namespace AIMusicGenerator
{
    internal static class ProcessSupervisor
    {
        public static bool StopTree(Process process, int waitMilliseconds)
        {
            if (process == null) return true;
            try
            {
                if (!process.HasExited)
                {
                    Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo("taskkill",
                        "/PID " + process.Id + " /T /F")
                    {
                        UseShellExecute = false,
                        CreateNoWindow = true
                    });
                }
                if (!process.WaitForExit(Math.Max(0, waitMilliseconds)))
                {
                    try
                    {
                        process.Kill();
                        process.WaitForExit(2000);
                    }
                    catch (Exception)
                    {
                    }
                }
                return process.HasExited;
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
}

    internal static class Program
