        [STAThread]
        private static void Main(string[] args)
        {
            if (args.Length >= 2 && args[0] == "--auto")
            {
                Environment.Exit(AutoRunner.Run(args[1]));
                return;
            }
            if (args.Length >= 3 && args[0] == "--audio-edit-test")
            {
                try
                {
                    System.Text.StringBuilder log = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
                    string input = args[1];
                    string outDir = args[2];
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(outDir);
                    string fadeOut = Path.Combine(outDir,
                        Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(input) + "_fade_test" + Path.GetExtension(input));
                    int fadeCode = AudioEditRunner.Run(
                        Paths.BuildAudioFadeStartInfo(input, 1.0, 1.0, fadeOut),
                        delegate(string line) { log.AppendLine(line); });
                    int normCode = AudioEditRunner.Run(
                        Paths.BuildAudioNormalizeStartInfo(new string[] { input }, outDir),
                        delegate(string line) { log.AppendLine(line); });
                    int mergeCode = 0;
                    if (args.Length >= 4)
                    {
                        string merged = Path.Combine(outDir, "merged_test.mp3");
                        mergeCode = AudioEditRunner.Run(
                            Paths.BuildAudioMergeStartInfo(new string[] { input, args[3] }, merged),
                            delegate(string line) { log.AppendLine(line); });
                    }
                    log.AppendLine("fade=" + fadeCode + " normalize=" + normCode +
                                   " merge=" + mergeCode);
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(Paths.TempDir);
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Paths.TempDir, "audio_edit_test.txt"), log.ToString());
                    Environment.Exit(fadeCode == 0 && normCode == 0 && mergeCode == 0 ? 0 : 1);
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(Paths.TempDir);
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Paths.TempDir, "audio_edit_test.txt"), ex.ToString());

                MainForm form = new MainForm();

                        Directory.CreateDirectory(Paths.TempDir);
                        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Paths.TempDir, "auto_video_test.txt"), report);
                        Environment.Exit(report.StartsWith("ok") ? 0 : 1);
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(Paths.TempDir);
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Paths.TempDir, "auto_video_test.txt"), ex.ToString());
                    Environment.Exit(1);
                }
                return;
            }
            if (args.Length >= 1 && args[0] == "--ui-selftest")
            {
                try

                    using (MainForm form = new MainForm())
                    {
                        string report = form.UiSelfTest();
                        Directory.CreateDirectory(Paths.TempDir);
                        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Paths.TempDir, "ui_selftest.txt"), report);
                        Environment.Exit(0);
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(Paths.TempDir);
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Paths.TempDir, "ui_selftest.txt"), ex.ToString());
                    Environment.Exit(1);
                }
                return;
            }
            if (args.Length >= 2 && args[0] == "--link-test")
            {
                try
                {
                    LinkInfo info = LinkAnalyzer.Analyze(args[1]);
                    bool zh = args.Length >= 3 && args[2].Equals("zh", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                    PromptSuggestion suggestion = LinkAnalyzer.Suggest(info, true, zh);
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(Paths.TempDir);
                    string body = "source=" + info.Source + "\r\nartist=" + info.Artist +
                                  "\r\ntitle=" + info.Title + "\r\nalbum=" + info.Album +
                                  "\r\nyear=" + info.Year + "\r\ngenre=" + suggestion.Genre +
                                  "\r\nbpm=" + suggestion.Bpm + "\r\nprompt=" + suggestion.Prompt +
                                  "\r\nnegative=" + suggestion.Negative;
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Paths.TempDir, "link_test.txt"), body);
                    Environment.Exit(0);
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(Paths.TempDir);
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Paths.TempDir, "link_test.txt"), ex.ToString());
                    Environment.Exit(1);
                }
                return;
            }

            if (args.Length >= 2 && args[0] == "--player-test")
            {
                try
                {
                    using (MainForm form = new MainForm())
                    {
                        string report = form.PlayerTest(args[1]);
                        Directory.CreateDirectory(Paths.TempDir);
                        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Paths.TempDir, "player_test.txt"), report);
                        Environment.Exit(0);
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(Paths.TempDir);
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Paths.TempDir, "player_test.txt"), ex.ToString());
                    Environment.Exit(1);
                }
                return;
            }
            if (args.Length >= 2 && args[0] == "--audio-test")
            {
                try
                {
                    double seconds;
                    float[] peaks = AudioTools.BuildPeaks(args[1], 128, out seconds);
                    string copy = Path.Combine(Paths.TempDir, "audio_test_copy" + Path.GetExtension(args[1]));
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(Paths.TempDir);
                    File.Copy(args[1], copy, true);
                    AudioTools.WriteTags(copy, "Audio test prompt", 123456, "78", "C Major", "wav", Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, "cover.png"));
                    string comment = AudioTools.ReadComment(copy);
                    string report = "seconds=" + seconds.ToString("F2") + " peaks=" + peaks.Length +
                                    " peak_max=" + MaxPeak(peaks).ToString("F3") + " comment=" + comment;
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Paths.TempDir, "audio_test.txt"), report);
                    Environment.Exit(0);
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(Paths.TempDir);
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Paths.TempDir, "audio_test.txt"), ex.ToString());
                    Environment.Exit(1);
                }
                return;
            }
            if (args.Length >= 1 && args[0] == "--style-report")
            {
                Application.EnableVisualStyles();
                Application.SetCompatibleTextRenderingDefault(false);
                MainForm form = new MainForm();
                form.ShowInTaskbar = false;
                form.Opacity = 0;
                form.Show();
                Application.DoEvents();
                System.Threading.Thread.Sleep(400);
                Application.DoEvents();
                System.IO.Directory.CreateDirectory(Paths.TempDir);
                System.IO.File.WriteAllText(System.IO.Path.Combine(Paths.TempDir, "style_report.txt"), form.StyleReport());
                form.Close();
                Environment.Exit(0);
                return;
            }
            if (args.Length >= 2 && args[0] == "--ui-screenshot")
            {
                // --ui-screenshot <out.png> [dark|light] [demoOutputDir] [dialog]
                Application.EnableVisualStyles();
                Application.SetCompatibleTextRenderingDefault(false);
                string target = args[1];
                bool dark = args.Length < 3 || !args[2].Equals("light", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                string demo = args.Length >= 4 ? args[3] : "";
                string dialog = args.Length >= 5 ? args[4] : "";
                int code = UiScreenshot.Run(target, dark, demo, dialog);
                Environment.Exit(code);
                return;
            }
            if (args.Length >= 1 && args[0] == "--ui-test-batch")
            {
                Application.EnableVisualStyles();
                Application.SetCompatibleTextRenderingDefault(false);
                MainForm form = new MainForm();
                form.StartUiTest(true);
                Application.Run(form);
                Environment.Exit(form.UiTestResult);
                return;
            }
            if (args.Length >= 1 && args[0] == "--ui-test")
            {
                Application.EnableVisualStyles();
                Application.SetCompatibleTextRenderingDefault(false);
                MainForm form = new MainForm();
                form.StartUiTest(false);
                Application.Run(form);
                Environment.Exit(form.UiTestResult);
                return;
            }
            if (args.Length >= 1 && args[0] == "--selftest")
            {
                Application.EnableVisualStyles();
                Application.SetCompatibleTextRenderingDefault(false);
                int code = 0;
                try
                {
                    using (MainForm form = new MainForm())
                    {
                        form.CreateControl();
                    }
                    string quoteDetails;
                    if (!Paths.SelfTestQuoting(out quoteDetails))
                    {
                        WriteSelftestError(quoteDetails);
                        code = 1;
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    WriteSelftestError(ex.ToString());
                    code = 1;
                }
                Environment.Exit(code);
                return;
            }
            Application.EnableVisualStyles();
            Application.SetCompatibleTextRenderingDefault(false);
            Application.Run(new MainForm());
        }

        private static float MaxPeak(float[] values)
        {
            float max = 0f;
            foreach (float value in values) if (value > max) max = value;
            return max;
        }

        private static void WriteSelftestError(string message)
        {
            try
            {
                string dir = Paths.TempDir;
                Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(dir, "selftest_error.txt"),
                    DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss") + Environment.NewLine + message,
                    new System.Text.UTF8Encoding(false));
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
            }
            Console.Error.WriteLine(message);
        }
    }
}

===== [26/66] AI_Music/app/src/SdCoverClient.cs  (8378 bytes) =====
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Net;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading;
using System.Web.Script.Serialization;

namespace AIMusicGenerator
{
    internal static class SdCoverClient
    {
        private static readonly JavaScriptSerializer Serializer = new JavaScriptSerializer();
        private static readonly object StartGate = new object();
        private static Process serverProcess;

        static SdCoverClient()
        {
            try { ServicePointManager.SecurityProtocol |= SecurityProtocolType.Tls12; }
            catch (Exception) { }
        }

        public static bool EnsureApi(string url, string installPath, Action<string> log)
        {
            lock (StartGate)
            {
            if (IsReady(url)) return true;
            string python = Path.Combine(installPath, "venv", "Scripts", "python.exe");
            string launch = Path.Combine(installPath, "launch.py");
            if (!File.Exists(python) || !File.Exists(launch))
            {
                if (log != null) log("找不到 Stable Diffusion WebUI: " + installPath);
                return false;
            }
            try
            {
                if (log != null) log("啟動 Stable Diffusion WebUI API...");
                int port = 7860;
                Uri parsed;
                if (Uri.TryCreate(url, UriKind.Absolute, out parsed) && !parsed.IsDefaultPort)
                    port = parsed.Port;
                ProcessStartInfo psi = new ProcessStartInfo(python);
                psi.Arguments = "\"" + launch + "\" --api --nowebui --lowvram --disable-extra-extensions --port " + port;
                psi.WorkingDirectory = installPath;
                psi.UseShellExecute = false;
                psi.CreateNoWindow = true;
                psi.RedirectStandardOutput = true;
                psi.RedirectStandardError = true;
                serverProcess = new Process();
                serverProcess.StartInfo = psi;
                serverProcess.OutputDataReceived += delegate { };
                serverProcess.ErrorDataReceived += delegate { };
                serverProcess.Start();
                serverProcess.BeginOutputReadLine();
                serverProcess.BeginErrorReadLine();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                if (log != null) log("SD 啟動失敗: " + ex.Message);
                return false;
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < 80; i++)
            {
                Thread.Sleep(3000);
                if (IsReady(url))
                {
                    if (log != null) log("SD API 就緒");
                    return true;
                }
            }
            if (log != null) log("SD API 啟動逾時");
            return false;
            }
        }

        public static bool IsReady(string url)
        {
            try
            {
                Get(url.TrimEnd('/') + "/sdapi/v1/options", 4000);
                return true;
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        public static List<string> GetModels(string url)
        {
            string json = Get(url.TrimEnd('/') + "/sdapi/v1/sd-models", 30000);
            List<Dictionary<string, object>> list =
                Serializer.Deserialize<List<Dictionary<string, object>>>(json);
            List<string> result = new List<string>();
            if (list != null)
            {
                foreach (Dictionary<string, object> item in list)
                {
                    object title;
                    if (item.TryGetValue("title", out title) && title != null)
                        result.Add(Convert.ToString(title));
                }
            }
            return result;
        }

        public static void SelectModel(string url, string title)
        {
            string body = Serializer.Serialize(new Dictionary<string, object>
            {
                { "sd_model_checkpoint", title }
            });
            PostJson(url.TrimEnd('/') + "/sdapi/v1/options", body, 300000);
        }

        public static void GenerateCover(string url, string prompt, string negative,
            int width, int height, int steps, string outputPath)
        {
            Dictionary<string, object> payload = new Dictionary<string, object>();
            payload["prompt"] = prompt;
            payload["negative_prompt"] = negative;
            payload["steps"] = steps;
            payload["width"] = width;
            payload["height"] = height;
            payload["cfg_scale"] = 7;
            payload["sampler_name"] = "Euler a";
            payload["seed"] = -1;
            string json = PostJson(url.TrimEnd('/') + "/sdapi/v1/txt2img",
                Serializer.Serialize(payload), 900000);
            Dictionary<string, object> data = Serializer.Deserialize<Dictionary<string, object>>(json);
            object imagesObject;
            if (data == null || !data.TryGetValue("images", out imagesObject))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("SD 回應沒有 images");
            IEnumerable images = imagesObject as IEnumerable;
            if (images == null) throw new InvalidOperationException("SD 回應 images 格式錯誤");
            string base64 = null;
            foreach (object image in images)
            {
                base64 = Convert.ToString(image);
                break;
            }
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(base64)) throw new InvalidOperationException("SD 回應 images 為空");
            int comma = base64.IndexOf(',');
            if (comma >= 0 && base64.Substring(0, comma).Contains("base64"))
                base64 = base64.Substring(comma + 1);
            byte[] bytes = Convert.FromBase64String(base64);
            File.WriteAllBytes(outputPath, bytes);
        }

        public static void UnloadCheckpoint(string url)
        {
            try
            {
                PostJson(url.TrimEnd('/') + "/sdapi/v1/unload-checkpoint", "{}", 30000);
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
            }
        }

        public static void Stop()
        {
            Process process = serverProcess;
            serverProcess = null;
            if (process == null) return;
            try
            {
                if (!process.HasExited)
                {
                    Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo("taskkill",
                        "/PID " + process.Id + " /T /F")
                    {
                        UseShellExecute = false,
                        CreateNoWindow = true
                    });
                }
            }
            catch (Exception) { }
            try { process.Dispose(); } catch (Exception) { }
        }

        private static string Get(string url, int timeout)
        {
            HttpWebRequest request = (HttpWebRequest)WebRequest.Create(url);
            request.Timeout = timeout;
            request.ReadWriteTimeout = timeout;
            using (HttpWebResponse response = (HttpWebResponse)request.GetResponse())
            using (StreamReader reader = new StreamReader(response.GetResponseStream(), Encoding.UTF8))
                return reader.ReadToEnd();
        }

        private static string PostJson(string url, string body, int timeout)
        {
            HttpWebRequest request = (HttpWebRequest)WebRequest.Create(url);
            request.Method = "POST";
            request.ContentType = "application/json";
            request.Timeout = timeout;
            request.ReadWriteTimeout = timeout;
            byte[] bytes = Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(body);
            request.ContentLength = bytes.Length;
            using (Stream stream = request.GetRequestStream())
                stream.Write(bytes, 0, bytes.Length);
            using (HttpWebResponse response = (HttpWebResponse)request.GetResponse())
            using (StreamReader reader = new StreamReader(response.GetResponseStream(), Encoding.UTF8))
                return reader.ReadToEnd();
        }
    }
}


===== [27/66] AI_Music/app/src/SdDialog.cs  (7062 bytes) =====
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Threading;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace AIMusicGenerator
{
    internal sealed class SdDialog : Form
    {
        private readonly CheckBox chkEnable = new ToggleSwitch();
        private readonly TextBox txtUrl = new TextBox();
        private readonly TextBox txtPath = new TextBox();
        private readonly ComboBox comboModel = new ThemedComboBox();
        private readonly ModernButton btnRefresh = new ModernButton();
        private readonly NumericUpDown numSteps = new NumericUpDown();
        private readonly ComboBox comboSize = new ThemedComboBox();
        private readonly Label lblStatus = new Label();

        public bool EnabledForCover { get; private set; }
        public string ApiUrl { get; private set; }
        public string InstallPath { get; private set; }
        public string ModelTitle { get; private set; }
        public int Steps { get; private set; }
        public int CoverSize { get; private set; }

        public SdDialog(bool enabled, string url, string path, string model, int steps, int size)
        {
            UiBuild.DialogParts parts = UiBuild.Dialog(this, "Stable Diffusion 封面",
                "音樂完成後呼叫本機 WebUI API 產生專輯封面", "image", 640, 520, false);
            TableLayoutPanel body = parts.Body;
            body.ColumnCount = 4;
            body.ColumnStyles.Clear();
            body.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
            body.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 50F));
            body.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
            body.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 50F));

            chkEnable.Text = "音樂生成後，自動用 Stable Diffusion 產生封面";
            chkEnable.Checked = enabled;
            chkEnable.Margin = new Padding(0, Dpi.S(2), 0, Dpi.S(6));
            UiBuild.AddFull(body, chkEnable, new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));

            txtUrl.Text = url;
            txtPath.Text = path;
            UiBuild.AddSection(body, "WebUI", true);
            UiBuild.AddWide(body, "API 網址", UiBuild.TextField(txtUrl));
            UiBuild.AddWide(body, "安裝路徑", UiBuild.TextField(txtPath));

            comboModel.Items.Add("(尚未取得模型清單)");
            comboModel.SelectedIndex = 0;
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(model))
            {
                comboModel.Items.Add(model);
                comboModel.SelectedItem = model;
            }
            UiBuild.Button(btnRefresh, "重新整理", "refresh-cw", ButtonKind.Secondary);
            btnRefresh.Click += OnRefresh;
            Control modelCombo = UiBuild.Combo(comboModel);
            UiBuild.AddSection(body, "模型與品質", true);
            UiBuild.AddWide(body, "模型", UiBuild.Fill(modelCombo, btnRefresh));

            numSteps.Minimum = 8;
            numSteps.Maximum = 40;
            numSteps.Value = Math.Max(8, Math.Min(40, steps));
            comboSize.Items.AddRange(new object[] { "512", "640", "768" });
            comboSize.SelectedItem = size.ToString();
            if (comboSize.SelectedIndex < 0) comboSize.SelectedIndex = 0;
            UiBuild.AddPair(body, "Steps", UiBuild.Stepper(numSteps, 0), "尺寸", UiBuild.Combo(comboSize));

            lblStatus.Text = "提示：第一次啟動 WebUI 約需 1–3 分鐘。";
            lblStatus.Tag = "accent";
            lblStatus.AutoSize = true;
            lblStatus.MaximumSize = new Size(Dpi.S(580), 0);
            lblStatus.Margin = new Padding(0, Dpi.S(12), 0, 0);
            UiBuild.AddFull(body, lblStatus, new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
            body.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100F));
            body.RowCount++;

            ModernButton ok = UiBuild.Button(new ModernButton(), "套用", "check", ButtonKind.Primary);
            ok.Click += delegate
            {
                EnabledForCover = chkEnable.Checked;
                ApiUrl = txtUrl.Text.Trim();
                InstallPath = txtPath.Text.Trim();
                ModelTitle = Convert.ToString(comboModel.SelectedItem);
                if (ModelTitle.StartsWith("(")) ModelTitle = "";
                Steps = (int)numSteps.Value;
                CoverSize = int.Parse(Convert.ToString(comboSize.SelectedItem));
                DialogResult = DialogResult.OK;
                Close();
            };
            ModernButton cancel = UiBuild.Button(new ModernButton(), "取消", "", ButtonKind.Secondary);
            cancel.Click += delegate { DialogResult = DialogResult.Cancel; Close(); };
            UiBuild.AddFooterButton(parts.Footer, ok);
            UiBuild.AddFooterButton(parts.Footer, cancel);
            CancelButton = cancel;

            ThemeManager.ApplyDialog(this);
        }

        private void OnRefresh(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            btnRefresh.Enabled = false;
            lblStatus.Text = "啟動／讀取 SD WebUI 中...";
            string url = txtUrl.Text.Trim();
            string path = txtPath.Text.Trim();
            Thread worker = new Thread(delegate()
            {
                try
                {
                    if (!SdCoverClient.EnsureApi(url, path, delegate(string message)
                    {
                        PostStatus(message);
                    }))
                    {
                        PostStatus("SD API 無法啟動，請確認路徑與 WebUI 可正常執行。");
                        return;
                    }
                    List<string> models = SdCoverClient.GetModels(url);
                    try
                    {
                        BeginInvoke(new Action(delegate()
                        {
                            comboModel.Items.Clear();
                            foreach (string title in models) comboModel.Items.Add(title);
                            if (comboModel.Items.Count > 0) comboModel.SelectedIndex = 0;
                            lblStatus.Text = "已取得 " + models.Count + " 個模型。";
                            btnRefresh.Enabled = true;
                        }));
                    }
                    catch (InvalidOperationException) { }
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    PostStatus("讀取模型失敗: " + ex.Message);
                }
            });
            worker.IsBackground = true;
            worker.Start();
        }

        private void PostStatus(string message)
        {
            try
            {
                BeginInvoke(new Action(delegate()
                {
                    lblStatus.Text = message;
                    btnRefresh.Enabled = true;
                }));
            }
            catch (InvalidOperationException) { }
        }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace AIMusicGenerator
{
    internal sealed class StyleTemplate
    {
        public string Name;
        public string Caption;
        public int Bpm;
        public string Key;
        public string Signature;
        public bool Instrumental;
        public string Lyrics;
        public string Language;

        public StyleTemplate(string name, string caption, int bpm, string key,
            string signature, bool instrumental)
            : this(name, caption, bpm, key, signature, instrumental, null, null)
        {
        }

        public StyleTemplate(string name, string caption, int bpm, string key,
            string signature, bool instrumental, string lyrics, string language)
        {
            Name = name;
            Caption = caption;
            Bpm = bpm;
            Key = key;
            Signature = signature;
            Instrumental = instrumental;
            Lyrics = lyrics;
            Language = language;
        }

        public override string ToString()
        {
            return Name;
        }
    }

===== [29/66] AI_Music/app/src/ThemeManager.cs  (14142 bytes) =====
using System;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace AIMusicGenerator
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Colour tokens and recursive styling.  Palettes are the official
    /// Catppuccin Mocha (dark) and Latte (light) colours (MIT licence).
    /// </summary>
    internal static class ThemeManager
    {
        public static bool Dark = true;

        public static Color FormBack;      // window background
        public static Color CardBack;      // card surface
        public static Color PanelBack;     // secondary buttons / chips
        public static Color InputBack;     // text boxes, lists
        public static Color LogBack;       // log console
        public static Color Text;
        public static Color SubText;
        public static Color Muted;
        public static Color Border;
        public static Color BorderStrong;
        public static Color Accent;
        public static Color AccentHover;
        public static Color AccentDown;
        public static Color Accent2;       // gradient partner of Accent
        public static Color OnAccent;      // text drawn on Accent
        public static Color Success;
        public static Color Danger;
        public static Color Warning;
        public static Color Hover;         // ghost button hover
        public static Color Selection;

        // Fonts are pixel-sized through Dpi.Font so they scale together with the layout.
        public static readonly Font UiFont = Dpi.Font(10F, FontStyle.Regular);
        public static readonly Font SmallFont = Dpi.Font(9F, FontStyle.Regular);
        public static readonly Font HeadingFont = Dpi.Font(10.5F, FontStyle.Bold);
        public static readonly Font TitleFont = Dpi.Font(15F, FontStyle.Bold);
        public static readonly Font ButtonFont = Dpi.Font(10F, FontStyle.Bold);
        public static readonly Font BigButtonFont = Dpi.Font(12F, FontStyle.Bold);
        public static readonly Font SectionFont = Dpi.Font(8.5F, FontStyle.Bold);
        public static readonly Font EditorFont = Dpi.Font(11F, FontStyle.Regular);
        public static readonly Font MonoFont = CreateMonoFont();

        static ThemeManager() { SetTheme(true); }

        private static Font CreateMonoFont()
        {
            foreach (string name in new string[] { "Cascadia Mono", "Consolas" })
            {
                using (Font probe = new Font(name, 9.5F))
                {
                    if (string.Equals(probe.Name, name, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        return Dpi.Font(name, 9.5F, FontStyle.Regular);
                }
            }
            return Dpi.Font(FontFamily.GenericMonospace.Name, 9.5F, FontStyle.Regular);
        }

        private static Color Hex(int rgb)
        {
            return Color.FromArgb((rgb >> 16) & 0xFF, (rgb >> 8) & 0xFF, rgb & 0xFF);
        }

        public static void SetTheme(bool dark)
        {
            Dark = dark;
            if (dark)
            {
                // Catppuccin Mocha
                FormBack = Hex(0x11111B);      // crust
                CardBack = Hex(0x1E1E2E);      // base
                PanelBack = Hex(0x313244);     // surface0
                InputBack = Hex(0x181825);     // mantle
                LogBack = Hex(0x11111B);       // crust
                Text = Hex(0xCDD6F4);
                SubText = Hex(0xA6ADC8);       // subtext0
                Muted = Hex(0x7F849C);         // overlay1
                Border = Hex(0x313244);        // surface0
                BorderStrong = Hex(0x45475A);  // surface1
                Accent = Hex(0xCBA6F7);        // mauve
                AccentHover = Hex(0xD9BFFA);
                AccentDown = Hex(0xB48EE8);
                Accent2 = Hex(0x89B4FA);       // blue
                OnAccent = Hex(0x11111B);
                Success = Hex(0xA6E3A1);       // green
                Danger = Hex(0xF38BA8);        // red
                Warning = Hex(0xFAB387);       // peach
                Hover = Hex(0x313244);
                Selection = Color.FromArgb(70, 0xCB, 0xA6, 0xF7);
            }
            else
            {
                // Catppuccin Latte
                FormBack = Hex(0xDCE0E8);      // crust
                CardBack = Hex(0xEFF1F5);      // base
                PanelBack = Hex(0xE6E9EF);     // mantle
                InputBack = Hex(0xFFFFFF);
                LogBack = Hex(0xE6E9EF);
                Text = Hex(0x4C4F69);
                SubText = Hex(0x5C5F77);       // subtext1
                Muted = Hex(0x8C8FA1);         // overlay1
                Border = Hex(0xCCD0DA);        // surface0
                BorderStrong = Hex(0xBCC0CC);  // surface1
                Accent = Hex(0x8839EF);        // mauve
                AccentHover = Hex(0x9A55F2);
                AccentDown = Hex(0x7428DA);
                Accent2 = Hex(0x1E66F5);       // blue
                OnAccent = Color.White;
                Success = Hex(0x40A02B);
                Danger = Hex(0xD20F39);
                Warning = Hex(0xFE640B);
                Hover = Hex(0xDCE0E8);
                Selection = Color.FromArgb(48, 0x88, 0x39, 0xEF);
            }
        }

        /// <summary>Text colour to use on top of a filled colour.</summary>
        public static Color OnFill(Color fill)
        {
            double luminance = (0.299 * fill.R + 0.587 * fill.G + 0.114 * fill.B) / 255.0;
            return luminance > 0.6 ? Hex(0x11111B) : Color.White;
        }

        public static Color Blend(Color a, Color b, double amount)
        {
            amount = Math.Max(0.0, Math.Min(1.0, amount));
            return Color.FromArgb(
                (int)Math.Round(a.R + (b.R - a.R) * amount),
                (int)Math.Round(a.G + (b.G - a.G) * amount),
                (int)Math.Round(a.B + (b.B - a.B) * amount));
        }

        public static void Apply(Form form)
        {
            form.BackColor = FormBack;
            form.ForeColor = Text;
            foreach (Control control in form.Controls) ApplyControl(control, FormBack);
            form.Invalidate(true);
        }

        public static void ApplyDialog(Form form)
        {
            Apply(form);
            form.Shown -= OnDialogShown;
            form.Shown += OnDialogShown;
        }

        private static void OnDialogShown(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            ApplyNativeScrollBars((Control)sender);
        }

        /// <summary>Dark/light native scroll bars on every scrollable child that already has a handle.</summary>
        public static void ApplyNativeScrollBars(Control root)
        {
            if (root is TextBoxBase || root is ListBox || root is ComboBox ||
                (root is ScrollableControl && ((ScrollableControl)root).AutoScroll))
                NativeTheme.ApplyScrollBars(root);
            foreach (Control child in root.Controls) ApplyNativeScrollBars(child);
        }

        /// <summary>Recursively styles a control placed on a surface of colour <paramref name="surface"/>.</summary>
        public static void ApplyControl(Control control, Color surface)
        {
            if (control is CardPanel)
            {
                control.BackColor = CardBack;
                control.ForeColor = Text;
                foreach (Control child in control.Controls) ApplyControl(child, CardBack);
                control.Invalidate();
                return;
            }
            if (control is InputHost)
            {
                Color back = control.Tag as string == "log" ? LogBack : InputBack;
                control.BackColor = back;
                foreach (Control child in control.Controls) ApplyControl(child, back);
                control.Invalidate();
                return;
            }
            if (control is ModernButton || control is ToggleSwitch || control is SlimProgressBar
                || control is SeekSlider || control is WaveformPanel)
            {
                control.BackColor = surface;
                control.ForeColor = Text;
                control.Invalidate();
                return;
            }
            if (control is TableLayoutPanel || control is FlowLayoutPanel || control is SplitContainer
                || control is SplitterPanel || control is Panel)
            {
                control.BackColor = surface;
                control.ForeColor = Text;
                foreach (Control child in control.Controls) ApplyControl(child, surface);
                return;
            }
            if (control is Label)
            {
                control.BackColor = Color.Transparent;
                string role = control.Tag as string;
                if (role == "title") control.ForeColor = Text;
                else if (role == "muted") control.ForeColor = Muted;
                else if (role == "accent") control.ForeColor = Accent;
                else control.ForeColor = SubText;
                return;
            }
            if (control is TextBoxBase)
            {
                control.BackColor = control.Tag as string == "log" ? LogBack : InputBack;
                control.ForeColor = Text;
                return;
            }
            if (control is ListBox)
            {
                control.BackColor = InputBack;
                control.ForeColor = Text;
                control.Invalidate();
                return;
            }
            if (control is ThemedComboBox)
            {
                control.BackColor = InputBack;
                control.ForeColor = Text;
                control.Invalidate();
                return;
            }
            if (control is ComboBox)
            {
                ComboBox combo = (ComboBox)control;
                combo.BackColor = InputBack;
                combo.ForeColor = Text;
                combo.FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat;
                if (combo.DrawMode == DrawMode.Normal)
                {
                    combo.DrawMode = DrawMode.OwnerDrawFixed;
                    combo.ItemHeight = Math.Max(combo.ItemHeight, combo.Font.Height + 8);
                    combo.DrawItem += DrawComboItem;
                }
                combo.Invalidate();
                return;
            }
            if (control is NumericUpDown)
            {
                control.BackColor = InputBack;
                control.ForeColor = Text;
                return;
            }
            if (control is CheckBox || control is RadioButton)
            {
                control.BackColor = Color.Transparent;
                control.ForeColor = Text;
                return;
            }
            if (control is TrackBar)
            {
                control.BackColor = surface;
                return;
            }
            if (control is PictureBox)
            {
                control.BackColor = InputBack;
                return;
            }
            if (control is Button)
            {
                StyleButton((Button)control);
                return;
            }
            foreach (Control child in control.Controls) ApplyControl(child, surface);
        }

        private static void DrawComboItem(object sender, DrawItemEventArgs e)
        {
            ComboBox combo = (ComboBox)sender;
            if (e.Index < 0) return;
            bool selected = (e.State & DrawItemState.Selected) != 0 &&
                            (e.State & DrawItemState.ComboBoxEdit) == 0;
            Color back = selected ? Blend(InputBack, Accent, Dark ? 0.35 : 0.18) : InputBack;
            using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(back)) e.Graphics.FillRectangle(brush, e.Bounds);
            string text = combo.GetItemText(combo.Items[e.Index]);
            Color fore = combo.Enabled ? Text : Muted;
            Rectangle bounds = new Rectangle(e.Bounds.X + 6, e.Bounds.Y, e.Bounds.Width - 6, e.Bounds.Height);
            TextRenderer.DrawText(e.Graphics, text, combo.Font, bounds, fore, back,
                TextFormatFlags.VerticalCenter | TextFormatFlags.Left | TextFormatFlags.EndEllipsis |
                TextFormatFlags.NoPrefix);
        }

        public static void StyleButton(Button button)
        {
            ModernButton modern = button as ModernButton;
            if (modern != null)
            {
                modern.Invalidate();
                return;
            }
            button.FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat;
            button.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 1;
            button.FlatAppearance.BorderColor = BorderStrong;
            button.BackColor = PanelBack;
            button.ForeColor = Text;
            button.Cursor = Cursors.Hand;
            button.FlatAppearance.MouseOverBackColor = Hover;
            button.FlatAppearance.MouseDownBackColor = BorderStrong;
        }

        public static void StylePrimary(Button button)
        {
            SetKind(button, ButtonKind.Primary);
        }

        public static void StyleSuccess(Button button)
        {
            SetKind(button, ButtonKind.Success);
        }

        public static void StyleDanger(Button button)
        {
            SetKind(button, ButtonKind.Danger);
        }

        private static void SetKind(Button button, ButtonKind kind)
        {
            ModernButton modern = button as ModernButton;
            if (modern != null)
            {
                modern.Kind = kind;
                modern.Invalidate();
                return;
            }
            Color fill = kind == ButtonKind.Danger ? Danger : (kind == ButtonKind.Success ? Success : Accent);
            button.FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat;
            button.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            button.BackColor = fill;
            button.ForeColor = OnFill(fill);
            button.Cursor = Cursors.Hand;
        }
    }
}


===== [30/66] AI_Music/app/src/UiBuild.cs  (18434 bytes) =====
using System;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace AIMusicGenerator
{
    /// <summary>Shared layout builders for the main window and the dialogs (all sizes DPI-scaled).</summary>
    internal static class UiBuild
    {
        public static int S(int value) { return Dpi.S(value); }

        public static int FieldHeight { get { return S(36); } }

        public static CardPanel NewCard(string title, string icon, string subtitle)
        {
            CardPanel card = new CardPanel();
            card.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            card.Title = title;
            card.IconName = icon;
            card.Subtitle = subtitle;
            return card;
        }

        public static InputHost Host(Control inner, Padding padding)
        {
            InputHost host = new InputHost(inner, padding);
            host.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            host.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            return host;
        }

        /// <summary>Single-line text box in a rounded frame, vertically centred.</summary>
        public static InputHost TextField(TextBox box)
        {
            box.Font = ThemeManager.UiFont;
            InputHost host = new InputHost(box, TextPadding(box));
            host.Height = FieldHeight;
            host.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            host.Margin = new Padding(0, S(3), 0, S(3));
            return host;
        }

        /// <summary>Padding that vertically centres a single-line text box in a field-height frame.</summary>
        public static Padding TextPadding(Control box)
        {
            int top = Math.Max(S(4), (FieldHeight - box.Font.Height) / 2);
            return new Padding(S(10), top, S(8), 0);
        }

        /// <summary>Multi-line text area in a rounded frame.</summary>
        public static InputHost TextArea(TextBoxBase box, Font font)
        {
            box.Font = font;
            box.Multiline = true;
            InputHost host = new InputHost(box, Dpi.S(12, 10, 6, 8));
            host.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            host.Margin = new Padding(0, S(3), 0, S(3));
            return host;
        }

        /// <summary>Number field with themed −/+ buttons (the native spin buttons are clipped away).</summary>
        public static Control Stepper(NumericUpDown number, int width)
        {
            number.Font = ThemeManager.UiFont;
            number.TextAlign = HorizontalAlignment.Center;
            number.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None;
            int height = FieldHeight;

            // The native spin buttons cannot be themed, so the NumericUpDown is made wider than
            // its clip panel and the buttons fall outside the visible area.
            Panel clip = new Panel();
            clip.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            clip.Controls.Add(number);
            int spin = SystemInformation.VerticalScrollBarWidth + S(4);
            EventHandler fit = delegate
            {
                number.Location = new Point(0, Math.Max(0, (clip.Height - number.Height) / 2));
                number.Width = clip.Width + spin;
            };
            clip.Resize += fit;

            InputHost host = new InputHost(clip, new Padding(S(2), S(2), S(2), S(2)));
            host.Size = new Size(width > 0 ? width : S(120), height);
            host.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            if (width <= 0) host.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            ModernButton minus = StepButton("minus", "減少", delegate { number.DownButton(); });
            ModernButton plus = StepButton("plus", "增加", delegate { number.UpButton(); });
            minus.Dock = DockStyle.Left;
            plus.Dock = DockStyle.Right;
            host.Controls.Add(minus);
            host.Controls.Add(plus);
            clip.BringToFront();
            number.GotFocus += delegate { host.Invalidate(); };
            number.LostFocus += delegate { host.Invalidate(); };
            number.EnabledChanged += delegate
            {
                minus.Enabled = number.Enabled;
                plus.Enabled = number.Enabled;
                host.Invalidate();
            };
            fit(null, EventArgs.Empty);
            return host;
        }

        private static ModernButton StepButton(string icon, string text, EventHandler click)
        {
            ModernButton button = new ModernButton();
            button.Kind = ButtonKind.Ghost;
            button.Text = text;
            button.IconName = icon;
            button.IconOnly = true;
            button.IconSize = S(14);
            button.Width = S(24);
            button.Radius = S(6);
            button.TabStop = false;
            button.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            button.Click += click;
            return button;
        }

        public static Control Combo(ComboBox combo)
        {
            combo.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            combo.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            Panel wrap = new Panel();
            wrap.Height = FieldHeight;
            wrap.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            wrap.Margin = new Padding(0, S(3), 0, S(3));
            wrap.Padding = new Padding(0, Math.Max(0, (FieldHeight - combo.Height) / 2), 0, 0);
            wrap.Controls.Add(combo);
            return wrap;
        }

        public static Control ComboFixed(ComboBox combo, int width)
        {
            combo.Width = width;
            combo.Margin = new Padding(0, Math.Max(0, (FieldHeight - combo.Height) / 2), 0, 0);
            return combo;
        }

        public static Control Row(params Control[] items)
        {
            FlowLayoutPanel row = new FlowLayoutPanel();
            row.WrapContents = false;
            row.AutoSize = true;
            row.Margin = new Padding(0, S(3), 0, S(3));
            row.Padding = Padding.Empty;
            foreach (Control item in items)
            {
                if (!(item is Label) && item.Margin == Padding.Empty) item.Margin = new Padding(0, 0, S(8), 0);
                if (item is ToggleSwitch) item.Margin = new Padding(S(4), (FieldHeight - item.Height) / 2, 0, 0);
                row.Controls.Add(item);
            }
            return row;
        }

        /// <summary>A row where the first control stretches and the second keeps its size.</summary>
        public static Control Fill(Control stretch, Control fixedRight)
        {
            TableLayoutPanel row = new TableLayoutPanel();
            row.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            row.Height = S(40);
            row.Margin = new Padding(0, S(2), 0, S(2));
            row.ColumnCount = 2;
            row.RowCount = 1;
            row.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100F));
            row.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Absolute, fixedRight.Width + S(6)));
            row.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100F));
            stretch.Margin = new Padding(0, S(2), S(6), S(2));
            stretch.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            fixedRight.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            fixedRight.Anchor = AnchorStyles.Right;
            row.Controls.Add(stretch, 0, 0);
            row.Controls.Add(fixedRight, 1, 0);
            return row;
        }

        public static Label Unit(string text)
        {
            Label label = new Label();
            label.Text = text;
            label.AutoSize = true;
            label.Tag = "muted";
            label.Font = ThemeManager.SmallFont;
            label.Margin = new Padding(0, (FieldHeight - ThemeManager.SmallFont.Height) / 2, S(10), 0);
            return label;
        }

        public static Label Caption(string text)
        {
            Label label = new Label();
            label.Text = text;
            label.AutoSize = true;
            label.Font = ThemeManager.UiFont;
            label.Margin = new Padding(0, S(3) + (FieldHeight - ThemeManager.UiFont.Height) / 2, S(10), 0);
            return label;
        }

        public static Label Note(string text)
        {
            Label label = new Label();
            label.Text = text;
            label.AutoSize = true;
            label.Tag = "muted";
            label.Font = ThemeManager.SmallFont;
            label.Margin = new Padding(0, S(2), 0, S(2));
            return label;
        }

        public static Label SectionLabel(string text, bool gapAbove)
        {
            Label label = new Label();
            label.Text = text;
            label.Font = ThemeManager.SectionFont;
            label.Tag = "accent";
            label.AutoSize = true;
            label.Margin = new Padding(0, gapAbove ? S(14) : S(2), 0, S(2));
            return label;
        }

        public static void AddSection(TableLayoutPanel grid, string text, bool gapAbove)
        {
            Label label = SectionLabel(text, gapAbove);
            int row = grid.RowCount++;
            grid.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
            grid.Controls.Add(label, 0, row);
            grid.SetColumnSpan(label, Math.Max(1, grid.ColumnCount));
        }

        public static void AddWide(TableLayoutPanel grid, string caption, Control control)
        {
            int row = grid.RowCount++;
            grid.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
            grid.Controls.Add(Caption(caption), 0, row);
            if (control.Dock != DockStyle.Fill) control.Anchor = AnchorStyles.Left | AnchorStyles.Top;
            grid.Controls.Add(control, 1, row);
            if (grid.ColumnCount > 2) grid.SetColumnSpan(control, grid.ColumnCount - 1);
        }

        public static void AddPair(TableLayoutPanel grid, string caption1, Control control1, string caption2, Control control2)
        {
            int row = grid.RowCount++;
            grid.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
            grid.Controls.Add(Caption(caption1), 0, row);
            control1.Margin = new Padding(0, S(3), S(12), S(3));
            grid.Controls.Add(control1, 1, row);
            grid.Controls.Add(Caption(caption2), 2, row);
            control2.Margin = new Padding(0, S(3), 0, S(3));
            grid.Controls.Add(control2, 3, row);
        }

        /// <summary>Adds a full-width control in a new row (row style given by caller).</summary>
        public static void AddFull(TableLayoutPanel grid, Control control, RowStyle style)
        {
            int row = grid.RowCount++;
            grid.RowStyles.Add(style);
            grid.Controls.Add(control, 0, row);
            grid.SetColumnSpan(control, Math.Max(1, grid.ColumnCount));
        }

        public static void AddHeaderTools(CardPanel card, params Control[] tools)
        {
            FlowLayoutPanel bar = new FlowLayoutPanel();
            bar.AutoSize = true;
            bar.WrapContents = false;
            bar.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            bar.Padding = Padding.Empty;
            int rowHeight = S(32);
            foreach (Control tool in tools)
            {
                int top = Math.Max(0, (rowHeight - tool.Height) / 2);
                tool.Margin = new Padding(S(8), top, 0, 0);
                bar.Controls.Add(tool);
            }
            Size preferred = bar.GetPreferredSize(Size.Empty);
            bar.AutoSize = false;
            bar.Size = new Size(preferred.Width, rowHeight);
            card.Controls.Add(bar);
            bar.BringToFront();
            EventHandler place = delegate
            {
                bar.Location = new Point(Math.Max(0, card.Width - bar.Width - S(12)), S(5));
                card.HeaderReserve = bar.Width + S(12);
                card.Invalidate();
            };
            card.Resize += place;
            bar.SizeChanged += place;
            place(null, EventArgs.Empty);
        }

        public static ModernButton Button(ModernButton button, string text, string icon, ButtonKind kind)
        {
            button.Text = text;
            button.IconName = icon;
            button.Kind = kind;
            button.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            button.Size = button.GetPreferredSize(Size.Empty);
            return button;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------------ dialogs

        internal sealed class DialogParts
        {
            public TableLayoutPanel Body;
            public FlowLayoutPanel Footer;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Standard dialog frame: header (icon tile, title, subtitle), a body grid and a
        /// right-aligned footer.  Buttons added to Footer appear right-to-left.
        /// </summary>
        public static DialogParts Dialog(Form form, string title, string subtitle, string icon,
            int width, int height, bool resizable)
        {
            form.AutoScaleMode = AutoScaleMode.None;
            form.Font = ThemeManager.UiFont;
            form.Text = title;
            form.ClientSize = Dpi.S(width, height);
            form.StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterParent;
            form.FormBorderStyle = resizable ? FormBorderStyle.Sizable : FormBorderStyle.FixedDialog;
            form.MaximizeBox = resizable;
            form.MinimizeBox = false;
            form.ShowInTaskbar = false;
            if (resizable) form.MinimumSize = form.Size;

            TableLayoutPanel root = new TableLayoutPanel();
            root.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            root.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            root.Padding = Dpi.S(20, 16, 20, 16);
            root.ColumnCount = 1;
            root.RowCount = 3;
            root.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100F));
            root.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
            root.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100F));
            root.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));

            TableLayoutPanel header = new TableLayoutPanel();
            header.AutoSize = true;
            header.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            header.Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, S(12));
            header.ColumnCount = 2;
            header.RowCount = 2;
            header.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Absolute, S(52)));
            header.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100F));
            IconTile tile = new IconTile(icon);
            tile.Margin = new Padding(0, S(2), 0, 0);
            header.Controls.Add(tile, 0, 0);
            header.SetRowSpan(tile, 2);
            Label titleLabel = new Label();
            titleLabel.Text = title;
            titleLabel.Font = Dpi.Font(13F, FontStyle.Bold);
            titleLabel.Tag = "title";
            titleLabel.AutoSize = true;
            titleLabel.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            header.Controls.Add(titleLabel, 1, 0);
            Label sub = Note(subtitle);
            sub.Margin = new Padding(S(1), S(2), 0, 0);
            header.Controls.Add(sub, 1, 1);
            root.Controls.Add(header, 0, 0);

            TableLayoutPanel body = new TableLayoutPanel();
            body.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            body.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            body.ColumnCount = 1;
            body.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100F));
            root.Controls.Add(body, 0, 1);

            FlowLayoutPanel footer = new FlowLayoutPanel();
            footer.FlowDirection = FlowDirection.RightToLeft;
            footer.WrapContents = false;
            footer.AutoSize = true;
            footer.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            footer.Margin = new Padding(0, S(14), 0, 0);
            root.Controls.Add(footer, 0, 2);

            form.Controls.Add(root);
            form.HandleCreated += delegate { DarkTitleBar(form); };

            DialogParts parts = new DialogParts();
            parts.Body = body;
            parts.Footer = footer;
            return parts;
        }

        public static void AddFooterButton(FlowLayoutPanel footer, ModernButton button)
        {
            button.Height = S(38);
            button.Size = new Size(Math.Max(S(104), button.GetPreferredSize(Size.Empty).Width), S(38));
            button.Margin = new Padding(S(10), 0, 0, 0);
            footer.Controls.Add(button);
        }

        [DllImport("dwmapi.dll")]
        private static extern int DwmSetWindowAttribute(IntPtr hwnd, int attr, ref int value, int size);

        public static void DarkTitleBar(Form form)
        {
            if (!form.IsHandleCreated) return;
            try
            {
                int enabled = ThemeManager.Dark ? 1 : 0;
                DwmSetWindowAttribute(form.Handle, 20, ref enabled, sizeof(int));
                DwmSetWindowAttribute(form.Handle, 19, ref enabled, sizeof(int));
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
            }
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Small rounded tile with a Lucide icon (dialog headers).</summary>
    internal class IconTile : Control
    {
        private readonly string icon;

        public IconTile(string icon)
        {
            this.icon = icon;
            SetStyle(ControlStyles.UserPaint | ControlStyles.AllPaintingInWmPaint |
                     ControlStyles.OptimizedDoubleBuffer | ControlStyles.ResizeRedraw, true);
            Size = Dpi.S(40, 40);
        }

        protected override void OnPaint(PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Graphics g = e.Graphics;
            Color surface = Shapes.SurfaceOf(this, ThemeManager.FormBack);
            g.Clear(surface);
            g.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
            RectangleF rect = new RectangleF(0, 0, Width - 1, Height - 1);
            using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath path = Shapes.RoundedRect(rect, Dpi.S(10)))
            using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ThemeManager.Blend(surface, ThemeManager.Accent, ThemeManager.Dark ? 0.20 : 0.14)))
                g.FillPath(brush, path);
            float size = Dpi.S(20F);
            IconRenderer.Draw(g, icon, new RectangleF((Width - size) / 2F, (Height - size) / 2F, size, size), ThemeManager.Accent);
        }
    }
}
