using System;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Drawing.Drawing2D;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace AIMusicGenerator
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Explicit DPI scaling.  The main window uses AutoScaleMode.None and every size goes
    /// through S(), so layouts look the same at 100 %, 125 %, 150 % ...
    /// AIMUSIC_UI_SCALE (e.g. "1" or "1.5") overrides the factor for screenshot tests.
    /// </summary>
    internal static class Dpi
    {
        public static readonly float Factor = Detect();

        private static float Detect()
        {
            string forced = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("AIMUSIC_UI_SCALE");
            float value;
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(forced) &&
                float.TryParse(forced, NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out value) &&
                value >= 0.75F && value <= 4F)
                return value;
            try
            {
                using (Graphics g = Graphics.FromHwnd(IntPtr.Zero)) return Math.Max(1F, g.DpiX / 96F);
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
                return 1F;
            }
        }

        public static int S(int value) { return (int)Math.Round(value * Factor); }
        public static float S(float value) { return value * Factor; }
        public static Size S(int width, int height) { return new Size(S(width), S(height)); }
        public static Padding S(int left, int top, int right, int bottom)
        {
            return new Padding(S(left), S(top), S(right), S(bottom));
        }

        /// <summary>Font sized in points at 96 DPI, scaled by Factor (pixel units, so it ignores system DPI).</summary>
        public static Font Font(float points, FontStyle style)
        {
            return Font("Microsoft JhengHei UI", points, style);
        }

        public static Font Font(string family, float points, FontStyle style)
        {
            return new Font(family, points * 96F / 72F * Factor, style, GraphicsUnit.Pixel);
        }
    }

    internal static class NativeTheme
    {
        [DllImport("uxtheme.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
        private static extern int SetWindowTheme(IntPtr hwnd, string appName, string idList);

        /// <summary>Dark or light native scroll bars for a scrollable control.</summary>
        public static void ApplyScrollBars(Control control)
        {
            if (control == null || !control.IsHandleCreated) return;
            try { SetWindowTheme(control.Handle, ThemeManager.Dark ? "DarkMode_Explorer" : "Explorer", null); }
            catch (Exception) { }
        }
    }

    internal enum ButtonKind
    {
        Secondary,
        Primary,
        Ghost,
        Success,
        Danger,
        DangerOutline
    }

    internal static class Shapes
    {
        public static GraphicsPath RoundedRect(RectangleF rect, float radius)
        {
            GraphicsPath path = new GraphicsPath();
            float r = Math.Max(0F, Math.Min(radius, Math.Min(rect.Width, rect.Height) / 2F));
            if (r < 0.5F)
            {
                path.AddRectangle(rect);
                return path;
            }
            float d = r * 2F;
            path.AddArc(rect.X, rect.Y, d, d, 180, 90);
            path.AddArc(rect.Right - d, rect.Y, d, d, 270, 90);
            path.AddArc(rect.Right - d, rect.Bottom - d, d, d, 0, 90);
            path.AddArc(rect.X, rect.Bottom - d, d, d, 90, 90);
            path.CloseFigure();
            return path;
        }

        public static Color SurfaceOf(Control control, Color fallback)
        {
            Control parent = control.Parent;
            while (parent != null && parent.BackColor.A < 255) parent = parent.Parent;
            return parent != null ? parent.BackColor : fallback;
        }

        public static void DrawChevronDown(Graphics g, RectangleF box, Color color)
        {
            float w = Math.Min(box.Width, box.Height) * 0.36F;
            float cx = box.X + box.Width / 2F, cy = box.Y + box.Height / 2F;
            using (Pen pen = new Pen(color, Dpi.S(1.6F)))
            {
                pen.StartCap = LineCap.Round;
                pen.EndCap = LineCap.Round;
                pen.LineJoin = LineJoin.Round;
                g.DrawLines(pen, new PointF[]
                {
                    new PointF(cx - w, cy - w / 2F), new PointF(cx, cy + w / 2F), new PointF(cx + w, cy - w / 2F)
                });
            }
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Rounded, theme-aware button with an optional Lucide icon.</summary>
    internal class ModernButton : Button
    {
        private ButtonKind kind = ButtonKind.Secondary;
        private string iconName = "";
        private bool hover;
        private bool pressed;

        public ModernButton()
        {
            SetStyle(ControlStyles.UserPaint | ControlStyles.AllPaintingInWmPaint |
                     ControlStyles.OptimizedDoubleBuffer | ControlStyles.ResizeRedraw, true);
            FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat;
            FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            Cursor = Cursors.Hand;
            Font = ThemeManager.ButtonFont;
            Radius = Dpi.S(8);
            IconSize = Dpi.S(18);
            Height = Dpi.S(36);
        }

        [DefaultValue(ButtonKind.Secondary)]
        public ButtonKind Kind
        {
            get { return kind; }
            set { kind = value; Invalidate(); }
        }

        public string IconName
        {
            get { return iconName; }
            set { iconName = value ?? ""; Invalidate(); }
        }

        /// <summary>Draw only the icon; Text is still used for the tooltip / accessibility.</summary>
        public bool IconOnly { get; set; }
        public int Radius { get; set; }
        public int IconSize { get; set; }

        protected override void OnMouseEnter(EventArgs e) { hover = true; Invalidate(); base.OnMouseEnter(e); }
        protected override void OnMouseLeave(EventArgs e) { hover = false; pressed = false; Invalidate(); base.OnMouseLeave(e); }

        protected override void OnMouseDown(MouseEventArgs e)
        {
            if (e.Button == MouseButtons.Left) { pressed = true; Invalidate(); }
            base.OnMouseDown(e);
        }

        protected override void OnMouseUp(MouseEventArgs e)
        {
            pressed = false;
            Invalidate();
            base.OnMouseUp(e);
        }

        protected override void OnEnabledChanged(EventArgs e) { Invalidate(); base.OnEnabledChanged(e); }
        protected override void OnTextChanged(EventArgs e) { Invalidate(); base.OnTextChanged(e); }

        public override Size GetPreferredSize(Size proposedSize)
        {
            if (IconOnly) return new Size(Height, Height);
            int icon = IconRenderer.Has(iconName) ? IconSize + Dpi.S(7) : 0;
            Size text = TextRenderer.MeasureText(Text ?? "", Font, Size.Empty, TextFormatFlags.NoPadding);
            return new Size(Math.Max(text.Width + icon + Dpi.S(28), Dpi.S(56)), Height);
        }

        private void GetColors(Color surface, out Color fill, out Color border, out Color fore)
        {
            switch (kind)
            {
                case ButtonKind.Primary:
                    fill = pressed ? ThemeManager.AccentDown : (hover ? ThemeManager.AccentHover : ThemeManager.Accent);
                    border = fill;
                    fore = ThemeManager.OnAccent;
                    break;
                case ButtonKind.Success:
                case ButtonKind.Danger:
                    fill = kind == ButtonKind.Success ? ThemeManager.Success : ThemeManager.Danger;
                    if (hover) fill = ThemeManager.Blend(fill, Color.White, 0.15);
                    if (pressed) fill = ThemeManager.Blend(fill, Color.Black, 0.12);
                    border = fill;
                    fore = ThemeManager.OnFill(fill);
                    break;
                case ButtonKind.DangerOutline:
                    fill = hover ? ThemeManager.Blend(surface, ThemeManager.Danger, ThemeManager.Dark ? 0.16 : 0.10) : surface;
                    border = ThemeManager.Blend(surface, ThemeManager.Danger, 0.75);
                    fore = ThemeManager.Danger;
                    break;
                case ButtonKind.Ghost:
                    fill = pressed ? ThemeManager.BorderStrong : (hover ? ThemeManager.Hover : surface);
                    border = fill;
                    fore = hover ? ThemeManager.Text : ThemeManager.SubText;
                    break;
                default:
                    fill = pressed ? ThemeManager.BorderStrong : (hover ? ThemeManager.Hover : ThemeManager.PanelBack);
                    border = ThemeManager.Dark ? ThemeManager.BorderStrong : ThemeManager.Border;
                    fore = ThemeManager.Text;
                    break;
            }
            if (!Enabled)
            {
                bool flat = kind == ButtonKind.Ghost || kind == ButtonKind.DangerOutline;
                fill = flat ? surface : ThemeManager.Blend(surface, fill, 0.35);
                border = ThemeManager.Blend(surface, border, 0.45);
                fore = ThemeManager.Muted;
            }
        }

        protected override void OnPaint(PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Graphics g = e.Graphics;
            Color surface = Shapes.SurfaceOf(this, ThemeManager.CardBack);
            g.Clear(surface);
            Color fill, border, fore;
            GetColors(surface, out fill, out border, out fore);
            g.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
            RectangleF rect = new RectangleF(0.5F, 0.5F, Width - 1.5F, Height - 1.5F);
            using (GraphicsPath path = Shapes.RoundedRect(rect, Radius))
            {
                using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(fill)) g.FillPath(brush, path);
                if (border != fill)
                    using (Pen pen = new Pen(border)) g.DrawPath(pen, path);
            }
            if (Focused && ShowFocusCues)
            {
                float inset = Dpi.S(2.5F);
                using (GraphicsPath ring = Shapes.RoundedRect(new RectangleF(inset, inset, Width - inset * 2 - 1, Height - inset * 2 - 1),
                    Math.Max(1, Radius - inset)))
                using (Pen pen = new Pen(Color.FromArgb(170, kind == ButtonKind.Primary ? ThemeManager.OnAccent : ThemeManager.Accent), Dpi.S(1.5F)))
                    g.DrawPath(pen, ring);
            }

            bool hasIcon = IconRenderer.Has(iconName);
            int iconSize = Math.Min(IconSize, Height - Dpi.S(10));
            if (IconOnly || string.IsNullOrEmpty(Text))
            {
                if (hasIcon)
                    IconRenderer.Draw(g, iconName, new RectangleF((Width - iconSize) / 2F, (Height - iconSize) / 2F,
                        iconSize, iconSize), fore);
                return;
            }
            Size textSize = TextRenderer.MeasureText(g, Text, Font, Size.Empty, TextFormatFlags.NoPadding);
            int gap = hasIcon ? Dpi.S(7) : 0;
            int total = textSize.Width + (hasIcon ? iconSize + gap : 0);
            int x = Math.Max(Dpi.S(8), (Width - total) / 2);
            if (hasIcon)
            {
                IconRenderer.Draw(g, iconName, new RectangleF(x, (Height - iconSize) / 2F, iconSize, iconSize), fore);
                x += iconSize + gap;
            }
            Rectangle textRect = new Rectangle(x, 0, Math.Max(0, Width - x - Dpi.S(6)), Height);
            TextRenderer.DrawText(g, Text, Font, textRect, fore,
                TextFormatFlags.VerticalCenter | TextFormatFlags.Left | TextFormatFlags.NoPadding |
                TextFormatFlags.EndEllipsis | TextFormatFlags.NoPrefix);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Rounded card surface with an optional header (icon + title + subtitle).</summary>
    internal class CardPanel : Panel
    {
        private string title = "";
        private string subtitle = "";
        private string iconName = "";

        public CardPanel()
        {
            SetStyle(ControlStyles.UserPaint | ControlStyles.AllPaintingInWmPaint |
                     ControlStyles.OptimizedDoubleBuffer | ControlStyles.ResizeRedraw, true);
            Radius = Dpi.S(12);
            UpdatePadding();
        }

        public static int HeaderHeight { get { return Dpi.S(42); } }

        public int Radius { get; set; }

        /// <summary>Width kept free on the right of the header for tool controls.</summary>
        public int HeaderReserve { get; set; }

        public string Title
        {
            get { return title; }
            set { title = value ?? ""; UpdatePadding(); Invalidate(); }
        }

        public string Subtitle
        {
            get { return subtitle; }
            set { subtitle = value ?? ""; Invalidate(); }
        }

        public string IconName
        {
            get { return iconName; }
            set { iconName = value ?? ""; Invalidate(); }
        }

        private void UpdatePadding()
        {
            Padding = new Padding(Dpi.S(16), title.Length > 0 ? HeaderHeight + Dpi.S(4) : Dpi.S(14), Dpi.S(16), Dpi.S(14));
        }

        protected override void OnPaintBackground(PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            e.Graphics.Clear(Shapes.SurfaceOf(this, ThemeManager.FormBack));
        }

        protected override void OnPaint(PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Graphics g = e.Graphics;
            g.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
            RectangleF rect = new RectangleF(0.5F, 0.5F, Width - 1.5F, Height - 1.5F);
            using (GraphicsPath path = Shapes.RoundedRect(rect, Radius))
            {
                using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ThemeManager.CardBack)) g.FillPath(brush, path);
                using (Pen pen = new Pen(ThemeManager.Border)) g.DrawPath(pen, path);
            }
            if (title.Length == 0) return;
            int x = Dpi.S(16);
            int top = Dpi.S(10);
            int tileSize = Dpi.S(26);
            if (IconRenderer.Has(iconName))
            {
                RectangleF tile = new RectangleF(x, top, tileSize, tileSize);
                using (GraphicsPath tilePath = Shapes.RoundedRect(tile, Dpi.S(7)))
                using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ThemeManager.Blend(ThemeManager.CardBack, ThemeManager.Accent,
                    ThemeManager.Dark ? 0.18 : 0.12)))
                    g.FillPath(brush, tilePath);
                float inner = Dpi.S(16);
                IconRenderer.Draw(g, iconName, new RectangleF(x + (tileSize - inner) / 2F, top + (tileSize - inner) / 2F, inner, inner),
                    ThemeManager.Accent);
                x += tileSize + Dpi.S(10);
            }
            int available = Math.Max(0, Width - x - Dpi.S(16) - HeaderReserve);
            Rectangle titleRect = new Rectangle(x, top, available, tileSize);
            TextRenderer.DrawText(g, title, ThemeManager.HeadingFont, titleRect, ThemeManager.Text,
                TextFormatFlags.VerticalCenter | TextFormatFlags.NoPadding | TextFormatFlags.NoPrefix | TextFormatFlags.EndEllipsis);
            if (subtitle.Length > 0)
            {
                Size titleSize = TextRenderer.MeasureText(g, title, ThemeManager.HeadingFont, Size.Empty, TextFormatFlags.NoPadding);
                int sx = x + titleSize.Width + Dpi.S(10);
                int width = available - titleSize.Width - Dpi.S(10);
                if (width > Dpi.S(40))
                    TextRenderer.DrawText(g, subtitle, ThemeManager.SmallFont, new Rectangle(sx, top, width, tileSize),
                        ThemeManager.Muted, TextFormatFlags.VerticalCenter | TextFormatFlags.NoPadding |
                        TextFormatFlags.EndEllipsis | TextFormatFlags.NoPrefix);
            }
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Rounded frame around a borderless input; highlights while the input has focus.</summary>
    internal class InputHost : Panel
    {
        private readonly Control inner;

        public InputHost(Control inner) : this(inner, Dpi.S(10, 7, 8, 5)) { }

        public InputHost(Control inner, Padding padding)
        {
            this.inner = inner;
            SetStyle(ControlStyles.UserPaint | ControlStyles.AllPaintingInWmPaint |
                     ControlStyles.OptimizedDoubleBuffer | ControlStyles.ResizeRedraw, true);
            Padding = padding;
            TextBoxBase box = inner as TextBoxBase;
            if (box != null) box.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None;
            NumericUpDown number = inner as NumericUpDown;
            if (number != null) number.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None;
            ListBox list = inner as ListBox;
            if (list != null) list.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None;
            inner.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            inner.Margin = Padding.Empty;
            Controls.Add(inner);
            inner.GotFocus += delegate { Invalidate(); };
            inner.LostFocus += delegate { Invalidate(); };
            inner.EnabledChanged += delegate { Invalidate(); };
            Cursor = Cursors.IBeam;
        }

        public Control Inner { get { return inner; } }

        protected override void OnClick(EventArgs e)
        {
            base.OnClick(e);
            inner.Focus();
        }

        protected override void OnPaintBackground(PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            e.Graphics.Clear(Shapes.SurfaceOf(this, ThemeManager.CardBack));
        }

        protected override void OnPaint(PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Graphics g = e.Graphics;
            g.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
            bool focused = inner.ContainsFocus;
            RectangleF rect = new RectangleF(0.5F, 0.5F, Width - 1.5F, Height - 1.5F);
            using (GraphicsPath path = Shapes.RoundedRect(rect, Dpi.S(8)))
            {
                using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(BackColor)) g.FillPath(brush, path);
                Color border = focused ? ThemeManager.Accent : (ThemeManager.Dark ? ThemeManager.BorderStrong : ThemeManager.Border);
                if (!inner.Enabled) border = ThemeManager.Border;
                using (Pen pen = new Pen(border, focused ? Dpi.S(1.5F) : 1F)) g.DrawPath(pen, path);
            }
        }
    }

    /// <summary>DropDownList combo box with themed item list, border and chevron.</summary>
    internal class ThemedComboBox : ComboBox
    {
        private const int WM_PAINT = 0x000F;

        public ThemedComboBox()
        {
            DropDownStyle = ComboBoxStyle.DropDownList;
            FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat;
            DrawMode = DrawMode.OwnerDrawFixed;
            ItemHeight = Dpi.S(26);
            Font = ThemeManager.UiFont;
            DoubleBuffered = true;
        }

        protected override void OnDrawItem(DrawItemEventArgs e)
        {
            if (e.Index < 0) return;
            bool edit = (e.State & DrawItemState.ComboBoxEdit) != 0;
            bool selected = (e.State & DrawItemState.Selected) != 0 && !edit;
            Color back = selected
                ? ThemeManager.Blend(ThemeManager.InputBack, ThemeManager.Accent, ThemeManager.Dark ? 0.32 : 0.16)
                : ThemeManager.InputBack;
            using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(back)) e.Graphics.FillRectangle(brush, e.Bounds);
            Rectangle bounds = new Rectangle(e.Bounds.X + Dpi.S(edit ? 6 : 8), e.Bounds.Y, e.Bounds.Width - Dpi.S(10), e.Bounds.Height);
            TextRenderer.DrawText(e.Graphics, GetItemText(Items[e.Index]), Font, bounds,
                Enabled ? ThemeManager.Text : ThemeManager.Muted, back,
                TextFormatFlags.VerticalCenter | TextFormatFlags.Left | TextFormatFlags.EndEllipsis | TextFormatFlags.NoPrefix);
        }

        protected override void OnDropDownClosed(EventArgs e) { base.OnDropDownClosed(e); Invalidate(); }
        protected override void OnGotFocus(EventArgs e) { base.OnGotFocus(e); Invalidate(); }
        protected override void OnLostFocus(EventArgs e) { base.OnLostFocus(e); Invalidate(); }

        protected override void WndProc(ref Message m)
        {
            base.WndProc(ref m);
            if (m.Msg == WM_PAINT && IsHandleCreated) PaintChrome();
        }

        private void PaintChrome()
        {
            using (Graphics g = Graphics.FromHwnd(Handle))
            {
                Rectangle r = ClientRectangle;
                Color surface = Shapes.SurfaceOf(this, ThemeManager.CardBack);
                int button = SystemInformation.VerticalScrollBarWidth + Dpi.S(2);
                using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ThemeManager.InputBack))
                    g.FillRectangle(brush, r.Right - button - 1, r.Top + 1, button, r.Height - 2);
                g.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
                Shapes.DrawChevronDown(g, new RectangleF(r.Right - button - 2, r.Top, button, r.Height),
                    Enabled ? ThemeManager.SubText : ThemeManager.Muted);
                RectangleF frame = new RectangleF(0.5F, 0.5F, r.Width - 1.5F, r.Height - 1.5F);
                using (GraphicsPath path = Shapes.RoundedRect(frame, Dpi.S(7)))
                using (Region outside = new Region(new Rectangle(0, 0, r.Width, r.Height)))
                {
                    outside.Exclude(path);
                    using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(surface)) g.FillRegion(brush, outside);
                    bool active = Focused || DroppedDown;
                    Color border = active ? ThemeManager.Accent : (ThemeManager.Dark ? ThemeManager.BorderStrong : ThemeManager.Border);
                    using (Pen pen = new Pen(border, active ? Dpi.S(1.5F) : 1F)) g.DrawPath(pen, path);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /// <summary>CheckBox drawn as a toggle switch; keeps the normal CheckBox API.</summary>
    internal class ToggleSwitch : CheckBox
    {
        public ToggleSwitch()
        {
            SetStyle(ControlStyles.UserPaint | ControlStyles.AllPaintingInWmPaint |
                     ControlStyles.OptimizedDoubleBuffer | ControlStyles.ResizeRedraw, true);
            Cursor = Cursors.Hand;
            AutoSize = true;
        }

        private static int TrackWidth { get { return Dpi.S(36); } }
        private static int TrackHeight { get { return Dpi.S(20); } }

        public override Size GetPreferredSize(Size proposedSize)
        {
            Size text = TextRenderer.MeasureText(Text ?? "", Font, Size.Empty, TextFormatFlags.NoPadding);
            return new Size(TrackWidth + Dpi.S(10) + text.Width + Dpi.S(4), Math.Max(Dpi.S(28), text.Height + Dpi.S(6)));
        }

        protected override void OnCheckedChanged(EventArgs e) { Invalidate(); base.OnCheckedChanged(e); }
        protected override void OnEnabledChanged(EventArgs e) { Invalidate(); base.OnEnabledChanged(e); }

        protected override void OnPaint(PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Graphics g = e.Graphics;
            Color surface = Shapes.SurfaceOf(this, ThemeManager.CardBack);
            g.Clear(surface);
            g.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
            float top = (Height - TrackHeight) / 2F;
            RectangleF track = new RectangleF(1, top, TrackWidth, TrackHeight);
            Color trackColor = Checked ? ThemeManager.Accent : ThemeManager.BorderStrong;
            if (!Enabled) trackColor = ThemeManager.Blend(surface, trackColor, 0.45);
            using (GraphicsPath path = Shapes.RoundedRect(track, TrackHeight / 2F))
            using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(trackColor))
                g.FillPath(brush, path);
            float pad = Dpi.S(3F);
            float knob = TrackHeight - pad * 2;
            float knobX = Checked ? track.Right - knob - pad : track.X + pad;
            Color knobColor = Checked ? ThemeManager.OnAccent : Color.White;
            using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(knobColor))
                g.FillEllipse(brush, knobX, top + pad, knob, knob);
            if (Focused && ShowFocusCues)
                using (GraphicsPath ring = Shapes.RoundedRect(new RectangleF(0, top - 1, TrackWidth + 2, TrackHeight + 2), TrackHeight / 2F + 1))
                using (Pen pen = new Pen(Color.FromArgb(150, ThemeManager.Accent), Dpi.S(1.5F)))
                    g.DrawPath(pen, ring);
            int textX = TrackWidth + Dpi.S(10);
            Rectangle textRect = new Rectangle(textX, 0, Math.Max(0, Width - textX), Height);
            TextRenderer.DrawText(g, Text, Font, textRect, Enabled ? ThemeManager.Text : ThemeManager.Muted,
                TextFormatFlags.VerticalCenter | TextFormatFlags.Left | TextFormatFlags.NoPrefix |
                TextFormatFlags.NoPadding | TextFormatFlags.EndEllipsis);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Thin rounded progress bar; API mirrors ProgressBar (Minimum/Maximum/Value/Style).</summary>
    internal class SlimProgressBar : Control
    {
        private readonly Timer marquee = new Timer();
        private ProgressBarStyle style = ProgressBarStyle.Blocks;
        private int minimum;
        private int maximum = 100;
        private int value;
        private float phase;

        public SlimProgressBar()
        {
            SetStyle(ControlStyles.UserPaint | ControlStyles.AllPaintingInWmPaint |
                     ControlStyles.OptimizedDoubleBuffer | ControlStyles.ResizeRedraw, true);
            Height = Dpi.S(8);
            marquee.Interval = 30;
            marquee.Tick += delegate { phase += 0.012F; if (phase > 1.2F) phase = -0.35F; Invalidate(); };
        }

        public ProgressBarStyle Style
        {
            get { return style; }
            set
            {
                if (style == value) return;
                style = value;
                if (style == ProgressBarStyle.Marquee) { phase = -0.35F; marquee.Start(); }
                else marquee.Stop();
                Invalidate();
            }
        }

        public int Minimum { get { return minimum; } set { minimum = value; Invalidate(); } }
        public int Maximum { get { return maximum; } set { maximum = value; Invalidate(); } }

        public int Value
        {
            get { return value; }
            set { this.value = Math.Max(minimum, Math.Min(maximum, value)); Invalidate(); }
        }

        protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
        {
            if (disposing) marquee.Dispose();
            base.Dispose(disposing);
        }

        protected override void OnPaint(PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Graphics g = e.Graphics;
            g.Clear(Shapes.SurfaceOf(this, ThemeManager.CardBack));
            g.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
            RectangleF track = new RectangleF(0, 0, Width - 1, Height - 1);
            float radius = Height / 2F;
            using (GraphicsPath path = Shapes.RoundedRect(track, radius))
            using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ThemeManager.Dark ? ThemeManager.Border : ThemeManager.BorderStrong))
                g.FillPath(brush, path);

            RectangleF fill;
            if (style == ProgressBarStyle.Marquee)
            {
                float w = track.Width * 0.3F;
                fill = RectangleF.Intersect(new RectangleF(track.X + phase * track.Width, 0, w, track.Height), track);
            }
            else
            {
                float ratio = maximum > minimum ? (value - minimum) / (float)(maximum - minimum) : 0F;
                fill = new RectangleF(0, 0, track.Width * ratio, track.Height);
            }
            if (fill.Width < 1F) return;
            if (fill.Width < Height) fill.Width = Math.Min(track.Width, Height);
            using (GraphicsPath path = Shapes.RoundedRect(fill, radius))
            using (LinearGradientBrush brush = new LinearGradientBrush(
                new RectangleF(-1, 0, Math.Max(2, Width + 2), Height), ThemeManager.Accent, ThemeManager.Accent2, 0F))
                g.FillPath(brush, path);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Slim seek slider; API mirrors the parts of TrackBar the player uses.</summary>
    internal class SeekSlider : Control
    {
        private int minimum;
        private int maximum = 1000;
        private int value;
        private bool dragging;
        private bool hover;

        public event EventHandler Scroll;

        public SeekSlider()
        {
            SetStyle(ControlStyles.UserPaint | ControlStyles.AllPaintingInWmPaint |
                     ControlStyles.OptimizedDoubleBuffer | ControlStyles.ResizeRedraw |
                     ControlStyles.Selectable, true);
            Height = Dpi.S(18);
            Cursor = Cursors.Hand;
            TabStop = true;
        }

        public int Minimum { get { return minimum; } set { minimum = value; Invalidate(); } }
        public int Maximum { get { return maximum; } set { maximum = value; Invalidate(); } }
        public TickStyle TickStyle { get; set; }

        public int Value
        {
            get { return value; }
            set
            {
                int clamped = Math.Max(minimum, Math.Min(maximum, value));
                if (clamped == this.value) return;
                this.value = clamped;
                Invalidate();
            }
        }

        private void RaiseScroll()
        {
            EventHandler handler = Scroll;
            if (handler != null) handler(this, EventArgs.Empty);
        }

        private void SetFromX(int x)
        {
            float usable = Math.Max(1, Width - Height);
            float ratio = Math.Max(0F, Math.Min(1F, (x - Height / 2F) / usable));
            Value = minimum + (int)Math.Round(ratio * (maximum - minimum));
            RaiseScroll();
        }

        protected override void OnMouseDown(MouseEventArgs e)
        {
            base.OnMouseDown(e);
            if (e.Button != MouseButtons.Left) return;
            Focus();
            dragging = true;
            Capture = true;
            SetFromX(e.X);
        }

        protected override void OnMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
        {
            base.OnMouseMove(e);
            if (dragging) SetFromX(e.X);
        }

        protected override void OnMouseUp(MouseEventArgs e)
        {
            dragging = false;
            Capture = false;
            base.OnMouseUp(e);
            Invalidate();
        }

        protected override void OnMouseEnter(EventArgs e) { hover = true; Invalidate(); base.OnMouseEnter(e); }
        protected override void OnMouseLeave(EventArgs e) { hover = false; Invalidate(); base.OnMouseLeave(e); }

        protected override bool IsInputKey(Keys keyData)
        {
            return keyData == Keys.Left || keyData == Keys.Right || base.IsInputKey(keyData);
        }

        protected override void OnKeyDown(KeyEventArgs e)
        {
            base.OnKeyDown(e);
            int step = Math.Max(1, (maximum - minimum) / 50);
            if (e.KeyCode == Keys.Left) { Value -= step; RaiseScroll(); }
            if (e.KeyCode == Keys.Right) { Value += step; RaiseScroll(); }
        }

        protected override void OnPaint(PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Graphics g = e.Graphics;
            g.Clear(Shapes.SurfaceOf(this, ThemeManager.CardBack));
            g.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
            float trackHeight = Dpi.S(4F);
            float left = Height / 2F, right = Width - Height / 2F;
            float y = (Height - trackHeight) / 2F;
            float ratio = maximum > minimum ? (value - minimum) / (float)(maximum - minimum) : 0F;
            float x = left + (right - left) * ratio;
            using (GraphicsPath path = Shapes.RoundedRect(new RectangleF(left, y, Math.Max(1, right - left), trackHeight), trackHeight / 2))
            using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ThemeManager.Dark ? ThemeManager.Border : ThemeManager.BorderStrong))
                g.FillPath(brush, path);
            Color active = Enabled ? ThemeManager.Accent : ThemeManager.Muted;
            if (x - left > 1)
                using (GraphicsPath path = Shapes.RoundedRect(new RectangleF(left, y, x - left, trackHeight), trackHeight / 2))
                using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(active))
                    g.FillPath(brush, path);
            float size = (hover || dragging || Focused) ? Height - Dpi.S(4) : Height - Dpi.S(8);
            using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(active))
                g.FillEllipse(brush, x - size / 2F, (Height - size) / 2F, size, size);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Pill-shaped segmented switch (e.g. 提示詞 | 歌詞).</summary>
    internal class SegmentedControl : Control
    {
        private readonly string[] items;
        private readonly string[] icons;
        private int selected;
        private int hover = -1;

        public event EventHandler SelectedIndexChanged;

        public int Count
        {
            get { return items.Length; }
        }

        public SegmentedControl(string[] items, string[] icons)
        {
            this.items = items;
            this.icons = icons;
            SetStyle(ControlStyles.UserPaint | ControlStyles.AllPaintingInWmPaint |
                     ControlStyles.OptimizedDoubleBuffer | ControlStyles.ResizeRedraw |
                     ControlStyles.Selectable, true);
            Font = ThemeManager.ButtonFont;
            Cursor = Cursors.Hand;
            TabStop = true;
            Size = GetPreferredSize(Size.Empty);
        }

        public int SelectedIndex
        {
            get { return selected; }
            set
            {
                int clamped = Math.Max(0, Math.Min(items.Length - 1, value));
                if (clamped == selected) return;
                selected = clamped;
                Invalidate();
                EventHandler handler = SelectedIndexChanged;
                if (handler != null) handler(this, EventArgs.Empty);
            }
        }

        private int SegmentWidth(int index)
        {
            int icon = icons != null && IconRenderer.Has(icons[index]) ? Dpi.S(16) + Dpi.S(6) : 0;
            return TextRenderer.MeasureText(items[index], Font, Size.Empty, TextFormatFlags.NoPadding).Width + icon + Dpi.S(26);
        }

        public override Size GetPreferredSize(Size proposedSize)
        {
            int width = Dpi.S(6);
            for (int i = 0; i < items.Length; i++) width += SegmentWidth(i);
            return new Size(width, Dpi.S(32));
        }

        private Rectangle SegmentBounds(int index)
        {
            int x = Dpi.S(3);
            for (int i = 0; i < index; i++) x += SegmentWidth(i);
            return new Rectangle(x, Dpi.S(3), SegmentWidth(index), Height - Dpi.S(6));
        }

        private int HitTest(Point point)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < items.Length; i++)
                if (SegmentBounds(i).Contains(point)) return i;
            return -1;
        }

        protected override void OnMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
        {
            base.OnMouseMove(e);
            int index = HitTest(e.Location);
            if (index != hover) { hover = index; Invalidate(); }
        }

        protected override void OnMouseLeave(EventArgs e) { base.OnMouseLeave(e); hover = -1; Invalidate(); }

        protected override void OnMouseDown(MouseEventArgs e)
        {
            base.OnMouseDown(e);
            int index = HitTest(e.Location);
            if (index >= 0) { Focus(); SelectedIndex = index; }
        }

        protected override bool IsInputKey(Keys keyData)
        {
            return keyData == Keys.Left || keyData == Keys.Right || base.IsInputKey(keyData);
        }

        protected override void OnKeyDown(KeyEventArgs e)
        {
            base.OnKeyDown(e);
            if (e.KeyCode == Keys.Left) SelectedIndex = selected - 1;
            if (e.KeyCode == Keys.Right) SelectedIndex = selected + 1;
        }

        protected override void OnGotFocus(EventArgs e) { base.OnGotFocus(e); Invalidate(); }
        protected override void OnLostFocus(EventArgs e) { base.OnLostFocus(e); Invalidate(); }

        protected override void OnPaint(PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Graphics g = e.Graphics;
            Color surface = Shapes.SurfaceOf(this, ThemeManager.CardBack);
            g.Clear(surface);
            g.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
            RectangleF frame = new RectangleF(0.5F, 0.5F, Width - 1.5F, Height - 1.5F);
            using (GraphicsPath path = Shapes.RoundedRect(frame, Height / 2F))
            {
                using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ThemeManager.InputBack)) g.FillPath(brush, path);
                Color border = Focused && ShowFocusCues ? ThemeManager.Accent
                    : (ThemeManager.Dark ? ThemeManager.BorderStrong : ThemeManager.Border);
                using (Pen pen = new Pen(border)) g.DrawPath(pen, path);
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < items.Length; i++)
            {
                Rectangle r = SegmentBounds(i);
                bool active = i == selected;
                if (active || i == hover)
                {
                    Color fill = active ? ThemeManager.Accent : ThemeManager.Hover;
                    using (GraphicsPath path = Shapes.RoundedRect(r, r.Height / 2F))
                    using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(fill))
                        g.FillPath(brush, path);
                }
                Color fore = active ? ThemeManager.OnAccent : ThemeManager.SubText;
                bool hasIcon = icons != null && IconRenderer.Has(icons[i]);
                int iconSize = Dpi.S(16);
                Size text = TextRenderer.MeasureText(g, items[i], Font, Size.Empty, TextFormatFlags.NoPadding);
                int total = text.Width + (hasIcon ? iconSize + Dpi.S(6) : 0);
                int x = r.X + (r.Width - total) / 2;
                if (hasIcon)
                {
                    IconRenderer.Draw(g, icons[i], new RectangleF(x, r.Y + (r.Height - iconSize) / 2F, iconSize, iconSize), fore);
                    x += iconSize + Dpi.S(6);
                }
                TextRenderer.DrawText(g, items[i], Font, new Rectangle(x, r.Y, r.Right - x, r.Height), fore,
                    TextFormatFlags.VerticalCenter | TextFormatFlags.Left | TextFormatFlags.NoPadding | TextFormatFlags.NoPrefix);
            }
        }
    }

    /// <summary>App logo: gradient tile with the Lucide "audio-lines" glyph.</summary>
    internal class LogoTile : Control
    {
        public LogoTile()
        {
            SetStyle(ControlStyles.UserPaint | ControlStyles.AllPaintingInWmPaint |
                     ControlStyles.OptimizedDoubleBuffer | ControlStyles.ResizeRedraw, true);
            Size = Dpi.S(40, 40);
        }

        protected override void OnPaint(PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Graphics g = e.Graphics;
            g.Clear(Shapes.SurfaceOf(this, ThemeManager.FormBack));
            g.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
            Draw(g, new RectangleF(0, 0, Width - 1, Height - 1));
        }

        public static void Draw(Graphics g, RectangleF rect)
        {
            using (GraphicsPath path = Shapes.RoundedRect(rect, rect.Width * 0.26F))
            using (LinearGradientBrush brush = new LinearGradientBrush(rect, ThemeManager.Accent, ThemeManager.Accent2, 45F))
                g.FillPath(brush, path);
            float pad = rect.Width * 0.22F;
            IconRenderer.Draw(g, "audio-lines", new RectangleF(rect.X + pad, rect.Y + pad, rect.Width - pad * 2,
                rect.Height - pad * 2), ThemeManager.OnAccent, 2.2F);
        }
    }
}


===== [32/66] AI_Music/app/src/UiScreenshot.cs  (11225 bytes) =====
using System;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Drawing.Imaging;
using System.IO;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace AIMusicGenerator
{
    /// <summary>Test helper: shows the main window or a dialog with demo content and saves a PNG.</summary>
    internal static class UiScreenshot
    {
        [System.Runtime.InteropServices.DllImport("user32.dll")]
        private static extern bool PrintWindow(IntPtr hwnd, IntPtr hdc, uint flags);

        public static int Run(string target, bool dark, string demoDir, string dialog)
        {
            int code = 1;
            if (dialog == "icons") return IconSheet(target, dark);
            if (dialog == "art") return ArtGenerator.Render(target);
            try
            {
                ThemeManager.SetTheme(dark);
                Form form;
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(dialog) || dialog == "main")
                {
                    MainForm main = new MainForm();
                    main.PrepareScreenshot(dark, demoDir);
                    form = main;
                }
                else
                {
                    form = CreateDialog(dialog, demoDir);
                    form.StartPosition = FormStartPosition.Manual;
                    form.Location = new Point(40, 40);
                }
                form.TopMost = true;
                form.ShowInTaskbar = false;
                Timer timer = new Timer();
                timer.Interval = 1500;
                timer.Tick += delegate
                {
                    timer.Stop();
                    try
                    {
                        Rectangle bounds = form.Bounds;
                        using (Bitmap bitmap = new Bitmap(bounds.Width, bounds.Height))
                        {
                            // PrintWindow renders the window itself (works while the session is locked
                            // and never captures other windows or the desktop).
                            bool ok;
                            using (Graphics g = Graphics.FromImage(bitmap))
                            {
                                IntPtr hdc = g.GetHdc();
                                try { ok = PrintWindow(form.Handle, hdc, 2); }
                                finally { g.ReleaseHdc(hdc); }
                            }
                            if (!ok) form.DrawToBitmap(bitmap, new Rectangle(Point.Empty, bounds.Size));
                            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(Path.GetFullPath(target)));
                            bitmap.Save(target, ImageFormat.Png);
                        }
                        code = 0;
                    }
                    catch (Exception ex)
                    {
                        Console.Error.WriteLine(ex);
                    }
                    form.Close();
                };
                form.Shown += delegate { timer.Start(); };
                Application.Run(form);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                File.WriteAllText(target + ".error.txt", ex.ToString());
            }
            return code;
        }

        private static int IconSheet(string target, bool dark)
        {
            ThemeManager.SetTheme(dark);
            int cell = 72, cols = 8;
            System.Collections.Generic.List<string> names = new System.Collections.Generic.List<string>(LucideIcons.Paths.Keys);
            names.Sort(StringComparer.Ordinal);
            int rows = (names.Count + cols - 1) / cols;
            using (Bitmap bitmap = new Bitmap(cols * cell, rows * (cell + 16)))
            using (Graphics g = Graphics.FromImage(bitmap))
            {
                g.Clear(ThemeManager.CardBack);
                for (int i = 0; i < names.Count; i++)
                {
                    int x = (i % cols) * cell, y = (i / cols) * (cell + 16);
                    IconRenderer.Draw(g, names[i], new RectangleF(x + 18, y + 8, 36, 36), ThemeManager.Text);
                    IconRenderer.Draw(g, names[i], new RectangleF(x + 4, y + 50, 16, 16), ThemeManager.Accent);
                    TextRenderer.DrawText(g, names[i], new Font("Segoe UI", 7F), new Rectangle(x, y + 70, cell, 14),
                        ThemeManager.SubText, TextFormatFlags.HorizontalCenter);
                }
                bitmap.Save(target, ImageFormat.Png);
            }
            return 0;
        }

        private static Form CreateDialog(string name, string demoDir)
        {
            switch (name)
            {
                case "advanced": return new AdvancedDialog("vocals, harsh noise", 2m, 3m, 1.0m, "192k", 48000);
                case "album": return new AlbumDialog("Warm lo-fi chill instrumental, no vocals.", 180);
                case "link": return new LinkPromptDialog();
                case "sd": return new SdDialog(true, "http://127.0.0.1:7860", @"G:\stable-diffusion-webui", "", 20, 512);
                case "media": return new MediaToolsDialog(string.IsNullOrEmpty(demoDir) ? Paths.Generated : demoDir, null);
                case "editor": return new PromptEditorDialog("Warm lo-fi hip hop, dusty boom bap drums, mellow Rhodes piano.");
                case "preview":
                {
                    string[] wavs = Directory.GetFiles(demoDir, "*.wav");
                    string cover = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, "cover.png");
                    return new PreviewDialog(wavs[0], cover, "");
                }
                default: throw new ArgumentException("unknown dialog: " + name);
            }
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Renders app.ico and the default cover.png from the Lucide "audio-lines" glyph and the
    /// Catppuccin Mocha palette (both open source), so no third-party bitmap is shipped.
    /// </summary>
    internal static class ArtGenerator
    {
        public static int Render(string folder)
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory(folder);
            ThemeManager.SetTheme(true);
            WriteIcon(Path.Combine(folder, "app.ico"), new int[] { 16, 20, 24, 32, 40, 48, 64, 128, 256 });
            using (Bitmap cover = RenderCover(1600))
                cover.Save(Path.Combine(folder, "cover.png"), ImageFormat.Png);
            using (Bitmap preview = RenderLogo(512))
                preview.Save(Path.Combine(folder, "app_icon_512.png"), ImageFormat.Png);
            return 0;
        }

        private static Bitmap RenderLogo(int size)
        {
            Bitmap bitmap = new Bitmap(size, size, PixelFormat.Format32bppArgb);
            using (Graphics g = Graphics.FromImage(bitmap))
            {
                g.Clear(Color.Transparent);
                g.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
                float pad = size <= 24 ? 0.5F : size * 0.04F;
                LogoTile.Draw(g, new RectangleF(pad, pad, size - pad * 2 - 1, size - pad * 2 - 1));
            }
            return bitmap;
        }

        private static void WriteIcon(string path, int[] sizes)
        {
            System.Collections.Generic.List<byte[]> images = new System.Collections.Generic.List<byte[]>();
            foreach (int size in sizes)
            {
                using (Bitmap bitmap = RenderLogo(size))
                using (MemoryStream png = new MemoryStream())
                {
                    bitmap.Save(png, ImageFormat.Png);
                    images.Add(png.ToArray());
                }
            }
            using (FileStream stream = File.Create(path))
            using (BinaryWriter writer = new BinaryWriter(stream))
            {
                writer.Write((short)0);
                writer.Write((short)1);
                writer.Write((short)sizes.Length);
                int offset = 6 + 16 * sizes.Length;
                for (int i = 0; i < sizes.Length; i++)
                {
                    writer.Write((byte)(sizes[i] >= 256 ? 0 : sizes[i]));
                    writer.Write((byte)(sizes[i] >= 256 ? 0 : sizes[i]));
                    writer.Write((byte)0);
                    writer.Write((byte)0);
                    writer.Write((short)1);
                    writer.Write((short)32);
                    writer.Write(images[i].Length);
                    writer.Write(offset);
                    offset += images[i].Length;
                }
                foreach (byte[] image in images) writer.Write(image);
            }
        }

        private static Bitmap RenderCover(int size)
        {
            Bitmap bitmap = new Bitmap(size, size, PixelFormat.Format32bppArgb);
            using (Graphics g = Graphics.FromImage(bitmap))
            {
                g.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
                Rectangle all = new Rectangle(0, 0, size, size);
                using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.LinearGradientBrush back = new System.Drawing.Drawing2D.LinearGradientBrush(
                    all, Color.FromArgb(0x11, 0x11, 0x1B), Color.FromArgb(0x1E, 0x1E, 0x2E), 60F))
                    g.FillRectangle(back, all);
                Glow(g, size * 0.28F, size * 0.30F, size * 0.55F, Color.FromArgb(0xCB, 0xA6, 0xF7), 70);
                Glow(g, size * 0.74F, size * 0.72F, size * 0.60F, Color.FromArgb(0x89, 0xB4, 0xFA), 60);
                using (Pen ring = new Pen(Color.FromArgb(28, 0xCD, 0xD6, 0xF4), size * 0.004F))
                {
                    for (int i = 1; i <= 4; i++)
                    {
                        float r = size * (0.22F + i * 0.085F);
                        g.DrawEllipse(ring, size / 2F - r, size / 2F - r, r * 2, r * 2);
                    }
                }
                float tile = size * 0.40F;
                RectangleF rect = new RectangleF((size - tile) / 2F, (size - tile) / 2F, tile, tile);
                using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath shadow = Shapes.RoundedRect(
                    new RectangleF(rect.X, rect.Y + size * 0.02F, rect.Width, rect.Height), tile * 0.26F))
                using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(Color.FromArgb(90, 0, 0, 0)))
                    g.FillPath(brush, shadow);
                LogoTile.Draw(g, rect);
            }
            return bitmap;
        }

        private static void Glow(Graphics g, float cx, float cy, float radius, Color color, int alpha)
        {
            using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath path = new System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath())
            {
                path.AddEllipse(cx - radius, cy - radius, radius * 2, radius * 2);
                using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.PathGradientBrush brush = new System.Drawing.Drawing2D.PathGradientBrush(path))
                {
                    brush.CenterColor = Color.FromArgb(alpha, color);
                    brush.SurroundColors = new Color[] { Color.FromArgb(0, color) };
                    g.FillPath(brush, path);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}


===== [33/66] AI_Music/app/src/VideoRunner.cs  (1851 bytes) =====
using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;

namespace AIMusicGenerator
{
    internal static class VideoRunner
    {
        public static int Run(string audioPath, string imagePath, Action<string> log)
        {
            return Run(audioPath, imagePath, "blur", 5, log);
        }

        public static int Run(string audioPath, string imagePath, string style, int fps,
            Action<string> log)
        {
            if (!File.Exists(Paths.VideoTool))
            {
                log("=== 找不到影片轉換腳本: " + Paths.VideoTool + " ===");
                return 2;
            }
            if (!File.Exists(Paths.PythonExe))
            {
                log("=== 找不到 Python: " + Paths.PythonExe + " ===");
                return 2;
            }
            try
            {
                using (Process process = new Process())
                {
                    process.StartInfo = Paths.BuildVideoStartInfo(audioPath, imagePath, style, fps);
                    process.OutputDataReceived += delegate(object sender, DataReceivedEventArgs e)
                    {
                        if (e.Data != null) log(e.Data);
                    };
                    process.ErrorDataReceived += delegate(object sender, DataReceivedEventArgs e)
                    {
                        if (e.Data != null) log("[video] " + e.Data);
                    };
                    process.Start();
                    process.BeginOutputReadLine();
                    process.BeginErrorReadLine();
                    process.WaitForExit();
                    return process.ExitCode;
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                log("=== 影片轉換啟動失敗: " + ex.Message + " ===");
                return 1;
            }
        }
    }
}


===== [34/66] AI_Music/app/src/WaveformPanel.cs  (4375 bytes) =====
using System;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Drawing.Drawing2D;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace AIMusicGenerator
{
    /// <summary>Rounded bar-style waveform with played/pending colouring and click-to-seek.</summary>
    internal sealed class WaveformPanel : Control
    {
        public float[] Peaks;
        public double Duration;
        public double Position;
        public bool ThemeDark = true;
        public event Action<double> SeekRequested;
        private int hoverX = -1;

        public WaveformPanel()
        {
            SetStyle(ControlStyles.UserPaint | ControlStyles.AllPaintingInWmPaint |
                     ControlStyles.OptimizedDoubleBuffer | ControlStyles.ResizeRedraw, true);
            Cursor = Cursors.Hand;
        }

        protected override void OnPaint(PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Graphics g = e.Graphics;
            Color surface = Parent != null ? Parent.BackColor : ThemeManager.CardBack;
            g.Clear(surface);
            g.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
            RectangleF frame = new RectangleF(0.5F, 0.5F, Width - 1.5F, Height - 1.5F);
            using (GraphicsPath path = Shapes.RoundedRect(frame, 10))
            using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ThemeManager.InputBack))
                g.FillPath(brush, path);

            int width = Width;
            int height = Height;
            if (Peaks == null || Peaks.Length == 0)
            {
                IconRenderer.Draw(g, "audio-waveform", new RectangleF(width / 2F - 92, height / 2F - 9, 18, 18), ThemeManager.Muted);
                TextRenderer.DrawText(g, "載入音檔後顯示波形，點擊可跳轉", ThemeManager.SmallFont,
                    new Rectangle(width / 2 - 68, 0, width / 2 + 68, height), ThemeManager.Muted,
                    TextFormatFlags.VerticalCenter | TextFormatFlags.Left | TextFormatFlags.NoPrefix);
                return;
            }

            const int bar = 3;
            const int gap = 2;
            int inner = Math.Max(1, width - 20);
            int count = Math.Max(1, inner / (bar + gap));
            float center = height / 2F;
            float maxAmp = (height - 14) / 2F;
            float playedX = 10 + (float)(Position * inner);
            using (SolidBrush played = new SolidBrush(ThemeManager.Accent))
            using (SolidBrush pending = new SolidBrush(ThemeManager.Blend(ThemeManager.InputBack, ThemeManager.SubText, 0.45)))
            using (SolidBrush hovered = new SolidBrush(ThemeManager.Blend(ThemeManager.InputBack, ThemeManager.Accent, 0.55)))
            {
                for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
                {
                    int start = (int)((long)i * Peaks.Length / count);
                    int end = Math.Max(start + 1, (int)((long)(i + 1) * Peaks.Length / count));
                    float peak = 0F;
                    for (int j = start; j < end && j < Peaks.Length; j++) peak = Math.Max(peak, Peaks[j]);
                    float amp = Math.Max(1.5F, (float)Math.Sqrt(Math.Min(1F, peak)) * maxAmp);
                    float x = 10 + i * (bar + gap);
                    Brush brush = x <= playedX ? played : (hoverX >= 0 && x <= hoverX ? hovered : pending);
                    using (GraphicsPath path = Shapes.RoundedRect(new RectangleF(x, center - amp, bar, amp * 2), 1.5F))
                        g.FillPath(brush, path);
                }
            }
            using (Pen cursor = new Pen(ThemeManager.Text, 1.5F))
                g.DrawLine(cursor, playedX, 6, playedX, height - 6);
        }

        protected override void OnMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
        {
            base.OnMouseMove(e);
            if (Peaks == null) return;
            hoverX = e.X;
            Invalidate();
        }

        protected override void OnMouseLeave(EventArgs e)
        {
            base.OnMouseLeave(e);
            hoverX = -1;
            Invalidate();
        }

        protected override void OnMouseDown(MouseEventArgs e)
        {
            base.OnMouseDown(e);
            if (Peaks == null || Width <= 20) return;
            double ratio = Math.Max(0.0, Math.Min(1.0, (e.X - 10.0) / (Width - 20.0)));
            if (SeekRequested != null) SeekRequested(ratio);
        }
    }
}

===== [37/66] AI_Music/AUTOMATION_REPORT_20261117.md  (9388 bytes) =====
# AI Music Maker 自動化建置報告

日期：2026-11-17  
範圍：測試音樂＋封面＋自動轉影片、時長與提示詞 UI、風格模板、GPU 面板評估、YouTube 上傳規劃

## 1. 總結

- 已完成 8 分鐘測試曲的生成、封面套用與自動轉 MP4，並實際驗證可播放。
- 已完成時長上限 120 分鐘、小時／分鐘輸入、提示詞高度解除固定上限、10 組音樂風格模板。
- GPU 監控面板已完成評估，結論是不直接併入 AI Music Maker，避免與 ACE-Step 搶 VRAM、連接埠與 F.A.H. 控制權。
- YouTube 自動上傳已完成流程與條件規劃，依指示尚未開發。
- 已重新編譯 App 並通過 selftest、UI selftest、自動轉影片測試。

## 2. 測試成品

輸出資料夾：`G:\AIMUSIC\AI_Music\generated`

| 檔案 | 大小 | 說明 |
|---|---:|---|
| `ace_20261117_021553_seed1706262108.mp3` | 7,813,920 bytes | 480.0 秒、48 kHz、立體聲、MP3 |
| `ace_20261117_021553_seed1706262108.json` | 2,318 bytes | 生成參數與音訊資訊 |
| `ace_20261117_021553_seed1706262108.mp4` | 17,993,038 bytes | 1920×1080、30 fps、H.264、AAC |

- 封面來源：`G:\AIMUSIC\AI_Music\app\cover.png`（目前 SD 封面未啟用，因此使用 App 預設封面）。
- 生成時間：約 591 秒（8 分鐘曲）。
- 影片轉檔時間：約 7 分鐘。
- MP4 實測長度：8 分 1.77 秒；音訊 480 秒，結尾多約 1.77 秒，為 MP3 解碼尾端補齊，不影響播放。
- 已抽格確認畫面為模糊背景＋中央封面，正常顯示。
- 註：P1-6 之後影片預設改為 5 fps、veryfast，8 分鐘轉檔約 81 秒（見 CODE_REVIEW_FIXES_20261117.md）。

## 3. 已完成變更

### 3.1 時長放寬與小時／分鐘

- 時長上限由 600 秒提高到 7200 秒（120 分鐘）。
- 主畫面改為「時」＋「分」兩個輸入，不再直接輸入秒數。
- 內部仍以秒傳給後端，設定檔 `duration` 保持秒數，相容舊設定。
- 專輯對話框每首長度上限同步提高到 7200 秒。
- 後端 `generate_backend.py` 驗證範圍改為 10–7200 秒。

驗證：

- `duration_max=2h0m total=7200`
- 舊設定 480 秒載入為 `0h8m total=480`
- 後端實測接受 7200、拒絕 7201。

### 3.2 提示詞高度

- 移除 `260 px` 固定上限，數字上限改為 10000。
- 高度只受目前視窗可用空間限制；在 1080p 最大化下實測可到 294 px。
- 新增「放大」按鈕，開啟 1000×760 可調大小的大型提示詞編輯視窗，可輸入任意長度內容。

### 3.3 音樂風格模板

新增 10 組模板，套用時會設定：

- 提示詞（英文，利於模型理解）
- BPM
- 調性
- 拍號
- 純音樂開關
- 歌詞欄設為 `[Instrumental]`

模板清單：

1. Lo-fi 深夜雨景
2. Chillhop 咖啡廳
3. Ambient 漂浮
4. Cinematic 電影感
5. Acoustic 木吉他
6. Jazz 深夜鋼琴
7. Synthwave 復古
8. EDM 柔和電子
9. Rock 溫暖抒情
10. Hip-Hop 慵懶

介面位置：BPM／時長同一列右側「模板」下拉選單＋「套用」按鈕。  
實測套用第一組：`72 BPM / C Minor / 206 chars`，控制項未超出列寬（右緣 1449 / 列寬 1476）。

### 3.4 自動轉影片

- 生成完成後依「自動轉影片」勾選自動排入轉檔佇列。
- 單曲與批次皆支援；SD 封面啟用時會等封面完成再轉。
- 已存在且大於 1 KB 的 MP4 會跳過；未完成的小檔會刪除重轉。
- 使用背景單一佇列，避免同時多個 ffmpeg 搶 CPU。

## 4. GPU 監控面板評估

評估對象：`D:\VS CODE\PROJECT\開啟AI監控面板.bat` 與 `GPU Intelligent Regulation System\ai_proxy_daemon.py`。

### 4.1 實際內容

- 不是單純的 GPU 監控面板，而是：
  - OpenAI 相容代理（127.0.0.1:8080）
  - 管理 llama-server（127.0.0.1:8081）
  - 載入 Qwen GGUF 模型
  - 協調 Folding@home 暫停／恢復
  - 休眠時釋放 VRAM
- 使用 nvidia-smi 讀取 GPU 使用率、VRAM、溫度與功耗。

### 4.2 結論

不建議直接整合進 AI Music Maker。

原因：

- 它會額外啟動一個 LLM 服務，與 ACE-Step 爭用 VRAM。
- 連接埠 8080／8081 與現有服務可能衝突。
- 它會控制 Folding@home，與音樂生成流程無關，誤操作風險高。
- 它無法控制 ACE-Step 的模型卸載；對 ACE-Step 的 VRAM 智慧調節沒有實質幫助。

建議：

1. 若要監控，只在 App 讀取 `nvidia-smi` 顯示使用率、VRAM、溫度、功耗（唯讀）。
2. 若要完整面板，維持獨立視窗，不併入 App。
3. 若要智慧調節 ACE-Step，應在生成流程中加入「生成前後卸載模型／清空 CUDA 快取」，而不是接這支 daemon。

## 5. YouTube 自動上傳規劃

完整內容見：`G:\AIMUSIC\AI_Music\YOUTUBE_AUTO_UPLOAD_PLAN.md`

重點：

- 使用 YouTube Data API v3 與 OAuth 2.0 Desktop app。
- 每次上傳約 1,600 quota units；每日 10,000 units，實務上每天約 6 支為上限。
- 上傳前必須通過：MP4 可解析、格式正確、音軌存在、標題／說明／標籤長度、縮圖 1280×720 且小於 2 MB、無重複內容、token 有效、quota 足夠。
- 建議預設上傳為「私人」或「不公開」，確認後再公開。
- 狀態機：pending → validated → uploading → uploaded → thumbnail_set → playlist_set → published / failed。
- 4xx 不重試；5xx／網路錯誤最多重試 3 次，使用 resumable upload。
- 憑證以 DPAPI 加密保存，不進 Git。
- 分三階段：手動上傳 → 半自動（私人）→ 全自動排程。

## 6. 驗證紀錄

| 項目 | 結果 |
|---|---|
| `build.ps1` 編譯 | 通過 |
| `--selftest` | 通過 |
| `--ui-selftest` | 通過，`duration_max=2h0m`、`style_templates=10`、`prompt_max_height=294` |
| 後端 7200 秒驗證 | 接受 |
| 後端 7201 秒驗證 | 拒絕 |
| `--auto-video-test` | 通過，產出 187,142 bytes MP4 |
| 實際 8 分鐘曲轉檔 | 通過，17,993,038 bytes、1920×1080、H.264＋AAC |
| 影片抽格 | 封面與模糊背景正常 |

## 7. 限制與風險

1. **120 分鐘目前只是設定可接受，不代表引擎一定能單次生成。**  
   ACE-Step 在 12 GB GPU 上的單次生成上限依官方文件約為 4–8 分鐘；超過時可能耗盡 VRAM 或失敗。若要真正支援 120 分鐘，需另做「長曲分段生成＋交叉淡入串接」，本次未實作。

2. 8 分鐘影片以 libx264 medium 轉檔約需 7 分鐘；120 分鐘影片會非常久，建議之後改用較快 preset 或硬體編碼。

3. SD 封面目前未啟用，測試成品使用 App 預設封面；啟用 SD 後會優先生成 `<歌名>.cover.png` 並用於影片。

4. YouTube 上傳尚未開發，且需要 Google Cloud 專案、OAuth 授權與頻道驗證。

5. GPU 監控面板未整合，僅完成評估。

## 8. 建議下一步

1. 實作長曲分段生成與串接，才能真正支援 30–120 分鐘。
2. 在 App 加入唯讀 GPU 狀態列（nvidia-smi）。
3. 實作 YouTube 半自動上傳（私人）與 metadata／縮圖產生。
4. 啟用 SD 封面並驗證「生成 → 封面 → 影片」完整鏈路。
5. 將轉檔 preset 改為 `veryfast` 或加入 GPU 編碼選項，降低長曲轉檔時間。

## 9. 後續追加（2026-11-17）

### 9.1 媒體工具

App 輸出資料夾列新增「工具」按鈕，開啟「媒體工具」對話框：

- 淡入／淡出：對選取音檔套用，輸出 `<歌名>_fade.<副檔名>`，不覆蓋原檔。
- 音量正規化：批次套用 loudnorm，目標 -14 LUFS、TP -1.5 dB，輸出 `<歌名>_norm.<副檔名>`。
- 合併：多選 2 首以上，依清單順序合併為 `merged_日期_時間.mp3`。
- 預覽：顯示封面、播放音訊、進度拖曳；有同名 MP4 時可開啟影片。

底層：`AI_Music/scripts/audio_edit.py`（fade／normalize／merge）。
UI：`MediaToolsDialog.cs`、`PreviewDialog.cs`、`AudioEditRunner.cs`。

驗證：`--audio-edit-test` 通過，fade／normalize／merge 三項 exit code 均為 0；工具對話框建立成功（15 個控制項）。

### 9.2 Telegram 遠端控制

`telegram_bot.py` 已擴充，仍只接受 `telegram.json` 設定的 chat_id：

- `/gen <提示詞>`：生成並回傳音訊。
- `/album 名稱 | 提示詞1 ; 提示詞2`：專輯生成。
- `/status`：工作狀態、長度、影片／淡入淡出設定、近期檔案數。
- `/cancel`：終止目前生成程序樹。
- `/duration 95｜1:35｜1:02:03`：設定長度，支援 10 秒–2 小時。
- `/video on|off`：生成後自動轉 MP4 並回傳。
- `/fade 2 3`、`/fade off`：設定或關閉淡入淡出。
- `/merge 2`：合併最近 2–10 首。
- `/outputs`：列出最近檔案。

設定保存在 `%APPDATA%\AIMusicGenerator\telegram_settings.json`。  
啟用方式：App 上方「TG」按鈕，或手動執行 `python scripts/telegram_bot.py`。

驗證：語法檢查通過；`parse_duration` 實測 `1:35=95`、`1:02:03=3723`、`9=拒絕`；格式化 `7200=2:00:00`。

### 9.3 尚未完成

- 自動 metadata（標題／說明／標籤）尚未實作。
- YouTube 上傳佇列尚未實作（仍為規劃）。
- 長曲分段生成尚未實作。
- 遠端控制目前為 Telegram；尚未建立 Web 面板。


===== [38/66] AI_Music/CODE_REVIEW_FIXES_20261117.md  (25024 bytes) =====
# 程式碼審查修正紀錄

日期：2026-11-17  
來源審查：`AI_Music_Maker_Review_20261117.md`（Telegram 收到的審查檔）

## 已修正

| 編號 | 修正內容 |
|---|---|
| P0-1 | `run_job()` 改為回傳 `(code, Path)`；專輯直接使用回傳音檔，不再用 mtime glob，避免把 `.json` 當音檔。 |
| P0-2 | 專輯先把 `prompt`／`duration` 合併進設定再 `normalize_config()`，超界 duration 會被拒絕。 |
| P0-3 | 音訊統計改為 `sf.info`＋`sf.blocks` 分塊計算 peak／RMS，不再整檔讀入記憶體；格式不支援時只跳過統計。 |
| P0-5 | Telegram 日誌與訊息加入 token redaction，例外訊息不再輸出 bot token。 |
| P0-6 | 關閉 App 時 Telegram 子程序改用 `taskkill /T /F`，避免 `generate_backend.py` 變成孤兒。 |
| P0-7 | 測試改為 10–7200 秒，新增專輯檔名與 duration 驗證回歸測試；`build.ps1` 會執行 `unittest`，失敗即中止。 |
| P0-8 | `verify_migration.ps1` 發現殘留舊路徑時會呼叫 `Add-Failure`，不再誤報 PASS。 |
| P1-3 | 專輯資料夾改用 `currentAlbumFolder`，不再永久改寫主畫面輸出資料夾。 |
| P1-4 | 批次完成時補上 `TryTagOutput()`；SD 封面仍未排入批次佇列。 |
| P1-6 | 影片預設改為 5 fps、`veryfast`、CRF 23、48 kHz，並用 `-t` 指定音訊長度；8 分鐘轉檔由約 7 分鐘降到約 81 秒，長度 8:00.00。 |
| P1-7 | TagLib 開檔改用 `using`，避免檔案鎖住。 |

## 尚未修正（需較大變更）

> 此段為第 1 批當時的紀錄；後續批次已完成多項，最新狀態請見文末「最新狀態（2026-11-17）」表。

- P0-4 GPU 跨程序互斥：仍需單一生成服務或跨程序鎖；目前 App daemon、Telegram、SD 仍可能同時搶 VRAM。
- P1-1 取消保留模型：仍是軟取消，daemon 會把目前曲跑完。
- P1-2 只把警告門檻改為 `maxDurationWithLm`；未自動關閉 thinking 或阻擋超界。
- P1-5 日誌進度行仍會整段重寫，長日誌可能卡頓。
- P1-8 SD WebUI 啟動埠與 static 程序鎖未處理。
- P1-9 其他小問題（LinkAnalyzer 子字串、UI 跨執行緒、原子寫入、seed 溢位等）未處理。
- P2 架構重構（拆 MainForm、狀態機、JSON Lines job id）未處理。

## 驗證

- `python -m unittest discover -s AI_Music\tests`：9 tests OK。
- `build.ps1`：編譯＋selftest＋unittest 通過。

- 影片實測：6 秒輸出 6.00 秒、5 fps、48 kHz；8 分鐘輸出 8:00.00，轉檔約 81 秒。

### N1 做法

- `run_album()` 在 `load_handlers()` 前先驗證全部曲目；失敗輸出
  `[error] album track <N> invalid: <原因>`，寫入 `invalid`。
- 全部不合法時不載入模型，仍寫 `album.json`（`tracks: []`）並輸出 `[albumdone]`。
- 合法曲目生成時若 `run_job` 拋例外，記錄到 `invalid` 後繼續；`album.json` 在
  `finally` 寫出，已完成的曲目不會遺失。
- `main()` 的 `--album` 加入 try/except，例外輸出 `[error] <類型>: <訊息>`。
- App `OnAlbum()` 拒絕任何超過 7200 秒的曲目，列出實際行號與內容後不送出。

### P0-4 做法

- 新增 `app/gpu_lock.py`：使用 `msvcrt.locking` 鎖定 `AI_Music/.gpu.lock`，
  持有者資訊寫入 `.gpu.owner.json`，程序死亡由 OS 釋放。
- `run_oneshot`、`run_daemon`、`run_album` 在載入模型前取得鎖，釋放模型後釋放；
  `--preflight`、`--limits` 不取鎖。
- 拿不到鎖輸出 `[busy] <owner>`、`[error] GPU 使用中`，exit code 75。
- App daemon／專輯啟動參數加 `--gpu-wait 20`；收到 `[busy]` 更新狀態列，
  exit 75 顯示 MessageBox 並恢復 UI。
- Telegram `/gen`、`/album` 遇到 exit 75 讀取 run.log 的 `[busy]` 並回覆使用者。

### P1-2 做法

- `ConfirmLongDuration()`：當 `chkThinking.Checked` 且超過 `maxDurationWithLm` 時，
  顯示 Yes／No／Cancel；Yes 關閉 LM 思考，Cancel 不生成。
- 單曲在送出前問一次；批次在開始前問一次；專輯以最長曲目問一次。
- `uiTestTimer != null` 時略過對話框，避免 UI 測試卡住；原警告日誌保留。

### N2 做法

- YouTube Gate 改為 `fps ≥ 1（目前預設 5 fps 靜態畫面）`。
- 自動化報告只在測試成品表格下方新增一行 P1-6 註記，未改寫歷史內容。

### 實機測試結果

- 後端持鎖 CLI：`[busy] pid=... source=child`、`[error] GPU 使用中`、exit 75（通過）。
- Telegram `--test-gen` 搭配假鎖：`run.log` 出現 `[busy]`／`[error]`，backend exit 75；
  未獨立確認聊天室訊息（未驗證）。
- App daemon 常駐後從 Telegram `/gen` 的完整 OOM 驗證：**未執行／未驗證**
  （需要載入模型與實際 Telegram 收發）。
- 關閉 App 後 `/gen` 成功、切換 offload 後重啟：**未執行／未驗證**。
- P1-2 15 分鐘對話框三分支：**未執行／未驗證**（UI selftest 僅確認不卡住）。

  沒有「卸載模型」按鈕，唯一釋放方式是關閉 App。

分支：`fix/review-batch8`

| 項目 | 修改檔案 | 狀態 | 驗證證據 |
|---|---|---|---|

| 批次每首 SD 封面 | `MainForm.cs` | 程式完成；實機未驗證 | `StartBatchCovers` 逐首產生與排入影片 |

### 長曲流程

1. `longform_generate.py` 讀 base config，依 target/chunk/crossfade 計算段數。
2. 建立 album JSON，第一段加 intro、中間段加 seamless continuation、最後段加 outro。
3. 呼叫 `generate_backend.py --album`，模型只載入一次。
4. 用 ffmpeg `acrossfade` 串接、loudnorm、fade、裁到目標長度。
5. 輸出 MP3 與 `.youtube.json`。

### 限制

- 長曲尚未接入 App UI／Telegram 指令，目前是 CLI。
- YouTube 尚未提供 OAuth client，無法做私人上傳實機測試。
- 分段接縫仍可能出現和聲／節奏漂移；需 30 分鐘實聽驗證。

| 長曲影片 | `telegram_bot.py` | 完成 | 30 分鐘內才自動轉 MP4，超過則略過 |
| 編譯 | - | 通過 | `py_compile telegram_bot.py` 成功 |

- `/longform <提示詞>` 需先 `/duration` 設定超過 5 分鐘。

- Telegram Bot API 單檔上限 50 MB，120 分鐘長曲無法直接傳送；檔案仍留在本機輸出資料夾。

### 長曲 OOM 修正內容

- `generate_backend.py` 匯入階段設定 `PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True`，
  降低連續生成造成的 VRAM 碎片。
- `longform_generate.py` 每 `--chunks-per-process`（預設 3）段就重新啟動 backend，
  以新程序取得乾淨的 CUDA allocator；`0` 表示全部同一程序（舊行為）。
- 每段 seed = 基礎 seed + 段序，修正原本所有段落共用同一 seed 的問題。
- `--python` 可指定 backend 解譯器，未指定時優先用 `AI_Music.venv`。
