===== [40/66] AI_Music/scripts/_common.ps1  (897 bytes) =====
# Shared PowerShell helpers for the AI_Music scripts.
# Dot-source from a script: . (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '_common.ps1')

# Run a native executable with stdout/stderr captured to temp files, so
# PowerShell 5.1 never turns uv/pip progress output into NativeCommandError
# records. Returns the process exit code.
function Run-Native($exe, [string[]]$argv) {
    $tmpO = [IO.Path]::GetTempFileName()
    $tmpE = [IO.Path]::GetTempFileName()
    $saved = $ErrorActionPreference
    $ErrorActionPreference = 'SilentlyContinue'
    & $exe @argv 1> $tmpO 2> $tmpE
    $code = $LASTEXITCODE
    $ErrorActionPreference = $saved
    foreach ($f in @($tmpO, $tmpE)) {
        Get-Content -LiteralPath $f -EA SilentlyContinue |
            Where-Object { $_ -ne '' } | ForEach-Object { Write-Host ("    " + $_) }
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $f -Force -EA SilentlyContinue
    }
    return $code
}


===== [41/66] AI_Music/scripts/_common.py  (1534 bytes) =====
"""Shared helpers for the AI_Music maintenance scripts.

Kept dependency-free so every script can import it before torch/ACE-Step.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
REPO_DIR = BASE_DIR / "ACE-Step-1.5"
MODELS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "models" / "Ace-Step1.5"


def safe_stdio() -> None:
    for stream in (sys.stdin, sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
        try:
            stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        except (AttributeError, ValueError):
            pass


def human(num: float) -> str:
    for unit in ("B", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB"):
        if abs(num) < 1024.0:
            return f"{num:.2f} {unit}"
        num /= 1024.0
    return f"{num:.2f} PB"


def bootstrap(base_dir: Path | None = None) -> None:
    """Point ACE-Step at the local models and force offline execution."""
    base = Path(base_dir) if base_dir is not None else BASE_DIR
    repo = base / "ACE-Step-1.5"
    models = base / "models" / "Ace-Step1.5"
    os.environ["ACESTEP_CHECKPOINTS_DIR"] = str(models)
    os.environ["HF_HOME"] = str(base / ".hf-cache")
    os.environ["HF_HUB_OFFLINE"] = "1"
    os.environ["TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE"] = "1"
    os.environ["TOKENIZERS_PARALLELISM"] = "false"
    os.environ["TORCHAUDIO_USE_BACKEND"] = "ffmpeg"
    for var in ("http_proxy", "https_proxy", "HTTP_PROXY", "HTTPS_PROXY", "ALL_PROXY"):
        os.environ.pop(var, None)
    if str(repo) not in sys.path:
        sys.path.insert(0, str(repo))


===== [42/66] AI_Music/scripts/audio_edit.py  (6994 bytes) =====
"""Post-process audio: fade in/out and merge multiple tracks with ffmpeg."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import re
import subprocess
import sys
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

from _common import BASE_DIR, safe_stdio

FFMPEG = BASE_DIR / "tools" / "ffmpeg" / "bin" / "ffmpeg.exe"
AUDIO_EXTS = {".mp3", ".wav", ".flac"}


def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Audio fade and merge tools.")
    parser.add_argument("--ffmpeg", type=Path, default=FFMPEG)
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)

    fade = sub.add_parser("fade", help="apply fade in/out to one audio file")
    fade.add_argument("input", type=Path)
    fade.add_argument("--fade-in", type=float, default=0.0)
    fade.add_argument("--fade-out", type=float, default=0.0)
    fade.add_argument("--out", type=Path)
    fade.add_argument("--overwrite", action="store_true")

    merge = sub.add_parser("merge", help="concatenate two or more audio files")
    merge.add_argument("inputs", nargs="+", type=Path)
    merge.add_argument("--out", type=Path)
    merge.add_argument("--overwrite", action="store_true")

    normalize = sub.add_parser("normalize", help="normalize loudness to a target LUFS")
    normalize.add_argument("inputs", nargs="+", type=Path)
    normalize.add_argument("--target", type=float, default=-14.0)
    normalize.add_argument("--outdir", type=Path)
    normalize.add_argument("--overwrite", action="store_true")
    return parser.parse_args()


def probe_duration(ffmpeg: Path, audio: Path) -> float | None:
    result = subprocess.run(
        [str(ffmpeg), "-hide_banner", "-i", str(audio)],
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        encoding="utf-8",
        errors="replace",
    )
    match = re.search(r"Duration:\s*(\d+):(\d+):(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)", result.stderr)
    if not match:
        return None
    hours, minutes, seconds = match.groups()
    return int(hours) * 3600 + int(minutes) * 60 + float(seconds)


def unique_output(path: Path) -> Path:
    if not path.exists():
        return path
    stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S")
    return path.with_name(f"{path.stem}_{stamp}{path.suffix}")


def audio_codec_args(path: Path) -> list[str]:
    ext = path.suffix.lower()
    if ext == ".mp3":
        return ["-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-b:a", "320k", "-ar", "48000"]
    if ext == ".flac":
        return ["-c:a", "flac"]
    return ["-c:a", "pcm_s16le"]


def run_ffmpeg(ffmpeg: Path, command: list[str]) -> int:
    result = subprocess.run([str(ffmpeg), "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", *command])
    return result.returncode


def fade(args: argparse.Namespace, ffmpeg: Path) -> int:
    if not args.input.is_file():
        print(f"[error] missing input: {args.input}")
        return 2
    if args.fade_in <= 0 and args.fade_out <= 0:
        print("[error] fade-in and fade-out are both zero")
        return 2
    duration = probe_duration(ffmpeg, args.input)
    if not duration:
        print(f"[error] cannot read duration: {args.input}")
        return 2

    fade_in = max(0.0, min(args.fade_in, duration))
    fade_out = max(0.0, min(args.fade_out, duration))
    filters = []
    if fade_in > 0:
        filters.append(f"afade=t=in:st=0:d={fade_in:.3f}")
    if fade_out > 0:
        start = max(0.0, duration - fade_out)
        filters.append(f"afade=t=out:st={start:.3f}:d={fade_out:.3f}")

    if args.out is not None:
        output = args.out
    else:
        output = args.input.with_name(f"{args.input.stem}_fade{args.input.suffix}")
        output = unique_output(output)
    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    command = [
        "-y" if args.overwrite else "-n",
        "-i",
        str(args.input),
        "-af",
        ",".join(filters),
        "-map_metadata",
        "0",
        *audio_codec_args(output),
        str(output),
    ]
    code = run_ffmpeg(ffmpeg, command)
    if code != 0:
        print(f"[error] ffmpeg exit={code}")
        return code
    print(f"[done] {output}")
    return 0


def merge(args: argparse.Namespace, ffmpeg: Path) -> int:
    inputs = [path for path in args.inputs if path.is_file()]
    if len(inputs) < 2:
        print("[error] merge needs at least two existing audio files")
        return 2
    if args.out is not None:
        output = args.out
    else:
        output = inputs[0].with_name(
            f"merged_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.mp3")
    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    parts = []
    labels = []
    for index in range(len(inputs)):
        parts.append(
            f"[{index}:a]aresample=48000,"
            f"aformat=sample_fmts=fltp:channel_layouts=stereo[a{index}]")
        labels.append(f"[a{index}]")
    filter_graph = ";".join(parts) + ";" + "".join(labels) + \
        f"concat=n={len(inputs)}:v=0:a=1[out]"

    command = ["-y" if args.overwrite else "-n"]
    for path in inputs:
        command += ["-i", str(path)]
    command += [
        "-filter_complex",
        filter_graph,
        "-map",
        "[out]",
        "-map_metadata",
        "0",
        *audio_codec_args(output),
        str(output),
    ]
    code = run_ffmpeg(ffmpeg, command)
    if code != 0:
        print(f"[error] ffmpeg exit={code}")
        return code
    print(f"[done] {output}")
    return 0


def normalize(args: argparse.Namespace, ffmpeg: Path) -> int:
    inputs = [path for path in args.inputs if path.is_file()]
    if not inputs:
        print("[error] no existing input files")
        return 2
    if args.outdir is not None:
        args.outdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    failed = 0
    for source in inputs:
        output_dir = args.outdir if args.outdir is not None else source.parent
        output = output_dir / f"{source.stem}_norm{source.suffix}"
        if not args.overwrite:
            output = unique_output(output)
        output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        command = [
            "-y" if args.overwrite else "-n",
            "-i",
            str(source),
            "-af",
            f"loudnorm=I={args.target:.1f}:TP=-1.5:LRA=11",
            "-ar",
            "48000",
            "-map_metadata",
            "0",
            *audio_codec_args(output),
            str(output),
        ]
        code = run_ffmpeg(ffmpeg, command)
        if code == 0:
            print(f"[done] {output}")
        else:
            print(f"[error] ffmpeg exit={code}: {source}")
            failed += 1
    return 0 if failed == 0 else 1


def main() -> int:
    safe_stdio()
    args = parse_args()
    ffmpeg = args.ffmpeg
    if not ffmpeg.is_file():
        print(f"[error] ffmpeg not found: {ffmpeg}")
        return 2
    if args.command == "fade":
        return fade(args, ffmpeg)
    if args.command == "normalize":
        return normalize(args, ffmpeg)
    return merge(args, ffmpeg)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())


===== [43/66] AI_Music/scripts/check_dependencies.py  (6298 bytes) =====
"""Report the third-party dependency closure of the ACE-Step inference path.

Scans the official repo source (excluding UI / API server / training code) with
`ast`, then checks which of the imported modules are importable in the current
environment.  Run it with the project venv python:

    <workspace>\\AI_Music.venv\\Scripts\\python.exe <workspace>\\AI_Music\\scripts\\check_dependencies.py

<workspace> = G:\\AIMUSIC.  All paths are resolved from this
file's location, so the project folder can be moved without editing the script.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import ast
import importlib.util
import sys
from pathlib import Path

# <workspace>\AI_Music\scripts\check_dependencies.py -> <workspace>\AI_Music
BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
REPO_DIR = BASE_DIR / "ACE-Step-1.5"
PACKAGE_DIR = REPO_DIR / "acestep"

# Directories that belong to the WebUI / REST server / training stacks, which this
# offline inference-only setup intentionally does not install.
SKIP_DIR_PARTS = {"ui", "api", "training", "training_v2", "third_parts", "mlx"}
SKIP_FILE_NAMES = {
    "acestep_v15_pipeline.py",  # Gradio web UI entry point (not used)
    "api_server.py",            # REST API server entry point (not used)
    "openrouter_models.py",
    "openrouter_adapter.py",
}
SKIP_NAME_PARTS = ("_test.py", "test_", "_support.py")


def _is_skipped(path: Path) -> bool:
    if any(part in SKIP_DIR_PARTS for part in path.relative_to(REPO_DIR).parts[:-1]):
        return True
    if path.name in SKIP_FILE_NAMES:
        return True
    return any(part in path.name for part in SKIP_NAME_PARTS)


def _local_modules() -> set[str]:
    modules = {"acestep"}
    for path in PACKAGE_DIR.rglob("*.py"):
        modules.add(path.stem)
    for path in PACKAGE_DIR.iterdir():
        if path.is_dir():
            modules.add(path.name)
    return modules


def _record(imports: dict, name: str, filename: str, guarded: bool) -> None:
    entry = imports.setdefault(name, {"files": set(), "guarded": guarded})
    entry["files"].add(filename)
    if not guarded:
        entry["guarded"] = False


def _scan_node(node: ast.AST, guarded: bool, imports: dict, filename: str) -> None:
    """Collect imports that run at module import time.

    Imports nested in functions/classes are lazy (vLLM backend, MLX, LoRA, ...)
    and are skipped.  Imports inside ``try:`` are marked "guarded" because the
    surrounding code handles their absence.
    """
    if isinstance(node, (ast.Import, ast.ImportFrom)):
        if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
            names = [alias.name.split(".")[0] for alias in node.names]
        elif node.module and not node.level:
            names = [node.module.split(".")[0]]
        else:
            names = []
        for name in names:
            _record(imports, name, filename, guarded)
    elif isinstance(node, ast.Try):
        for child in node.body:
            _scan_node(child, guarded, imports, filename)
        for handler in node.handlers:
            for child in handler.body:
                _scan_node(child, True, imports, filename)
        for child in list(node.orelse) + list(node.finalbody):
            _scan_node(child, guarded, imports, filename)
    elif isinstance(node, ast.If):
        test = node.test
        if (isinstance(test, ast.Name) and test.id == "TYPE_CHECKING") or (
            isinstance(test, ast.Attribute) and test.attr == "TYPE_CHECKING"
        ):
            return
        for child in list(node.body) + list(node.orelse):
            _scan_node(child, guarded, imports, filename)
    elif isinstance(node, (ast.With, ast.AsyncWith)):
        for child in node.body:
            _scan_node(child, guarded, imports, filename)


def collect_imports() -> dict:
    imports: dict = {}
    for path in sorted(PACKAGE_DIR.rglob("*.py")):
        if _is_skipped(path):
            continue
        try:
            tree = ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore"))
        except SyntaxError:
            continue
        for statement in tree.body:
            _scan_node(statement, False, imports, path.name)
    return imports


def main() -> int:
    if not PACKAGE_DIR.is_dir():
        print(f"[deps] repo source not found: {PACKAGE_DIR}")
        return 1

    stdlib = set(getattr(sys, "stdlib_module_names", set()))
    local = _local_modules()
    imports = collect_imports()

    third_party = {
        name: info
        for name, info in imports.items()
        if name not in stdlib and name not in local and not name.startswith("_")
    }

    def probe(name: str) -> bool:
        try:
            return importlib.util.find_spec(name) is not None
        except Exception:
            return False

    required_ok: list[str] = []
    required_missing: list[str] = []
    optional_ok: list[str] = []
    optional_missing: list[str] = []
    for name in sorted(third_party):
        if third_party[name]["guarded"]:
            bucket = (optional_ok, optional_missing)
        else:
            bucket = (required_ok, required_missing)
        bucket[0 if probe(name) else 1].append(name)

    total_required = len(required_ok) + len(required_missing)
    print(f"[deps] repo      : {REPO_DIR}")
    print(f"[deps] python    : {sys.executable}")
    print(f"[deps] required  : {len(required_ok)}/{total_required} importable")
    for name in required_ok:
        print(f"[deps]   OK      {name}")
    for name in required_missing:
        files = ", ".join(sorted(third_party[name]["files"]))[:80]
        print(f"[deps]   MISSING {name}  <- {files}")
    print(f"[deps] optional  : {len(optional_ok)} present, {len(optional_missing)} absent "
          f"(try/except or platform guarded)")
    for name in optional_ok:
        print(f"[deps]   present {name}")
    for name in optional_missing:
        print(f"[deps]   absent  {name}")
    if required_missing:
        print("[deps] result: INCOMPLETE - install the required modules listed above")
        return 2
    print("[deps] result: OK - inference dependency closure satisfied")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())


===== [44/66] AI_Music/scripts/check_lock.py  (1403 bytes) =====
"""Compare installed package versions against requirements_lock.txt."""

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.metadata
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

BASE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
LOCK = BASE / "requirements_lock.txt"


def main() -> int:
    if not LOCK.is_file():
        print(f"[error] missing {LOCK}")
        return 1
    mismatches = []
    checked = 0
    for line in LOCK.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        line = line.strip()
        if not line or line.startswith("#") or line.startswith("-"):
            continue
        match = re.fullmatch(r"([A-Za-z0-9_.-]+)==([^\s]+)", line)
        if not match:
            continue
        name, expected = match.groups()
        checked += 1
        try:
            actual = importlib.metadata.version(name)
        except importlib.metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
            mismatches.append(f"{name}: missing (expected {expected})")
            continue
        if actual != expected:
            mismatches.append(f"{name}: {actual} != {expected}")
    if mismatches:
        print("[error] requirements lock mismatch")
        for item in mismatches:
            print("  " + item)
        return 1
    print(f"[ok] requirements lock matched ({checked} packages)")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


===== [45/66] AI_Music/scripts/download_ace_models.py  (5433 bytes) =====
"""Download the official ACE-Step 1.5 model files from Hugging Face.

Offline / local setup helper (no WebUI, no API server).

* Repo  : ACE-Step/Ace-Step1.5  (official)
* Target: <workspace>\\AI_Music\\models\\Ace-Step1.5  (acts as ACESTEP_CHECKPOINTS_DIR)
* Engine: huggingface_hub + hf_xet (Xet backend, resumable)
* Safety: retries on network errors, skips files that are already complete.

Usage:
    <workspace>\\AI_Music\\tools\\hfvenv\\Scripts\\python.exe <workspace>\\AI_Music\\scripts\\download_ace_models.py

<workspace> = G:\\AIMUSIC.  All paths are resolved from this
file's location, so the project folder can be moved without editing the script.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

REPO_ID = "ACE-Step/Ace-Step1.5"
# <workspace>\AI_Music\scripts\download_ace_models.py -> <workspace>\AI_Music
BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
TARGET_DIR = BASE_DIR / "models" / "Ace-Step1.5"
HF_HOME = BASE_DIR / ".hf-cache"

# Only the files that the local inference pipeline needs (turbo DiT + 1.7B LM +
# text encoder + VAE + configs/tokenizers). No XL / no 4B / no alternatives.
ALLOW_PATTERNS = [
    "*.json",
    "*.py",
    "*.jinja",
    "*.txt",
    ".gitattributes",
    "README.md",
    "vae/*.json",
    "vae/*.safetensors",
    "Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B/*",
    "acestep-v15-turbo/*",
    "acestep-5Hz-lm-1.7B/*",
]

EXPECTED_FILES = [
    "config.json",
    "vae/config.json",
    "vae/diffusion_pytorch_model.safetensors",
    "Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B/config.json",
    "Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B/model.safetensors",
    "acestep-v15-turbo/config.json",
    "acestep-v15-turbo/model.safetensors",
    "acestep-v15-turbo/silence_latent.pt",
    "acestep-5Hz-lm-1.7B/config.json",
    "acestep-5Hz-lm-1.7B/model.safetensors",
]

MAX_RETRIES = 40


def _human(num_bytes: float) -> str:
    for unit in ("B", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB"):
        if abs(num_bytes) < 1024.0:
            return f"{num_bytes:3.1f}{unit}"
        num_bytes /= 1024.0
    return f"{num_bytes:.1f}PB"


def _current_size() -> float:
    total = 0
    for path in TARGET_DIR.rglob("*"):
        try:
            if path.is_file():
                total += path.stat().st_size
        except OSError:
            pass
    return float(total)


def _configure_env() -> None:
    # This repository is stored on the HF "Xet" backend.  The classic HTTP
    # downloader is capped at ~300 KB/s for anonymous clients and hf_transfer
    # cannot handle Xet at all, so the Xet engine (package: hf_xet) is used with
    # high-performance multi-connection chunking.
    os.environ.pop("HF_HUB_ENABLE_HF_TRANSFER", None)
    os.environ.pop("HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET", None)
    os.environ["HF_XET_HIGH_PERFORMANCE"] = "1"
    os.environ["HF_HOME"] = str(HF_HOME)
    os.environ["HF_HUB_DISABLE_TELEMETRY"] = "1"
    os.environ.pop("HF_HUB_DOWNLOAD_TIMEOUT", None)


def main() -> int:
    _configure_env()
    TARGET_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    from huggingface_hub import snapshot_download

    print(f"[download] repo        : {REPO_ID}", flush=True)
    print(f"[download] target dir  : {TARGET_DIR}", flush=True)
    print(f"[download] hf home     : {HF_HOME}", flush=True)
    try:
        import hf_xet  # noqa: F401
        engine = "hf_xet (Xet backend)"
    except ImportError:
        engine = "plain HTTP (hf_xet not installed)"
    print(f"[download] engine      : {engine}", flush=True)
    print(f"[download] already on disk: {_human(_current_size())}", flush=True)

    last_error: Exception | None = None
    for attempt in range(1, MAX_RETRIES + 1):
        started = time.time()
        before = _current_size()
        try:
            snapshot_download(
                repo_id=REPO_ID,
                local_dir=str(TARGET_DIR),
                allow_patterns=ALLOW_PATTERNS,
                max_workers=8,
                resume_download=True,
            )
            break
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - network errors are expected here
            last_error = exc
            after = _current_size()
            elapsed = max(time.time() - started, 0.001)
            print(
                f"[download] attempt {attempt}/{MAX_RETRIES} failed after {elapsed:.0f}s "
                f"(+{_human(after - before)}, rate {(after - before) / elapsed / 1024:.0f} KB/s): "
                f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
                flush=True,
            )
            time.sleep(min(5 * attempt, 30))
    else:
        print(f"[download] FAILED after {MAX_RETRIES} attempts: {last_error}", flush=True)
        return 1

    print(f"[download] snapshot complete: {_human(_current_size())}", flush=True)
    missing = [rel for rel in EXPECTED_FILES if not (TARGET_DIR / rel).exists()]
    if missing:
        print("[download] MISSING FILES:", flush=True)
        for rel in missing:
            print(f"  - {rel}", flush=True)
        return 2

    print("[download] all required files present:", flush=True)
    for rel in EXPECTED_FILES:
        size = (TARGET_DIR / rel).stat().st_size
        print(f"  - {_human(size):>10}  {rel}", flush=True)
    print(f"[download] TOTAL: {_human(_current_size())}", flush=True)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())


===== [46/66] AI_Music/scripts/download_ace_models_parallel.py  (16673 bytes) =====
"""Parallel (aria2-style) downloader for the official ACE-Step 1.5 files.

Why this exists
---------------
The official Hugging Face clients are unusable on this machine:
  * plain HTTP downloader  -> capped at ~300 KB/s for anonymous clients
  * hf_transfer            -> fails on Xet-backed repos
  * hf_xet (Xet engine)    -> stalls with 0 bytes on this network
This script fetches the *same official files* directly over HTTPS with many
parallel byte-range requests, supports resume, and verifies the SHA256 that the
Hugging Face API publishes for every LFS file.

Usage:
    python download_ace_models_parallel.py                 # download everything
    python download_ace_models_parallel.py --limit-mb 128  # speed benchmark only
    python download_ace_models_parallel.py --workers 16
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import fnmatch
import hashlib
import json
import shutil
import sys
import threading
import time
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

import requests

REPO_ID = "ACE-Step/Ace-Step1.5"
# <workspace>\AI_Music\scripts\download_ace_models_parallel.py -> <workspace>\AI_Music
BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
TARGET_DIR = BASE_DIR / "models" / "Ace-Step1.5"
API_URL = f"https://huggingface.co/api/models/{REPO_ID}?blobs=true"
RESOLVE_BASE = f"https://huggingface.co/{REPO_ID}/resolve/main"
PARTS_DIR = TARGET_DIR / ".parts"
VERIFIED_FILE = TARGET_DIR / ".verified.json"
CHUNK_SIZE = 8 * 1024 * 1024
READ_BLOCK = 1024 * 1024
CHUNK_RETRIES = 12

# Only the official files this offline setup needs: turbo DiT + 1.7B LM +
# Qwen3 text encoder + VAE + their configs/tokenizers.
ALLOW_PATTERNS = [
    ".gitattributes",
    "README.md",
    "*.json",
    "*.py",
    "*.jinja",
    "*.txt",
    "vae/*",
    "Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B/*",
    "acestep-v15-turbo/*",
    "acestep-5Hz-lm-1.7B/*",
]

_local = threading.local()


def _session() -> requests.Session:
    session = getattr(_local, "session", None)
    if session is None:
        session = requests.Session()
        session.headers.update({"User-Agent": "acestep-local-downloader/1.0"})
        _local.session = session
    return session


def _human(num: float) -> str:
    for unit in ("B", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB"):
        if abs(num) < 1024.0:
            return f"{num:6.1f}{unit}"
        num /= 1024.0
    return f"{num:.1f}PB"


class Progress:
    """Thread-safe byte counter with a background reporter."""

    def __init__(self, total_bytes: int, label: str) -> None:
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._done = 0
        self._total = total_bytes
        self._label = label
        self._start = time.time()
        self._stop = threading.Event()
        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._report, daemon=True)
        self._thread.start()

    def add(self, num: int) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            self._done += num

    def _report(self) -> None:
        while not self._stop.wait(10):
            with self._lock:
                done = self._done
            elapsed = max(time.time() - self._start, 0.001)
            rate = done / elapsed
            if self._total > 0 and rate > 0:
                tail = f" ETA {(self._total - done) / rate / 60:5.1f} min"
            else:
                tail = ""
            print(
                f"[{self._label}] {_human(float(done))}/{_human(float(self._total))} "
                f"({100.0 * done / max(self._total, 1):5.1f}%) {rate / 1024:6.0f} KB/s{tail}",
                flush=True,
            )

    def finish(self) -> None:
        self._stop.set()
        self._thread.join(timeout=1)


def _list_repo_files(patterns: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> List[Dict]:
    selected_patterns = patterns or ALLOW_PATTERNS
    response = _session().get(API_URL, timeout=60)
    response.raise_for_status()
    siblings = response.json().get("siblings", [])
    selected = []
    for entry in siblings:
        rel = entry.get("rfilename", "")
        if any(fnmatch.fnmatch(rel, pattern) for pattern in selected_patterns):
            selected.append(entry)
    selected.sort(key=lambda e: -int(e.get("size") or 0))
    return selected


def _fetch_chunk(url: str, start: int, end: int, part_path: Path) -> int:
    expected = end - start + 1
    last_error: Optional[Exception] = None
    for attempt in range(1, CHUNK_RETRIES + 1):
        try:
            response = _session().get(
                url,
                headers={"Range": f"bytes={start}-{end}"},
                stream=True,
                timeout=(20, 120),
            )
            if response.status_code == 416:
                raise RuntimeError(f"range rejected ({start}-{end})")
            if response.status_code == 200:
                raise RuntimeError("server ignored Range header")
            response.raise_for_status()
            tmp_path = part_path.with_suffix(".tmp")
            written = 0
            with open(tmp_path, "wb") as handle:
                for block in response.iter_content(READ_BLOCK):
                    if block:
                        handle.write(block)
                        written += len(block)
            if written != expected:
                raise RuntimeError(f"short read {written}/{expected}")
            tmp_path.replace(part_path)
            return written
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - network flakiness is expected
            last_error = exc
            time.sleep(min(3 * attempt, 90))
    raise RuntimeError(f"chunk {start}-{end} failed after {CHUNK_RETRIES} tries: {last_error}")


def _sha256_of(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as handle:
        for block in iter(lambda: handle.read(4 * 1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(block)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _load_verified() -> dict:
    try:
        data = json.loads(VERIFIED_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if isinstance(data, dict) and isinstance(data.get("files"), dict):
            return data
    except Exception:
        pass
    return {"version": 1, "files": {}}


def _save_verified(data: dict) -> None:
    VERIFIED_FILE.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    tmp = VERIFIED_FILE.with_suffix(".tmp")
    tmp.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8")
    tmp.replace(VERIFIED_FILE)


def _record_verified(rel_path: str, path: Path, sha256: str) -> None:
    data = _load_verified()
    stat = path.stat()
    data["files"][rel_path] = {
        "size": int(stat.st_size),
        "mtime": int(stat.st_mtime),
        "sha256": sha256.lower(),
        "verified_at": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S"),
    }
    _save_verified(data)


def _marker_matches(rel_path: str, size: int, sha256: str) -> bool:
    final_path = TARGET_DIR / rel_path
    if not final_path.is_file():
        return False
    entry = _load_verified()["files"].get(rel_path)
    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
        return False
    stat = final_path.stat()
    return (
        int(entry.get("size", -1)) == size
        and int(entry.get("size", -1)) == int(stat.st_size)
        and int(entry.get("mtime", -1)) == int(stat.st_mtime)
        and str(entry.get("sha256", "")).lower() == sha256.lower()
    )


def _already_complete(final_path: Path, size: int, sha256: Optional[str], rel_path: str) -> bool:
    if not final_path.exists() or final_path.stat().st_size != size:
        return False
    if not sha256:
        return True
    if _marker_matches(rel_path, size, sha256):
        return True
    if _sha256_of(final_path) == sha256:
        _record_verified(rel_path, final_path, sha256)
        return True
    return False


def _download_big_file(
    rel_path: str,
    size: int,
    sha256: Optional[str],
    workers: int,
    note: str,
    limit_bytes: Optional[int],
) -> Tuple[bool, int]:
    final_path = TARGET_DIR / rel_path
    url = f"{RESOLVE_BASE}/{rel_path}"
    effective_size = min(size, limit_bytes) if limit_bytes else size

    part_key = rel_path.replace("/", "__")
    part_dir = PARTS_DIR / f"{part_key}__{effective_size}__{CHUNK_SIZE // (1024 * 1024)}MB"
    part_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    chunks: List[Tuple[int, int, Path]] = []
    offset = 0
    index = 0
    while offset < effective_size:
        end = min(offset + CHUNK_SIZE, effective_size) - 1
        chunks.append((offset, end, part_dir / f"{index:05d}.part"))
        offset = end + 1
        index += 1

    pending = []
    for start, end, part_path in chunks:
        expected = end - start + 1
        if part_path.exists() and part_path.stat().st_size == expected:
            continue
        pending.append((start, end, part_path))

    print(
        f"[file] {rel_path}: {_human(float(size))}, {len(chunks)} chunks, "
        f"{len(pending)} to fetch{note}",
        flush=True,
    )

    progress = Progress(effective_size, rel_path.split("/")[0])
    progress.add(effective_size - sum(end - start + 1 for start, end, _ in pending))

    pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=workers)
    try:
        futures = {
            pool.submit(_fetch_chunk, url, start, end, part_path): (start, end, part_path)
            for start, end, part_path in pending
        }
        for future in as_completed(futures):
            start, end, part_path = futures[future]
            written = future.result()
            progress.add(written)
        pool.shutdown(wait=True)
    except Exception:
        print(f"[file] {rel_path}: cancelling queued chunks after error", flush=True)
        pool.shutdown(wait=False, cancel_futures=True)
        raise
    finally:
        progress.finish()

    if limit_bytes:
        print(f"[file] {rel_path}: benchmark stopped at {_human(float(effective_size))}", flush=True)
        return False, effective_size

    tmp_final = final_path.with_suffix(final_path.suffix + ".assembling")
    final_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(tmp_final, "wb") as out:
        for _, _, part_path in chunks:
            with open(part_path, "rb") as part:
                shutil.copyfileobj(part, out, 4 * 1024 * 1024)
    tmp_final.replace(final_path)
    shutil.rmtree(part_dir, ignore_errors=True)

    if sha256:
        actual = _sha256_of(final_path)
        if actual != sha256:
            final_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            print(f"[file] {rel_path}: SHA256 MISMATCH (got {actual})", flush=True)
            return False, effective_size
        _record_verified(rel_path, final_path, sha256)
        print(f"[file] {rel_path}: SHA256 verified", flush=True)
    return True, effective_size


def _download_small_file(rel_path: str, size: int) -> Tuple[bool, int]:
    final_path = TARGET_DIR / rel_path
    final_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    url = f"{RESOLVE_BASE}/{rel_path}"
    for attempt in range(1, 6):
        try:
            response = _session().get(url, stream=True, timeout=(20, 120))
            response.raise_for_status()
            data = response.content
            with open(final_path, "wb") as handle:
                handle.write(data)
            return True, len(data)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            print(f"[file] {rel_path}: attempt {attempt} failed: {exc}", flush=True)
            time.sleep(3 * attempt)
    return False, 0


def _parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=16, help="parallel range connections per file")
    parser.add_argument("--limit-mb", type=int, default=0, help="benchmark: only fetch this many MB per file")
    parser.add_argument("--passes", type=int, default=50, help="sweeps over the file list (unattended retry)")
    parser.add_argument("--pass-delay", type=int, default=120, help="seconds to wait between sweeps")
    parser.add_argument("--chunk-mb", type=int, default=8, help="range chunk size in MB (bigger = fewer requests)")
    parser.add_argument("--only", action="append", default=None,
                        help="only fetch files matching this glob (repeatable)")
    return parser.parse_args()


def _attempt_pass(
    files: List[Dict], args: argparse.Namespace, limit_bytes: Optional[int]
) -> Tuple[int, List[str], List[str]]:
    done_bytes = 0
    failed: List[str] = []
    skipped: List[str] = []
    total_size = sum(int(entry.get("size") or 0) for entry in files)
    print(f"[main] sweep over {len(files)} files, {_human(float(total_size))} total", flush=True)

    for rank, entry in enumerate(files, start=1):
        rel_path = entry["rfilename"]
        size = int(entry.get("size") or 0)
        sha256 = (entry.get("lfs") or {}).get("sha256")
        final_path = TARGET_DIR / rel_path

        if not limit_bytes and _already_complete(final_path, size, sha256, rel_path):
            print(f"[{rank}/{len(files)}] {rel_path}: already present ({_human(float(size))})", flush=True)
            skipped.append(rel_path)
            done_bytes += size
            continue

        note = " (sha256 verified)" if sha256 and not limit_bytes else ""
        t0 = time.time()
        try:
            if size >= CHUNK_SIZE:
                ok, got = _download_big_file(
                    rel_path, size, sha256, args.workers, note, limit_bytes
                )
            else:
                ok, got = _download_small_file(rel_path, size)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - keep sweeping the remaining files
            ok, got = False, 0
            print(
                f"[{rank}/{len(files)}] {rel_path}: ERROR {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
                flush=True,
            )

        elapsed = max(time.time() - t0, 0.001)
        print(
            f"[{rank}/{len(files)}] {rel_path}: {'OK' if ok else 'INCOMPLETE'} "
            f"{_human(float(got))} in {elapsed:.0f}s ({got / elapsed / 1024:.0f} KB/s){note}",
            flush=True,
        )
        if ok:
            done_bytes += got
        elif not limit_bytes:
            failed.append(rel_path)

    return done_bytes, failed, skipped


def main() -> int:
    global CHUNK_SIZE
    args = _parse_args()
    CHUNK_SIZE = max(1, args.chunk_mb) * 1024 * 1024
    limit_bytes = args.limit_mb * 1024 * 1024 if args.limit_mb > 0 else None
    TARGET_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    files = _list_repo_files(args.only)
    if not files:
        print("[main] no files selected", flush=True)
        return 1

    print(f"[main] repo={REPO_ID}", flush=True)
    print(f"[main] target={TARGET_DIR}", flush=True)
    print(f"[main] workers={args.workers} passes={args.passes} "
          f"pass_delay={args.pass_delay}s", flush=True)

    grand_start = time.time()
    failed: List[str] = []
    for pass_index in range(1, args.passes + 1):
        print(f"[main] === pass {pass_index}/{args.passes} ===", flush=True)
        done_bytes, failed, skipped = _attempt_pass(files, args, limit_bytes)
        print(
            f"[main] pass {pass_index}: {len(skipped)} complete, {len(failed)} pending, "
            f"{_human(float(done_bytes))} accounted",
            flush=True,
        )
        if limit_bytes:
            print("[main] benchmark mode: nothing was assembled or verified", flush=True)
            return 0
        if not failed:
            elapsed = max(time.time() - grand_start, 0.001)
            on_disk = sum(
                p.stat().st_size
                for p in TARGET_DIR.rglob("*")
                if p.is_file() and PARTS_DIR not in p.parents
            )
            print(f"[main] ALL FILES COMPLETE in {elapsed / 60:.1f} min", flush=True)
            print(f"[main] on-disk total: {_human(float(on_disk))}", flush=True)
            return 0
        if pass_index < args.passes:
            print(
                f"[main] sleeping {args.pass_delay}s before the next sweep; pending: {failed}",
                flush=True,
            )
            time.sleep(args.pass_delay)

    print(f"[main] gave up after {args.passes} passes; still pending: {failed}", flush=True)
    return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())


===== [47/66] AI_Music/scripts/fetch_file.py  (7316 bytes) =====
"""Fetch one large file with many parallel HTTP range requests.

Generic, resumable, sha256-verifying downloader used for the official PyTorch
CUDA wheels (the link on this machine is heavily rate limited per connection, so
single-stream downloaders stall).

Usage:
    python fetch_file.py --url <url> --out <path> [--sha256 <hex>] [--workers 24]

Part files live in "<out>.parts" so an interrupted run resumes where it stopped.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import shutil
import sys
import threading
import time
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Optional, Tuple

import requests

CHUNK_SIZE = 8 * 1024 * 1024
READ_BLOCK = 1024 * 1024
RETRIES = 8
_local = threading.local()


def human(num: float) -> str:
    for unit in ("B", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB"):
        if abs(num) < 1024.0:
            return f"{num:.2f}{unit}"
        num /= 1024.0
    return f"{num:.2f}PB"


def session() -> requests.Session:
    client = getattr(_local, "client", None)
    if client is None:
        client = requests.Session()
        client.headers.update({"User-Agent": "acestep-local-fetch/1.0"})
        _local.client = client
    return client


def remote_size(url: str) -> int:
    response = session().head(url, allow_redirects=True, timeout=(20, 60))
    response.raise_for_status()
    length = response.headers.get("Content-Length")
    if length is None:
        raise RuntimeError("server did not report Content-Length")
    return int(length)


def fetch_chunk(url: str, start: int, end: int, part_path: Path) -> int:
    expected = end - start + 1
    last_error: Optional[Exception] = None
    for attempt in range(1, RETRIES + 1):
        try:
            response = session().get(
                url, headers={"Range": f"bytes={start}-{end}"}, stream=True, timeout=(20, 120)
            )
            if response.status_code == 200:
                raise RuntimeError("server ignored Range header")
            response.raise_for_status()
            tmp_path = part_path.with_suffix(".tmp")
            written = 0
            with open(tmp_path, "wb") as handle:
                for block in response.iter_content(READ_BLOCK):
                    if block:
                        handle.write(block)
                        written += len(block)
            if written != expected:
                raise RuntimeError(f"short read {written}/{expected}")
            tmp_path.replace(part_path)
            return written
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - flaky link, retry
            last_error = exc
            time.sleep(min(2 * attempt, 15))
    raise RuntimeError(f"chunk {start}-{end} failed after {RETRIES} tries: {last_error}")


def sha256_of(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as handle:
        for block in iter(lambda: handle.read(4 * 1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(block)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--url", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--sha256", default=None)
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=24)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    out_path = Path(args.out)
    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    if out_path.is_file():
        if args.sha256 and sha256_of(out_path) == args.sha256:
            print(f"[fetch] {out_path.name}: already present and sha256 verified")
            return 0
        if not args.sha256 and out_path.stat().st_size == remote_size(args.url):
            print(f"[fetch] {out_path.name}: already present ({human(float(out_path.stat().st_size))})")
            return 0

    size = remote_size(args.url)
    chunk_mb = CHUNK_SIZE // (1024 * 1024)
    part_dir = out_path.with_name(f"{out_path.name}.{size}.{chunk_mb}mb.parts")
    part_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    print(f"[fetch] parts dir     : {part_dir}", flush=True)
    print(f"[fetch] {out_path.name}: {human(float(size))} <- {args.url}", flush=True)

    chunks: List[Tuple[int, int, Path]] = []
    offset, index = 0, 0
    while offset < size:
        end = min(offset + CHUNK_SIZE, size) - 1
        chunks.append((offset, end, part_dir / f"{index:05d}.part"))
        offset = end + 1
        index += 1

    pending = [
        item
        for item in chunks
        if not (item[2].is_file() and item[2].stat().st_size == item[1] - item[0] + 1)
    ]
    done_bytes = sum(end - start + 1 for start, end, _ in chunks) - sum(
        end - start + 1 for start, end, _ in pending
    )
    print(f"[fetch] {len(chunks)} chunks, {len(pending)} to fetch, "
          f"{human(float(done_bytes))} already on disk", flush=True)

    started = time.time()
    lock = threading.Lock()
    counter = {"done": float(done_bytes)}
    stop = threading.Event()

    def reporter() -> None:
        while not stop.wait(15):
            with lock:
                done = counter["done"]
            elapsed = max(time.time() - started, 0.001)
            rate = (done - done_bytes) / elapsed
            eta = (size - done) / rate / 60 if rate > 0 else 0.0
            print(f"[fetch] {human(done)}/{human(float(size))} "
                  f"({100.0 * done / size:5.1f}%) {rate / 1024:6.0f} KB/s ETA {eta:5.1f} min",
                  flush=True)

    report_thread = threading.Thread(target=reporter, daemon=True)
    report_thread.start()
    pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.workers)
    try:
        futures = {
            pool.submit(fetch_chunk, args.url, start, end, part_path): (start, end, part_path)
            for start, end, part_path in pending
        }
        for future in as_completed(futures):
            _, _, part_path = futures[future]
            written = future.result()
            with lock:
                counter["done"] += written
        pool.shutdown(wait=True)
    except Exception:
        print("[fetch] cancelling queued chunks after error", flush=True)
        pool.shutdown(wait=False, cancel_futures=True)
        raise
    finally:
        stop.set()
        report_thread.join(timeout=1)

    tmp_out = out_path.with_suffix(out_path.suffix + ".assembling")
    with open(tmp_out, "wb") as out:
        for _, _, part_path in chunks:
            with open(part_path, "rb") as part:
                shutil.copyfileobj(part, out, 4 * 1024 * 1024)
    tmp_out.replace(out_path)
    shutil.rmtree(part_dir, ignore_errors=True)

    elapsed = max(time.time() - started, 0.001)
    written = out_path.stat().st_size
    print(f"[fetch] assembled {human(float(written))} in {elapsed / 60:.1f} min "
          f"({written / elapsed / 1024:.0f} KB/s)")

    if args.sha256:
        actual = sha256_of(out_path)
        if actual != args.sha256:
            out_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            print(f"[fetch] SHA256 MISMATCH: got {actual}")
            return 1
        print("[fetch] SHA256 verified")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

"""Generate the fixed 3-minute lo-fi test track via generate_backend.py.

The generation logic lives in app/generate_backend.py; this wrapper only
supplies the fixed config and mirrors the result into logs/generate_report.json
so write_setup_report.py keeps working.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

from _common import safe_stdio

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
BACKEND = BASE_DIR / "app" / "generate_backend.py"
GENERATED_DIR = BASE_DIR / "generated"
LOG_DIR = BASE_DIR / "logs"
TARGET_WAV = GENERATED_DIR / "test_lofi_01.wav"

CAPTION = (
    "Warm lo-fi chill instrumental. Dusty vinyl crackle and soft tape hiss, mellow "
    "Rhodes electric piano chords, gentle jazzy guitar licks, laid-back dusty "
    "hip-hop drum groove with brushed snare and soft kick, warm rounded upright "
    "bass line, relaxed swing feel, nostalgic late-night study atmosphere, "
    "instrumental only, no vocals."
)

CONFIG = {
    "caption": CAPTION,
    "lyrics": "[Instrumental]",
    "instrumental": True,
    "thinking": True,
    "bpm": 78,
    "keyscale": "C Major",
    "timesignature": "4",
    "vocal_language": "unknown",
    "duration": 180,
    "inference_steps": 8,
    "guidance_scale": 1.0,
    "seed": 20260928,
    "output_dir": str(GENERATED_DIR),
    "filename_prefix": "test_lofi",
    "audio_format": "wav",
    "offload_mode": "auto",
}


def main() -> int:
    safe_stdio()
    LOG_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    GENERATED_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    config_path = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / "aimusic_generate_test.json"
    config_path.write_text(json.dumps(CONFIG, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

    produced = None
    process = subprocess.Popen(
        [sys.executable, "-u", str(BACKEND), "--config", str(config_path)],
        stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
        stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
        text=True,
        encoding="utf-8",
        errors="replace",
        bufsize=1,
    )
    assert process.stdout is not None
    for line in process.stdout:
        print(line, end="", flush=True)
        if line.startswith("[done]"):
            produced = Path(line[6:].strip())
    code = process.wait()

    audio = {}
    report = {"ok": code == 0, "stage": "done" if code == 0 else "generate", "errors": []}
    if produced is not None and produced.is_file():
        shutil.copy2(produced, TARGET_WAV)
        sidecar_path = produced.with_suffix(".json")
        if sidecar_path.is_file():
            sidecar = json.loads(sidecar_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            audio = sidecar.get("audio", {})
            audio["path"] = str(TARGET_WAV)
            report["generate_seconds"] = sidecar.get("generate_seconds")
            report["actual_seed"] = sidecar.get("actual_seed")
        report["audio"] = audio
        report["raw_output"] = str(produced)
    elif code != 0:
        report["errors"].append(f"generate_backend exit={code}")

    (LOG_DIR / "generate_report.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, default=str), encoding="utf-8")
    return 0 if code == 0 and produced is not None else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())


===== [50/66] AI_Music/scripts/gpu_lock_hold.py  (1081 bytes) =====
"""Hold the cross-process GPU lock while an external worker uses the GPU."""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path

BASE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(BASE / "app"))

from gpu_lock import GpuBusy, GpuLockError, acquire  # noqa: E402


def main() -> int:
    source = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "external"
    wait_seconds = float(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 0.0
    try:
        lock = acquire(source, wait_seconds)
    except GpuBusy as exc:
        print(f"[busy] {exc}", flush=True)
        return 75
    except GpuLockError as exc:
        print(f"[error] GPU lock error: {exc}", flush=True)
        return 75
    print(f"[locked] pid={os.getpid()} source={source}", flush=True)
    try:
        for line in sys.stdin:
            if line.strip().lower() in ("quit", "exit"):
                break
    except Exception:
        pass
    finally:
        lock.release()
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

# Install the local (offline, no-WebUI) ACE-Step 1.5 Python environment.
#
# 1. waits for the PyTorch wheel fetcher to finish
# 2. installs the three official CUDA 12.8 wheels from <project>\wheels
# 3. installs the remaining requirements (PyPI) with the wheels as a fallback source
# 4. prints the CUDA / GPU verification block
#
# Run: powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "<project>\scripts\install_env.ps1"
#      (all paths are derived from this script's location, so the project folder
#       can be moved without editing anything)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
. (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '_common.ps1')
$proj = Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot        # <workspace>\AI_Music
$base = Split-Path -Parent $proj                # <workspace> (holds AI_Music.venv)

$venvPy = Join-Path $base 'AI_Music.venv\Scripts\python.exe'
$env:UV_CACHE_DIR = Join-Path $proj '.uv-cache'

Write-Host '[install] waiting for the PyTorch wheel fetch to finish (max 120 min)...'
$deadline = (Get-Date).AddMinutes(120)
while ((Get-Date) -lt $deadline) {
    $running = Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter "Name='python.exe'" |
        Where-Object { $_.CommandLine -like '*fetch_file.py*' }
    if (-not $running) { break }
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 20
}

$wheelsDir = Join-Path $proj 'wheels'
$wheels = @(Get-ChildItem (Join-Path $wheelsDir '*.whl') -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | ForEach-Object { $_.FullName })
Write-Host "[install] found $($wheels.Count) wheel(s) in $wheelsDir"
if ($wheels.Count -lt 3) { throw "expected 3 PyTorch wheels, found $($wheels.Count)" }

Write-Host '[install] installing PyTorch wheels...'
$wheelArgs = @('pip', 'install', '--python', $venvPy) + $wheels
$code = Run-Native $uv $wheelArgs
if ($code -ne 0) { throw "PyTorch wheel install failed (exit=$code)" }

Write-Host '[install] installing remaining requirements...'
$reqArgs = @('pip', 'install', '--python', $venvPy, '-r', (Join-Path $proj 'requirements_local.txt'),
    '--index-url', 'https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cu128',
    '--extra-index-url', 'https://pypi.org/simple',
    '--index-strategy', 'unsafe-best-match',
    '--find-links', $wheelsDir)
$code = Run-Native $uv $reqArgs
if ($code -ne 0) { throw "requirements install failed (exit=$code)" }

Write-Host '[install] environment verification:'
& $venvPy -c "import sys, torch, transformers, diffusers, soundfile, einops, vector_quantize_pytorch; print('python           :', sys.version.split()[0]); print('torch            :', torch.__version__); print('torch cuda build :', torch.version.cuda); print('cuda available   :', torch.cuda.is_available()); print('gpu              :', torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)); free, total = torch.cuda.mem_get_info(); print('vram free/total  : %.2f / %.2f GB' % (free/1024**3, total/1024**3)); print('bf16 supported   :', torch.cuda.is_bf16_supported()); print('transformers     :', transformers.__version__); print('diffusers        :', diffusers.__version__)"

Write-Host '[install] DONE'


===== [52/66] AI_Music/scripts/longform_generate.py  (10608 bytes) =====
"""Generate a long track by chunked album generation and ffmpeg crossfade."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

BASE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
BACKEND = BASE / "app" / "generate_backend.py"
FFMPEG = BASE / "tools" / "ffmpeg" / "bin" / "ffmpeg.exe"
DEFAULT_OUT = BASE / "generated"


def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--config", type=Path, required=True,
                        help="base config JSON, same format as generate_backend")
    parser.add_argument("--duration", type=float,
                        help="target seconds; defaults to config duration")
    parser.add_argument("--chunk", type=float, default=180.0,
                        help="seconds per chunk (default 180)")
    parser.add_argument("--crossfade", type=float, default=5.0,
                        help="crossfade seconds (default 5)")
    parser.add_argument("--chunks-per-process", type=int, default=3,
                        help="chunks generated per backend process; "
                             "0 keeps one process for everything (default 3)")
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, help="final MP3 path")
    parser.add_argument("--keep-chunks", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--gpu-wait", type=float, default=60.0)
    parser.add_argument("--python", help="interpreter for the backend "
                                         "(default: AI_Music\\.venv if present)")
    return parser.parse_args()


def backend_python(explicit: str | None) -> str:
    if explicit:
        return explicit
    for candidate in (BASE.parent / "AI_Music.venv" / "Scripts" / "python.exe",
                      BASE / ".venv" / "Scripts" / "python.exe"):
        if candidate.is_file():
            return str(candidate)
    return sys.executable


def build_album(base: dict, target: float, chunk: float, crossfade: float,
                out_dir: Path) -> tuple[dict, int]:
    if chunk <= crossfade or chunk < 30:
        raise ValueError("chunk 必須大於 crossfade，且至少 30 秒")
    if target <= chunk:
        raise ValueError("target 必須大於單一 chunk；短曲請用一般生成")
    count = 1
    while count * chunk - (count - 1) * crossfade < target:
        count += 1
    caption = str(base.get("caption") or "").strip()
    if not caption:
        raise ValueError("config 缺少 caption")
    tracks = []
    seed = int(base.get("seed") if base.get("seed") is not None else -1)
    for index in range(1, count + 1):
        if index == 1:
            prompt = caption + ", intro, establish the theme, seamless continuation"
        elif index == count:
            prompt = caption + ", outro, resolve and fade to a close"
        else:
            prompt = caption + ", seamless continuation, no intro, no outro, same groove"
        track = {
            "title": f"Part {index:02d}",
            "prompt": prompt,
            "duration": chunk,
        }
        if seed >= 0:
            track["seed"] = seed + index - 1
        tracks.append(track)
    shared = dict(base)
    shared["duration"] = chunk
    shared["fade_in_duration"] = 0
    shared["fade_out_duration"] = 0
    album = {
        "album_title": base.get("caption", "Longform"),
        "artist": "AI Music Maker",
        "output_dir": str(out_dir),
        "shared": shared,
        "tracks": tracks,
    }
    return album, count


def merge(chunks: list[Path], output: Path, target: float, crossfade: float,
          fade_in: float, fade_out: float, work_dir: Path) -> int:
    filters = []
    last = "[0:a]"
    for index in range(1, len(chunks)):
        label = f"[a{index}]"
        filters.append(
            f"{last}[{index}:a]acrossfade=d={crossfade:.3f}:c1=tri:c2=tri{label}")
        last = label
    final_filters = [f"loudnorm=I=-14:TP=-1.5:LRA=11"]
    if fade_in > 0:
        final_filters.append(f"afade=t=in:st=0:d={fade_in:.3f}")
    if fade_out > 0:
        final_filters.append(
            f"afade=t=out:st={max(0.0, target - fade_out):.3f}:d={fade_out:.3f}")
    filters.append(f"{last}{','.join(final_filters)}[out]")
    command = [str(FFMPEG), "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", "-y"]
    for path in chunks:
        command += ["-i", str(path)]
    command += [
        "-filter_complex", ";".join(filters),
        "-map", "[out]",
        "-t", f"{target:.3f}",
        "-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-b:a", "320k", "-ar", "48000",
        str(output),
    ]
    result = subprocess.run(command, cwd=str(work_dir))
    return result.returncode


def write_metadata(output: Path, base: dict, target: float, count: int,
                   crossfade: float) -> None:
    caption = str(base.get("caption") or "").strip()
    title = caption[:90] or output.stem
    description = (
        f"{caption}\n\n"
        f"AI-generated longform music ({target / 60:.1f} min).\n"
        f"Generated with AI Music Maker / ACE-Step.\n"
    )
    words = [word.strip(",.;:()[]") for word in caption.split()]
    tags = [word for word in words if 2 <= len(word) <= 20][:12]
    tags += ["AI music", "instrumental", "background music"]
    metadata = {
        "title": title,
        "description": description,
        "tags": tags,
        "categoryId": "10",
        "privacyStatus": "private",
        "madeForKids": False,
        "duration_seconds": target,
        "chunks": count,
        "crossfade_seconds": crossfade,
    }
    output.with_suffix(".youtube.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(metadata, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")


def split_batches(album: dict, per_process: int) -> list[list[dict]]:
    tracks = list(album.get("tracks") or [])
    if per_process <= 0 or per_process >= len(tracks):
        return [tracks]
    return [tracks[start:start + per_process]
            for start in range(0, len(tracks), per_process)]


def generate(album: dict, batches: list[list[dict]], work_dir: Path,
             gpu_wait: float, dry_run: bool, python: str | None = None) -> list[Path]:
    chunks: list[Path] = []
    for number, group in enumerate(batches, 1):
        batch_dir = work_dir / f"batch_{number:02d}"
        batch_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        batch_album = dict(album)
        batch_album["tracks"] = group
        batch_album["output_dir"] = str(batch_dir)
        album_path = batch_dir / "album_input.json"
        album_path.write_text(json.dumps(batch_album, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
                              encoding="utf-8")
        label = f"batch {number}/{len(batches)} tracks={len(group)}"
        if dry_run:
            print(f"[dry-run] {label} -> {album_path}")
            continue
        print(f"[longform] {label}")
        album_out = batch_dir / "album.json"
        try:
            album_out.unlink()
        except OSError:
            pass
        result = subprocess.run([python or backend_python(None), "-u", str(BACKEND),
                                 "--album", str(album_path),
                                 "--gpu-wait", str(gpu_wait)])
        produced: list[Path] = []
        if album_out.is_file():
            meta = json.loads(album_out.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            produced = [Path(track["file"]) for track in meta.get("tracks", [])
                        if Path(track.get("file", "")).is_file()]
            failed = meta.get("invalid") or []
            if failed:
                detail = ", ".join(f"#{item.get('index')} {item.get('error')}"
                                   for item in failed)
                raise RuntimeError(f"[error] {label} failed: {detail}")
        if result.returncode != 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"[error] {label} backend exit={result.returncode}")
        if len(produced) != len(group):
            raise RuntimeError(f"[error] {label} produced {len(produced)}/{len(group)}")
        chunks.extend(produced)
    return chunks


def main() -> int:
    args = parse_args()
    if not args.config.is_file():
        print(f"[error] missing config: {args.config}")
        return 2
    base = json.loads(args.config.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
    target = float(args.duration or base.get("duration") or 0)
    if target < 60:
        print("[error] duration must be at least 60 seconds")
        return 2
    out_dir = Path(base.get("output_dir") or DEFAULT_OUT)
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    work_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="longform_", dir=str(out_dir)))
    try:
        album, count = build_album(base, target, args.chunk, args.crossfade, work_dir)
        batches = split_batches(album, args.chunks_per_process)
        print(f"[longform] target={target:.0f}s chunks={count} chunk={args.chunk:.0f}s "
              f"batches={len(batches)}")
        chunks = generate(album, batches, work_dir, args.gpu_wait, args.dry_run,
                          backend_python(args.python))
        if args.dry_run:
            return 0
        output = args.out or (out_dir / f"longform_{stamp}.mp3")
        fade_in = float(base.get("fade_in_duration") or 0)
        fade_out = float(base.get("fade_out_duration") or 0)
        print(f"[longform] merging {len(chunks)} chunks crossfade={args.crossfade}s")
        code = merge(chunks, output, target, args.crossfade, fade_in, fade_out, work_dir)
        if code != 0 or not output.is_file():
            print(f"[error] merge exit={code}")
            return 1
        write_metadata(output, base, target, count, args.crossfade)
        print(f"[done] {output}")
        print(f"[meta] {output.with_suffix('.youtube.json')}")
        if not args.keep_chunks:
            for path in chunks:
                try:
                    path.unlink()
                except OSError:
                    pass
        return 0
    except RuntimeError as exc:
        print(str(exc))
        return 1
    finally:
        if args.keep_chunks:
            print(f"[chunks] kept in {work_dir}")
        else:
            import shutil
            shutil.rmtree(work_dir, ignore_errors=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


===== [53/66] AI_Music/scripts/make_code_bundle.py  (3634 bytes) =====
"""Build a single UTF-8 text bundle of the AI Music Maker source and docs."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

BASE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
ROOT = BASE.parent
EXCLUDE_DIRS = {
    ".git", ".venv", "AI_Music.venv", "ACE-Step-1.5", "models", "wheels",
    "generated", "logs", "tools", "lib", "__pycache__", ".cache", ".hf-cache",
    ".uv-cache", ".xet-test", "node_modules",
}
INCLUDE_EXTENSIONS = {
    ".py", ".cs", ".ps1", ".bat", ".cmd", ".sh", ".json", ".md", ".txt",
    ".manifest", ".config", ".yaml", ".yml", ".toml",
}
ROOT_FILES = (
    ".gitignore",
    "migrate_venv_paths.ps1",
    "verify_migration.ps1",
    "retarget_workspace_paths.ps1",
)
SCAN_DIRS = (
    BASE / "app",
    BASE / "scripts",
    BASE / "tests",
)


def is_bundle(path: Path) -> bool:
    return path.name.startswith("AI_Music_code_bundle_")


def iter_candidates() -> list[Path]:
    files: list[Path] = []
    for name in ROOT_FILES:
        path = ROOT / name
        if path.is_file() and not is_bundle(path):
            files.append(path)
    for directory in SCAN_DIRS:
        if not directory.is_dir():
            continue
        for path in directory.rglob("*"):
            if not path.is_file() or is_bundle(path):
                continue
            if any(part in EXCLUDE_DIRS for part in path.relative_to(ROOT).parts):
                continue
            if path.suffix.lower() not in INCLUDE_EXTENSIONS:
                continue
            if path.stat().st_size > 5 * 1024 * 1024:
                continue
            files.append(path)
    for path in BASE.glob("*.md"):
        if path.is_file() and not is_bundle(path):
            files.append(path)
    for name in ("requirements_local.txt", "requirements.txt"):
        path = BASE / name
        if path.is_file():
            files.append(path)
    unique = {}
    for path in files:
        unique[path.resolve()] = None
    return sorted(unique, key=lambda item: str(item.relative_to(ROOT)).lower())


def build(output: Path) -> tuple[int, int]:
    files = iter_candidates()
    sections = []
    for index, path in enumerate(files, 1):
        try:
            text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
        except (UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
            continue
        relative = path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
        size = path.stat().st_size
        sections.append(
            f"===== [{index}/{len(files)}] {relative}  ({size} bytes) =====\n{text}\n")
    now = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    header = (
        "AI Music Maker / ACE-Step 1.5 - 全部程式碼與文件彙整\n"
        f"建立時間: {now}\n"
        f"專案根目錄: {ROOT}\n"
        "內容: 程式碼、設定、文件；排除 models/.venv/ACE-Step/generated/logs/tools/二進位/快取\n"
        f"檔案數: {len(sections)}\n"
        "================================================================================\n\n"
    )
    body = "\n".join(sections)
    output.write_text(header + body, encoding="utf-8")
    return len(sections), output.stat().st_size


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.out is None:
        stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
        args.out = BASE / f"AI_Music_code_bundle_{stamp}.txt"
    count, size = build(args.out)
    print(f"[done] {args.out}")
    print(f"[files] {count}")
    print(f"[bytes] {size}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
