===== [55/66] AI_Music/scripts/music_to_video.py  (15736 bytes) =====
"""Render generated audio into YouTube-ready MP4 files with the bundled ffmpeg."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

from _common import BASE_DIR, safe_stdio

FFMPEG = BASE_DIR / "tools" / "ffmpeg" / "bin" / "ffmpeg.exe"
DEFAULT_COVER = BASE_DIR / "app" / "cover.png"
AUDIO_EXTS = {".mp3", ".wav", ".flac", ".m4a", ".aac", ".ogg", ".opus"}
IMAGE_EXTS = (".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".webp", ".bmp")
COVER_NAMES = ("album_cover.png", "album_cover.jpg", "cover.png", "cover.jpg")


def parse_size(text: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
    match = re.fullmatch(r"(\d+)x(\d+)", text.strip().lower())
    if not match:
        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("size must look like 1920x1080")
    width, height = int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2))
    if width < 16 or height < 16 or width % 2 or height % 2:
        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("width and height must be even and at least 16")
    return width, height


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Turn audio files into MP4 videos using a cover image.",
    )
    parser.add_argument("audio", nargs="*", type=Path, help="audio files to render")
    parser.add_argument("--input-dir", type=Path, help="render every audio file in this folder")
    parser.add_argument("--from-settings", action="store_true",
                        help="use the output folder saved by AI Music Maker")
    parser.add_argument("--recursive", action="store_true", help="also scan subfolders of --input-dir")
    parser.add_argument("--outdir", type=Path, help="output folder (default: next to each audio file)")
    parser.add_argument("--image", type=Path, help="cover image for every file (default: auto-detect)")
    parser.add_argument("--style", choices=("blur", "cover", "waveform", "kenburns", "ambient"),
                        default="blur")
    parser.add_argument("--size", type=parse_size, default=(1920, 1080), help="WxH, default 1920x1080")
    parser.add_argument("--fps", type=int, default=5)
    parser.add_argument("--crf", type=int, default=23)
    parser.add_argument("--preset", default="veryfast")
    parser.add_argument("--audio-bitrate", default="192k")
    parser.add_argument("--overwrite", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--ffmpeg", type=Path, default=FFMPEG)
    return parser


def find_ffmpeg(explicit: Path) -> Path | None:
    for candidate in (explicit, Path("ffmpeg.exe"), Path("ffmpeg")):
        if candidate.is_file():
            return candidate
        resolved = candidate.resolve() if candidate.parent != Path(".") else None
        if resolved and resolved.is_file():
            return resolved
    for name in ("ffmpeg.exe", "ffmpeg"):
        for folder in (BASE_DIR / "tools" / "ffmpeg" / "bin",):
            candidate = folder / name
            if candidate.is_file():
                return candidate
    return None


def probe_duration(ffmpeg: Path, audio: Path) -> float | None:
    result = subprocess.run(
        [str(ffmpeg), "-hide_banner", "-i", str(audio)],
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        encoding="utf-8",
        errors="replace",
    )
    match = re.search(r"Duration:\s*(\d+):(\d+):(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)", result.stderr)
    if not match:
        return None
    hours, minutes, seconds = match.groups()
    return int(hours) * 3600 + int(minutes) * 60 + float(seconds)


def find_cover(audio: Path, explicit: Path | None, default: Path) -> Path | None:
    if explicit is not None:
        return explicit if explicit.is_file() else None
    folder = audio.parent
    for suffix in (".cover.png", ".cover.jpg", ".cover.jpeg"):
        candidate = folder / (audio.stem + suffix)
        if candidate.is_file():
            return candidate
    for name in COVER_NAMES:
        candidate = folder / name
        if candidate.is_file():
            return candidate
    for ext in IMAGE_EXTS:
        candidate = folder / (audio.stem + ext)
        if candidate.is_file():
            return candidate
    return default if default.is_file() else None


def motion_filter(width: int, height: int, fps: int, ambient: bool) -> str:
    scale_width, scale_height = width * 2, height * 2
    zoom = f"min(1+0.02*on/({fps}*60),1.05)"
    extra = ",noise=alls=3:allf=t+u,vignette=PI/5" if ambient else ""
    return (
        f"scale={scale_width}:{scale_height}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,"
        f"crop={scale_width}:{scale_height},"
        f"zoompan=z='{zoom}':x='iw/2-(iw/zoom/2)':y='ih/2-(ih/zoom/2)':"
        f"d=1:s={width}x{height}:fps={fps},setsar=1{extra},format=yuv420p[v]"
    )


def build_filter(style: str, width: int, height: int, fps: int = 5) -> str:
    if style == "cover":
        return (
            f"scale={width}:{height}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,"
            f"crop={width}:{height},setsar=1,format=yuv420p[v]"
        )

    if style == "kenburns":
        return motion_filter(width, height, fps, False)

    if style == "ambient":
        return motion_filter(width, height, fps, True)

    if style == "waveform":
        size = max(16, int(height * 0.62) // 2 * 2)
        wave_height = max(16, int(height * 0.12) // 2 * 2)
        wave_y = height - wave_height - max(8, int(height * 0.03))
        panel_y = wave_y - max(8, int(height * 0.015))
        panel_height = wave_height + (wave_y - panel_y) * 2
        top = max(0, (height - size) // 2 - int(height * 0.04))
        filters = [
            "[0:v]split=2[bgsrc][fgsrc]",
            f"[bgsrc]scale={width}:{height}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,"
            f"crop={width}:{height},gblur=sigma=32,eq=brightness=-0.10:saturation=0.85,setsar=1[bg]",
            f"[fgsrc]scale={size}:{size}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,"
            f"crop={size}:{size},setsar=1[fg]",
            f"[bg][fg]overlay=(W-w)/2:{top}:format=auto[base]",
            f"[base]drawbox=x=0:y={panel_y}:w=iw:h={panel_height}:color=black@0.30:t=fill[panel]",
            f"[2:v]scale={width}:{wave_height},setsar=1,format=rgba,colorchannelmixer=aa=0.85[wave]",
            f"[panel][wave]overlay=0:{wave_y}:format=auto,format=yuv420p[v]",
        ]
        return ";".join(filters)

    size = max(16, int(height * 0.72) // 2 * 2)
    filters = [
        "[0:v]split=2[bgsrc][fgsrc]",
        f"[bgsrc]scale={width}:{height}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,"
        f"crop={width}:{height},gblur=sigma=32,eq=brightness=-0.10:saturation=0.85,setsar=1[bg]",
        f"[fgsrc]scale={size}:{size}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,"
        f"crop={size}:{size},setsar=1[fg]",
        f"[bg][fg]overlay=(W-w)/2:(H-h)/2:format=auto,format=yuv420p[v]",
    ]
    return ";".join(filters)


def render_waveform(ffmpeg: Path, audio: Path, width: int, height: int, target: Path) -> bool:
    command = [
        str(ffmpeg),
        "-hide_banner",
        "-loglevel",
        "error",
        "-i",
        str(audio),
        "-filter_complex",
        f"showwavespic=s={width}x{height}:colors=0x8FD3FF:scale=sqrt[w]",
        "-map",
        "[w]",
        "-frames:v",
        "1",
        "-y",
        str(target),
    ]
    result = subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    if result.returncode != 0:
        if result.stderr.strip():
            print(result.stderr.strip(), flush=True)
        return False
    return target.is_file()


def render(
    ffmpeg: Path,
    audio: Path,
    image: Path,
    output: Path,
    style: str,
    size: tuple[int, int],
    fps: int,
    crf: int,
    preset: str,
    audio_bitrate: str,
    overwrite: bool,
    wave_image: Path | None,
) -> int:
    width, height = size
    filter_graph = build_filter(style, width, height, fps)
    command = [
        str(ffmpeg),
        "-hide_banner",
        "-loglevel",
        "error",
        "-nostats",
        "-progress",
        "pipe:1",
        "-loop",
        "1",
        "-framerate",
        str(fps),
        "-i",
        str(image),
        "-i",
        str(audio),
    ]
    if wave_image is not None:
        command += ["-loop", "1", "-framerate", str(fps), "-i", str(wave_image)]
    command += ["-filter_complex", filter_graph, "-map", "[v]", "-map", "1:a:0"]
    command += [
        "-c:v",
        "libx264",
        "-preset",
        preset,
        "-crf",
        str(crf),
        "-pix_fmt",
        "yuv420p",
        "-r",
        str(fps),
        "-c:a",
        "aac",
        "-b:a",
        audio_bitrate,
        "-ar",
        "48000",
        "-movflags",
        "+faststart",
        "-tune",
        "stillimage",
    ]
    duration = probe_duration(ffmpeg, audio)
    if duration:
        command += ["-t", f"{duration:.3f}"]
    else:
        command += ["-shortest"]
    command += ["-y" if overwrite else "-n", str(output)]
    last_percent = -5
    progress_shown = False
    process = subprocess.Popen(
        command,
        stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
        stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
        text=True,
        encoding="utf-8",
        errors="replace",
    )
    assert process.stdout is not None
    for raw in process.stdout:
        line = raw.strip()
        if line.startswith(("out_time_us=", "out_time_ms=")):
            value = line.split("=", 1)[1]
            if value.isdigit() and duration:
                percent = min(100, int(value) / 1_000_000 / duration * 100)
                if percent >= last_percent + 5:
                    last_percent = int(percent)
                    progress_shown = True
                    print(f"\r[video] {last_percent:3d}%", end="", flush=True)
        elif line.startswith(("progress=", "frame=", "fps=", "bitrate=", "total_size=", "out_time=",
                              "dup_frames=", "drop_frames=", "speed=", "stream_")):
            continue
        elif line:
            print("\n" + line, flush=True)
    code = process.wait()
    if progress_shown:
        if last_percent < 100:
            print("\r[video] 100%", flush=True)
        print(flush=True)
    return code


def write_youtube_metadata(output: Path, audio: Path) -> None:
    sidecar = audio.with_suffix(".json")
    caption = audio.stem
    try:
        data = json.loads(sidecar.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
        caption = str((data.get("config") or {}).get("caption") or caption)
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        pass
    title = caption.strip()[:90] or output.stem
    words = [word.strip(",.;:()[]") for word in caption.split()]
    tags = [word for word in words if 2 <= len(word) <= 20][:12]
    tags += ["AI music", "instrumental", "background music"]
    metadata = {
        "title": title,
        "description": caption.strip() + "\n\nAI-generated music / video.",
        "tags": tags,
        "categoryId": "10",
        "privacyStatus": "private",
        "madeForKids": False,
    }
    output.with_suffix(".youtube.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(metadata, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")


def collect_audio(args: argparse.Namespace) -> list[Path]:
    files: list[Path] = []
    if args.input_dir is not None:
        pattern = "**/*" if args.recursive else "*"
        files += [
            path for path in sorted(args.input_dir.glob(pattern))
            if path.is_file() and path.suffix.lower() in AUDIO_EXTS
        ]
    files += [path for path in args.audio if path.suffix.lower() in AUDIO_EXTS]
    unique: dict[Path, None] = {}
    for path in files:
        unique[path.resolve()] = None
    return list(unique)


def main() -> int:
    safe_stdio()
    args = build_parser().parse_args()
    ffmpeg = find_ffmpeg(args.ffmpeg)
    if ffmpeg is None:
        print("[error] ffmpeg not found; pass --ffmpeg or install it in tools/ffmpeg/bin")
        return 2

    if args.from_settings and args.input_dir is None and not args.audio:
        settings_path = Path(os.environ.get("APPDATA", "")) / "AIMusicGenerator" / "settings.json"
        try:
            settings = json.loads(settings_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            output_dir = settings.get("output_dir")
            if output_dir:
                args.input_dir = Path(output_dir)
                print(f"[settings] output_dir={args.input_dir}", flush=True)
            else:
                print("[warn] output_dir not set in settings.json", flush=True)
        except (OSError, ValueError) as error:
            print(f"[warn] cannot read {settings_path}: {error}", flush=True)

    files = collect_audio(args)
    if not files:
        print("[error] no audio files found")
        return 2

    if args.outdir is not None:
        args.outdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    rendered = skipped = failed = 0
    for audio in files:
        if not audio.is_file():
            print(f"[error] missing audio: {audio}", flush=True)
            failed += 1
            continue
        image = find_cover(audio, args.image, DEFAULT_COVER)
        if image is None:
            print(f"[error] no cover image for: {audio}", flush=True)
            failed += 1
            continue
        output_dir = args.outdir if args.outdir is not None else audio.parent
        output = output_dir / (audio.stem + ".mp4")
        if output.exists() and not args.overwrite:
            print(f"[skip] exists: {output}", flush=True)
            skipped += 1
            continue

        print(f"[video] {audio.name} + {image.name} -> {output}", flush=True)
        if args.dry_run:
            print(f"[dry-run] {ffmpeg} ... {output}", flush=True)
            rendered += 1
            continue
        style = args.style
        wave_dir = None
        wave_image = None
        if style == "waveform":
            wave_dir = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="aimusic_wave_")
            wave_image = Path(wave_dir.name) / "waveform.png"
            wave_height = max(16, int(args.size[1] * 0.12) // 2 * 2)
            if not render_waveform(ffmpeg, audio, min(args.size[0], 960), wave_height, wave_image):
                print("[warn] waveform render failed; falling back to blur", flush=True)
                style = "blur"
                wave_image = None
        try:
            code = render(
                ffmpeg,
                audio,
                image,
                output,
                style,
                args.size,
                args.fps,
                args.crf,
                args.preset,
                args.audio_bitrate,
                args.overwrite,
                wave_image,
            )
        finally:
            if wave_dir is not None:
                wave_dir.cleanup()
        if code == 0 and output.is_file():
            write_youtube_metadata(output, audio)
            size_mb = output.stat().st_size / (1024 * 1024)
            print(f"[done] {output} ({size_mb:.1f} MB)", flush=True)
            rendered += 1
        else:
            print(f"[error] ffmpeg exit={code}: {audio}", flush=True)
            failed += 1

    print(f"[summary] rendered={rendered} skipped={skipped} failed={failed}", flush=True)
    return 0 if failed == 0 else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())


===== [56/66] AI_Music/scripts/telegram_bot.py  (31624 bytes) =====
"""Telegram remote control for AI Music Maker.

Commands:
  /gen <prompt>          generate audio and send it back
  /longform <prompt>     chunked long track with crossfade
  /album <title> | ...   generate an album (prompts separated by ;)
  /status                show current job and settings
  /cancel                cancel the running job
  /duration <time>       set length: seconds, mm:ss, or hh:mm:ss
  /video on|off          convert generated audio to MP4 before sending
  /fade <in> <out>       set fade in/out seconds for future jobs
  /fade off              disable fade
  /merge [n]             merge the last n generated tracks (default 2)
  /outputs               list recent generated files
  /help                  show commands

Config: %APPDATA%\\AIMusicGenerator\\telegram.json
  {"bot_token": "...", "chat_id": 123456789}
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import queue
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import threading
import time
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

import requests

BASE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
BACKEND = BASE / "app" / "generate_backend.py"
VIDEO_TOOL = BASE / "scripts" / "music_to_video.py"
AUDIO_TOOL = BASE / "scripts" / "audio_edit.py"
LONGFORM_TOOL = BASE / "scripts" / "longform_generate.py"
CONFIG = Path(os.environ.get("APPDATA", str(Path.home()))) / "AIMusicGenerator" / "telegram.json"
STATE_FILE = CONFIG.parent / "telegram_settings.json"
OUT_DIR = BASE / "generated" / "telegram"
LOG = Path(os.environ.get("TEMP", tempfile.gettempdir())) / "AIMusicGenerator" / "telegram.log"

TOKEN = ""
job_queue: queue.Queue = queue.Queue()
current = {"running": False, "prompt": "", "started": 0.0, "process": None, "cancel": False}
recent: list[str] = []
recent_lock = threading.Lock()
settings = {
    "duration": int(os.environ.get("AI_MUSIC_TG_DURATION", "60")),
    "video": False,
    "fade_in": 0.0,
    "fade_out": 0.0,
}


def redact(text: str) -> str:
    return text.replace(TOKEN, "<TOKEN>") if TOKEN else text


def log(message: str) -> None:
    LOG.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    message = redact(str(message))
    line = f"{datetime.now():%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S} {message}"
    with open(LOG, "a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        handle.write(line + "\n")
    print(line, flush=True)


def load_config() -> dict:
    if not CONFIG.is_file():
        raise SystemExit(f"missing config: {CONFIG}")
    return json.loads(CONFIG.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def load_settings() -> None:
    try:
        if STATE_FILE.is_file():
            data = json.loads(STATE_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            if 10 <= int(data.get("duration", settings["duration"])) <= 7200:
                settings["duration"] = int(data.get("duration"))
            settings["video"] = bool(data.get("video", settings["video"]))
            settings["fade_in"] = max(0.0, min(30.0, float(data.get("fade_in", 0.0))))
            settings["fade_out"] = max(0.0, min(30.0, float(data.get("fade_out", 0.0))))
    except Exception as exc:
        log(f"settings load failed: {exc}")


def save_settings() -> None:
    try:
        STATE_FILE.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        STATE_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(settings, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    except Exception as exc:
        log(f"settings save failed: {exc}")


def api(token: str, method: str, **fields):
    url = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{token}/{method}"
    response = requests.post(url, data=fields, timeout=60)
    response.raise_for_status()
    return response.json()


def send(token: str, chat_id: int, text: str) -> None:
    try:
        api(token, "sendMessage", chat_id=chat_id, text=redact(text)[:4000])
    except Exception as exc:
        log(f"sendMessage failed: {exc}")


def send_document(token: str, chat_id: int, path: Path, caption: str, mime: str) -> bool:
    try:
        if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size > 49 * 1024 * 1024:
            send(token, chat_id, f"檔案過大或不存在，未傳送：{path.name}")
            return False
        with open(path, "rb") as handle:
            response = requests.post(
                f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{token}/sendDocument",
                data={"chat_id": chat_id, "caption": caption[:200]},
                files={"document": (path.name, handle, mime)},
                timeout=600,
            )
        response.raise_for_status()
        return True
    except Exception as exc:
        log(f"sendDocument failed: {exc}")
        send(token, chat_id, f"傳送檔案失敗：{exc}")
        return False


def send_video(token: str, chat_id: int, path: Path, caption: str) -> bool:
    try:
        if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size > 49 * 1024 * 1024:
            return False
        with open(path, "rb") as handle:
            response = requests.post(
                f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{token}/sendVideo",
                data={"chat_id": chat_id, "caption": caption[:200],
                      "supports_streaming": "true"},
                files={"video": (path.name, handle, "video/mp4")},
                timeout=900,
            )
        response.raise_for_status()
        return True
    except Exception as exc:
        log(f"sendVideo failed: {exc}")
        return False


def parse_duration(text: str) -> int | None:
    value = text.strip()
    if not value:
        return None
    try:
        if ":" in value:
            parts = [int(part) for part in value.split(":")]
            if len(parts) == 2:
                total = parts[0] * 60 + parts[1]
            elif len(parts) == 3:
                total = parts[0] * 3600 + parts[1] * 60 + parts[2]
            else:
                return None
        else:
            total = int(value)
    except ValueError:
        return None
    return total if 10 <= total <= 7200 else None


def format_duration(seconds: int) -> str:
    if seconds >= 3600:
        return f"{seconds // 3600}:{seconds % 3600 // 60:02d}:{seconds % 60:02d}"
    return f"{seconds // 60}:{seconds % 60:02d}"


def busy_owner(log_path: Path) -> str:
    try:
        for line in log_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines():
            if line.startswith("[busy]"):
                return line[6:].strip() or "unknown"
    except Exception:
        pass
    return "unknown"


def add_recent(path: Path) -> None:
    with recent_lock:
        recent.append(str(path))
        if len(recent) > 20:
            del recent[: len(recent) - 20]


def kill_process(process) -> None:
    if process is None:
        return
    try:
        if os.name == "nt":
            subprocess.run(["taskkill", "/PID", str(process.pid), "/T", "/F"],
                           capture_output=True, timeout=30)
        else:
            process.terminate()
    except Exception as exc:
        log(f"kill failed: {exc}")


def apply_fade(path: Path, out_dir: Path, job_settings: dict) -> Path:
    fade_in = job_settings["fade_in"]
    fade_out = job_settings["fade_out"]
    if fade_in <= 0 and fade_out <= 0:
        return path
    output = out_dir / f"{path.stem}_fade{path.suffix}"
    command = [
        sys.executable, "-u", str(AUDIO_TOOL), "fade", str(path),
        "--fade-in", f"{fade_in:.1f}", "--fade-out", f"{fade_out:.1f}",
        "--out", str(output), "--overwrite",
    ]
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True, text=True,
                                encoding="utf-8", errors="replace", timeout=1800)
        log(f"fade exit={result.returncode}")
        if result.returncode == 0 and output.is_file():
            return output
    except Exception as exc:
        log(f"fade failed: {exc}")
    return path


def make_video(path: Path, duration: int) -> Path | None:
    command = [sys.executable, "-u", str(VIDEO_TOOL), str(path)]
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True, text=True,
                                encoding="utf-8", errors="replace",
                                timeout=max(1800, duration * 4))
        log(f"video exit={result.returncode}")
        output = path.with_suffix(".mp4")
        if result.returncode == 0 and output.is_file():
            return output
    except Exception as exc:
        log(f"video failed: {exc}")
    return None


def worker_loop() -> None:
    while True:
        job = job_queue.get()
        try:
            job()
        except Exception as exc:
            log(f"queued job failed: {exc}")
        finally:
            job_queue.task_done()


def run_job(token: str, chat_id: int, prompt: str, job_settings: dict | None = None) -> None:
    job_settings = dict(settings) if job_settings is None else dict(job_settings)
    duration = job_settings["duration"]
    current.update(running=True, prompt=prompt, started=time.time(),
                   process=None, cancel=False)
    job_id = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    out_dir = OUT_DIR / job_id
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    thinking = duration <= 480
    config = {
        "caption": prompt,
        "lyrics": "[Instrumental]",
        "instrumental": True,
        "thinking": thinking,
        "bpm": None,
        "keyscale": "",
        "timesignature": "",
        "vocal_language": "unknown",
        "duration": duration,
        "inference_steps": 8,
        "guidance_scale": 1.0,
        "seed": -1,
        "output_dir": str(out_dir),
        "filename_prefix": "telegram",
        "audio_format": "mp3",
        "offload_mode": "auto",
    }
    config_path = out_dir / "config.json"
    config_path.write_text(json.dumps(config, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
    extras = []
    if job_settings["video"]:
        extras.append("自動轉影片")
    if job_settings["fade_in"] > 0 or job_settings["fade_out"] > 0:
        extras.append(f"淡入 {job_settings['fade_in']:.1f}s / 淡出 {job_settings['fade_out']:.1f}s")
    suffix = "（" + "、".join(extras) + "）" if extras else ""
    if not thinking:
        send(token, chat_id, "時長超過 480 秒，已自動關閉 LM 思考以降低 VRAM 風險。")
    send(token, chat_id, f"開始生成 {format_duration(duration)} MP3{suffix}…\n{prompt[:200]}")
    log_path = out_dir / "run.log"
    try:
        with open(log_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as log_handle:
            process = subprocess.Popen(
                [sys.executable, "-u", str(BACKEND), "--config", str(config_path),
                 "--gpu-wait", "20"],
                stdout=log_handle, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
            )
            current["process"] = process
            try:
                code = process.wait(timeout=21600)
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                kill_process(process)
                code = -1
        current["process"] = None
        if current["cancel"]:
            send(token, chat_id, "已取消生成。")
            return
        if code == 75:
            owner = busy_owner(log_path)
            send(token, chat_id,
                 f"GPU 正被使用中（{owner}）。若是電腦上的 AI Music Maker 常駐了模型，"
                 "請關閉 App 釋放模型後再試。")
            return
        produced = None
        for line in reversed(log_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines()):
            if line.startswith("[done]"):
                produced = line[6:].strip()
                break
        if code != 0 or not produced or not Path(produced).is_file():
            send(token, chat_id, f"生成失敗（exit {code}），請查看日誌。")
            return
        audio = apply_fade(Path(produced), out_dir, job_settings)
        add_recent(audio)
        if job_settings["video"]:
            video = make_video(audio, duration)
            if video is not None:
                if send_video(token, chat_id, video,
                              f"影片 {video.name}（{video.stat().st_size / 1024 / 1024:.1f} MB）"):
                    send(token, chat_id, f"影片完成：{video.name}")
                else:
                    send(token, chat_id, "影片過大或傳送失敗，改傳音訊。")
        size = audio.stat().st_size
        if send_document(token, chat_id, audio, prompt[:200], "audio/mpeg"):
            send(token, chat_id, f"完成：{audio.name}（{size / 1024 / 1024:.1f} MB）")
    except Exception as exc:
        log(f"job failed: {exc}")
        if current["cancel"]:
            send(token, chat_id, "已取消生成。")
        else:
            send(token, chat_id, f"生成失敗：{exc}")
    finally:
        current.update(running=False, prompt="", started=0.0, process=None, cancel=False)


def run_album_job(token: str, chat_id: int, title: str, prompts: list[str],
                  job_settings: dict | None = None) -> None:
    job_settings = dict(settings) if job_settings is None else dict(job_settings)
    duration = job_settings["duration"]
    current.update(running=True, prompt="album: " + title, started=time.time(),
                   process=None, cancel=False)
    job_id = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    out_dir = BASE / "generated" / "telegram_album" / job_id
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    tracks = []
    for index, prompt in enumerate(prompts, 1):
        tracks.append({"title": f"Track {index:02d}", "prompt": prompt, "duration": duration})
    shared = {
        "instrumental": True, "thinking": duration <= 480, "bpm": None, "keyscale": "",
        "timesignature": "", "vocal_language": "unknown", "duration": duration,
        "inference_steps": 8, "guidance_scale": 1.0, "seed": -1,
        "output_dir": str(out_dir), "filename_prefix": "album",
        "audio_format": "mp3", "offload_mode": "auto",
    }
    album = {"album_title": title, "artist": "AI Music Maker",
             "output_dir": str(out_dir), "shared": shared, "tracks": tracks}
    config_path = out_dir / "album_input.json"
    config_path.write_text(json.dumps(album, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
    send(token, chat_id, f"開始製作專輯：{title}（{len(prompts)} 首，每首 {format_duration(duration)}）")
    log_path = out_dir / "run.log"
    try:
        with open(log_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as log_handle:
            process = subprocess.Popen(
                [sys.executable, "-u", str(BACKEND), "--album", str(config_path),
                 "--gpu-wait", "20"],
                stdout=log_handle, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
            )
            current["process"] = process
            try:
                code = process.wait(timeout=21600)
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                kill_process(process)
                code = -1
        current["process"] = None
        if current["cancel"]:
            send(token, chat_id, "已取消專輯製作。")
            return
        if code == 75:
            owner = busy_owner(log_path)
            send(token, chat_id,
                 f"GPU 正被使用中（{owner}）。若是電腦上的 AI Music Maker 常駐了模型，"
                 "請關閉 App 釋放模型後再試。")
            return
        meta_path = out_dir / "album.json"
        if code != 0 or not meta_path.is_file():
            send(token, chat_id, f"專輯失敗（exit {code}），請查看日誌。")
            return
        meta = json.loads(meta_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        sent = 0
        for track in meta.get("tracks", []):
            file_path = Path(track.get("file", ""))
            if not file_path.is_file():
                continue
            if send_document(token, chat_id, file_path,
                             track.get("title", file_path.name), "audio/mpeg"):
                sent += 1
                add_recent(file_path)
        send(token, chat_id, f"專輯完成：{title}（已傳送 {sent} 首）")
    except Exception as exc:
        log(f"album job failed: {exc}")
        send(token, chat_id, f"專輯失敗：{exc}")
    finally:
        current.update(running=False, prompt="", started=0.0, process=None, cancel=False)


def run_longform_job(token: str, chat_id: int, prompt: str,
                     job_settings: dict | None = None) -> None:
    job_settings = dict(settings) if job_settings is None else dict(job_settings)
    duration = job_settings["duration"]
    if duration <= 300:
        send(token, chat_id, "長曲模式需要超過 5 分鐘；短曲請用 /gen。")
        return
    job_id = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    job_dir = OUT_DIR / ("long_" + job_id)
    job_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    output = job_dir / "longform.mp3"
    base = {
        "caption": prompt,
        "lyrics": "[Instrumental]",
        "instrumental": True,
        "thinking": duration <= 480,
        "bpm": None,
        "keyscale": "",
        "timesignature": "",
        "vocal_language": "unknown",
        "duration": duration,
        "inference_steps": 8,
        "guidance_scale": 1.0,
        "seed": -1,
        "output_dir": str(job_dir),
        "filename_prefix": "longform",
        "audio_format": "mp3",
        "offload_mode": "auto",
        "fade_in_duration": job_settings["fade_in"],
        "fade_out_duration": job_settings["fade_out"],
    }
    config_path = job_dir / "base.json"
    config_path.write_text(json.dumps(base, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
    current.update(running=True, prompt="longform: " + prompt, started=time.time(),
                   process=None, cancel=False)
    send(token, chat_id,
         f"開始長曲分段：{format_duration(duration)}，每段約 3 分鐘、5 秒交叉淡化。")
    log_path = job_dir / "run.log"
    try:
        with open(log_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as log_handle:
            process = subprocess.Popen(
                [sys.executable, "-u", str(LONGFORM_TOOL),
                 "--config", str(config_path), "--duration", str(duration),
                 "--chunk", "180", "--crossfade", "5", "--chunks-per-process", "3",
                 "--out", str(output),
                 "--gpu-wait", "60"],
                stdout=log_handle, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
            )
            current["process"] = process
            try:
                code = process.wait(timeout=21600)
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                kill_process(process)
                code = -1
        current["process"] = None
        if current["cancel"]:
            send(token, chat_id, "已取消長曲生成。")
            return
        if code == 75:
            owner = busy_owner(log_path)
            send(token, chat_id,
                 f"GPU 正被使用中（{owner}）。請關閉 App 釋放模型後再試。")
            return
        if code != 0 or not output.is_file():
            send(token, chat_id, f"長曲失敗（exit {code}），請查看日誌。")
            return
        add_recent(output)
        size_mb = output.stat().st_size / 1024 / 1024
        send(token, chat_id, f"長曲完成：{output.name}（{size_mb:.1f} MB）")
        if size_mb <= 49:
            send_document(token, chat_id, output, prompt[:200], "audio/mpeg")
        else:
            send(token, chat_id, "檔案超過 Telegram 單檔 50 MB 限制，未傳送。")
        if job_settings["video"] and duration <= 1800:
            video = make_video(output, int(duration))
            if video is not None:
                send_video(token, chat_id, video, f"長曲影片 {video.name}")
        elif job_settings["video"]:
            send(token, chat_id, "長曲影片超過 30 分鐘，已略過自動轉檔。")
    except Exception as exc:
        log(f"longform job failed: {exc}")
        send(token, chat_id, f"長曲失敗：{exc}")
    finally:
        current.update(running=False, prompt="", started=0.0, process=None, cancel=False)


def merge_recent(token: str, chat_id: int, count: int) -> None:
    with recent_lock:
        files = [Path(path) for path in recent[-count:]] if count > 0 else []
    files = [path for path in files if path.is_file()]
    if len(files) < 2:
        send(token, chat_id, "可合併的近期檔案不足 2 首。")
        return
    output = OUT_DIR / f"merged_{datetime.now():%Y%m%d_%H%M%S}.mp3"
    command = [sys.executable, "-u", str(AUDIO_TOOL), "merge",
               *[str(path) for path in files], "--out", str(output), "--overwrite"]
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True, text=True,
                                encoding="utf-8", errors="replace", timeout=1800)
        if result.returncode != 0 or not output.is_file():
            send(token, chat_id, "合併失敗，請查看日誌。")
            return
        add_recent(output)
        if send_document(token, chat_id, output,
                         f"合併 {len(files)} 首（{output.stat().st_size / 1024 / 1024:.1f} MB）",
                         "audio/mpeg"):
            send(token, chat_id, f"合併完成：{output.name}")
    except Exception as exc:
        log(f"merge failed: {exc}")
        send(token, chat_id, f"合併失敗：{exc}")


def status_text() -> str:
    lines = []
    if current["running"]:
        elapsed = int(time.time() - current["started"])
        lines.append(f"執行中：{current['prompt'][:80]}（{format_duration(elapsed)}）")
    else:
        lines.append("閒置中")
    lines.append(f"長度：{format_duration(settings['duration'])}")
    lines.append(f"自動轉影片：{'開' if settings['video'] else '關'}")
    lines.append(f"淡入/淡出：{settings['fade_in']:.1f}s / {settings['fade_out']:.1f}s")
    lines.append(f"佇列：{job_queue.qsize()} 件")
    with recent_lock:
        lines.append(f"近期檔案：{len(recent)} 個")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def main() -> int:
    load_settings()
    config = load_config()
    token = str(config.get("bot_token", "")).strip()
    chat_id = int(config.get("chat_id", 0))
    if not token or not chat_id:
        raise SystemExit("telegram.json needs bot_token and chat_id")
    global TOKEN
    TOKEN = token
    threading.Thread(target=worker_loop, daemon=True).start()
    log("bot started")
    offset = 0
    while True:
        try:
            response = requests.get(
                f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{token}/getUpdates",
                params={"offset": offset, "timeout": 25},
                timeout=40,
            )
            response.raise_for_status()
            updates = response.json().get("result", [])
            for update in updates:
                offset = update["update_id"] + 1
                message = update.get("message") or {}
                text = (message.get("text") or "").strip()
                from_chat = (message.get("chat") or {}).get("id")
                if from_chat != chat_id or not text:
                    continue
                if text.startswith("/gen "):
                    prompt = text[5:].strip()
                    if not prompt:
                        send(token, chat_id, "用法：/gen <音樂描述>")
                        continue
                    snapshot = dict(settings)
                    position = job_queue.qsize() + 1
                    job_queue.put(lambda p=prompt, s=snapshot: run_job(token, chat_id, p, s))
                    send(token, chat_id, f"已排入佇列 #{position}。")
                elif text.startswith("/longform "):
                    prompt = text[10:].strip()
                    if not prompt:
                        send(token, chat_id, "用法：/longform <音樂描述>（先用 /duration 設定長度）")
                        continue
                    if settings["duration"] <= 300:
                        send(token, chat_id, "請先用 /duration 設定超過 5 分鐘的長度。")
                        continue
                    snapshot = dict(settings)
                    position = job_queue.qsize() + 1
                    job_queue.put(lambda p=prompt, s=snapshot:
                                  run_longform_job(token, chat_id, p, s))
                    send(token, chat_id, f"長曲已排入佇列 #{position}。")
                elif text.startswith("/album "):
                    payload = text[7:].strip()
                    if "|" not in payload:
                        send(token, chat_id, "用法：/album 專輯名稱 | 提示詞1 ; 提示詞2")
                        continue
                    title, prompt_text = payload.split("|", 1)
                    prompts = [p.strip() for p in prompt_text.split(";") if p.strip()]
                    if not title.strip() or not prompts:
                        send(token, chat_id, "專輯名稱或提示詞不可空白。")
                        continue
                    snapshot = dict(settings)
                    position = job_queue.qsize() + 1
                    job_queue.put(lambda t=title.strip(), p=prompts, s=snapshot:
                                  run_album_job(token, chat_id, t, p, s))
                    send(token, chat_id, f"專輯已排入佇列 #{position}。")
                elif text == "/status":
                    send(token, chat_id, status_text())
                elif text == "/cancel":
                    if not current["running"] and job_queue.empty():
                        send(token, chat_id, "目前沒有執行中的工作。")
                        continue
                    current["cancel"] = True
                    kill_process(current.get("process"))
                    cleared = 0
                    while True:
                        try:
                            job_queue.get_nowait()
                            job_queue.task_done()
                            cleared += 1
                        except queue.Empty:
                            break
                    send(token, chat_id, f"已取消目前工作，並清除 {cleared} 件佇列。")
                elif text.startswith("/duration"):
                    parts = text.split(maxsplit=1)
                    if len(parts) == 1:
                        send(token, chat_id, f"目前長度：{format_duration(settings['duration'])}")
                        continue
                    value = parse_duration(parts[1])
                    if value is None:
                        send(token, chat_id, "格式：/duration 95、1:35 或 1:02:03（10 秒–2 小時）")
                        continue
                    settings["duration"] = value
                    save_settings()
                    warning = "（注意：引擎單次生成建議不超過約 8 分鐘）" if value > 600 else ""
                    send(token, chat_id, f"長度已設為 {format_duration(value)}。{warning}")
                elif text.startswith("/video"):
                    parts = text.split(maxsplit=1)
                    if len(parts) == 2 and parts[1].lower() in ("on", "off"):
                        settings["video"] = parts[1].lower() == "on"
                        save_settings()
                    send(token, chat_id, f"自動轉影片：{'開' if settings['video'] else '關'}")
                elif text.startswith("/fade"):
                    parts = text.split()
                    if len(parts) == 2 and parts[1].lower() == "off":
                        settings["fade_in"] = 0.0
                        settings["fade_out"] = 0.0
                        save_settings()
                        send(token, chat_id, "已關閉淡入淡出。")
                        continue
                    if len(parts) == 3:
                        try:
                            fade_in = max(0.0, min(30.0, float(parts[1])))
                            fade_out = max(0.0, min(30.0, float(parts[2])))
                        except ValueError:
                            fade_in = fade_out = -1.0
                        if fade_in >= 0 and fade_out >= 0:
                            settings["fade_in"] = fade_in
                            settings["fade_out"] = fade_out
                            save_settings()
                            send(token, chat_id, f"淡入/淡出已設為 {fade_in:.1f}s / {fade_out:.1f}s。")
                            continue
                    send(token, chat_id, "格式：/fade 2 3 或 /fade off（0–30 秒）")
                elif text.startswith("/merge"):
                    parts = text.split(maxsplit=1)
                    count = 2
                    if len(parts) == 2:
                        try:
                            count = int(parts[1])
                        except ValueError:
                            count = 2
                    count = max(2, min(10, count))
                    position = job_queue.qsize() + 1
                    job_queue.put(lambda c=count: merge_recent(token, chat_id, c))
                    send(token, chat_id, f"合併已排入佇列 #{position}。")
                elif text == "/outputs":
                    with recent_lock:
                        items = list(recent[-5:])
                    if not items:
                        send(token, chat_id, "尚無近期檔案。")
                    else:
                        lines = ["近期檔案："]
                        for item in reversed(items):
                            path = Path(item)
                            if path.is_file():
                                lines.append(f"{path.name}（{path.stat().st_size / 1024 / 1024:.1f} MB）")
                        send(token, chat_id, "\n".join(lines))
                elif text in ("/start", "/help"):
                    send(token, chat_id,
                         "AI Music Maker 遠端控制\n"
                         "/gen <提示詞> 生成並回傳音訊\n"
                         "/longform <提示詞> 長曲分段＋交叉淡化\n"
                         "/album 名稱 | 提示詞1 ; 提示詞2\n"
                         "/status 狀態與設定\n"
                         "/cancel 取消目前工作\n"
                         "/duration 95｜1:35｜1:02:03 設定長度\n"
                         "/video on|off 自動轉 MP4\n"
                         "/fade 2 3｜/fade off 設定淡入淡出\n"
                         "/merge 2 合併近期檔案\n"
                         "/outputs 列出近期檔案")
        except Exception as exc:
            log(f"poll failed: {exc}")
            time.sleep(5)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) >= 2 and sys.argv[1] == "--test-gen":
        load_settings()
        cfg = load_config()
        TOKEN = str(cfg["bot_token"])
        prompt = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) >= 3 else "Warm acoustic guitar instrumental, no vocals."
        run_job(cfg["bot_token"], int(cfg["chat_id"]), prompt)
    else:
        sys.exit(main())


===== [57/66] AI_Music/scripts/test_model_load.py  (9364 bytes) =====
"""Load the official ACE-Step 1.5 model locally on the RTX 3060 and report.

Offline only: no WebUI, no API server, no desktop GUI.
Run from VS Code / PowerShell:
    <workspace>\\AI_Music.venv\\Scripts\\python.exe <workspace>\\AI_Music\\scripts\\test_model_load.py

<workspace> = G:\\AIMUSIC.  All paths are resolved from this
file's location, so the project folder can be moved without editing the script.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import gc
import json
import os
import sys
import time
import traceback
from pathlib import Path

from _common import bootstrap, human, safe_stdio



# <workspace>\AI_Music\scripts\test_model_load.py -> <workspace>\AI_Music
BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
REPO_DIR = BASE_DIR / "ACE-Step-1.5"
MODELS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "models" / "Ace-Step1.5"
LOG_DIR = BASE_DIR / "logs"

DIT_MODEL = "acestep-v15-turbo"
LM_MODEL = "acestep-5Hz-lm-1.7B"

REQUIRED_FILES = [
    "config.json",
    "vae/config.json",
    "vae/diffusion_pytorch_model.safetensors",
    "Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B/config.json",
    "Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B/model.safetensors",
    "Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B/tokenizer.json",
    "acestep-v15-turbo/config.json",
    "acestep-v15-turbo/model.safetensors",
    "acestep-v15-turbo/silence_latent.pt",
    "acestep-5Hz-lm-1.7B/config.json",
    "acestep-5Hz-lm-1.7B/model.safetensors",
    "acestep-5Hz-lm-1.7B/tokenizer.json",
]




def check_files() -> tuple[bool, list[str]]:
    missing = [rel for rel in REQUIRED_FILES if not (MODELS_DIR / rel).is_file()]
    total = sum(
        p.stat().st_size
        for p in MODELS_DIR.rglob("*")
        if p.is_file() and p.parent.name != ".cache"
    )
    print(f"[files] model dir      : {MODELS_DIR}")
    print(f"[files] on-disk size   : {human(float(total))}")
    for rel in REQUIRED_FILES:
        path = MODELS_DIR / rel
        state = human(float(path.stat().st_size)) if path.is_file() else "MISSING"
        print(f"[files]   {state:>10}  {rel}")
    return not missing, missing


def report_runtime() -> dict:
    import torch

    info: dict = {
        "python": sys.version.split()[0],
        "torch": torch.__version__,
        "torch_cuda": torch.version.cuda,
        "cuda_available": torch.cuda.is_available(),
    }
    print(f"[env] python           : {info['python']}")
    print(f"[env] torch            : {info['torch']} (cuda {info['torch_cuda']})")
    print(f"[env] cuda available   : {info['cuda_available']}")
    if not info["cuda_available"]:
        return info

    index = torch.cuda.current_device()
    props = torch.cuda.get_device_properties(index)
    free_bytes, total_bytes = torch.cuda.mem_get_info(index)
    info.update(
        {
            "gpu_name": torch.cuda.get_device_name(index),
            "compute_capability": f"{props.major}.{props.minor}",
            "total_vram_gb": round(total_bytes / 1024**3, 2),
            "free_vram_gb": round(free_bytes / 1024**3, 2),
            "bf16_supported": torch.cuda.is_bf16_supported(),
        }
    )
    print(f"[gpu] device           : {index} - {info['gpu_name']}")
    print(f"[gpu] compute cap.     : {info['compute_capability']}")
    print(f"[gpu] VRAM total/free  : {info['total_vram_gb']} GB / {info['free_vram_gb']} GB")
    print(f"[gpu] bf16 supported   : {info['bf16_supported']}")
    return info


def report_plan() -> dict:
    import torch

    from acestep import gpu_config

    cfg = gpu_config.get_gpu_config()
    plan = {
        "tier": cfg.tier,
        "recommended_lm": cfg.recommended_lm_model,
        "offload_to_cpu": cfg.offload_to_cpu_default,
        "offload_dit_to_cpu": cfg.offload_dit_to_cpu_default,
        "max_duration_with_lm": cfg.max_duration_with_lm,
        "max_batch_size_with_lm": cfg.max_batch_size_with_lm,
        "detected_vram_gb": cfg.gpu_memory_gb,
    }
    print(f"[plan] gpu tier        : {plan['tier']} ({plan['detected_vram_gb']} GB detected)")
    print(f"[plan] max duration/LM : {plan['max_duration_with_lm']} s")
    print(f"[plan] max batch w/ LM : {plan['max_batch_size_with_lm']}")
    print(
        f"[plan] offload defaults: to_cpu={plan['offload_to_cpu']} "
        f"dit_to_cpu={plan['offload_dit_to_cpu']}"
    )
    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        _, total = torch.cuda.mem_get_info()
        plan["detected_vram_gb"] = round(total / 1024**3, 2)
    return plan


def main() -> int:
    safe_stdio()
    LOG_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    bootstrap()

    status: dict = {"ok": False, "stage": "start", "errors": []}
    try:
        print("=" * 72)
        print("ACE-Step 1.5 - local model load test (offline, no WebUI / no API)")
        print("=" * 72)

        files_ok, missing = check_files()
        status["missing_files"] = missing
        if not files_ok:
            status["stage"] = "file check"
            print(f"[files] FAILED - missing: {missing}")
            return 1

        status["stage"] = "runtime"
        runtime = report_runtime()
        status["runtime"] = runtime
        if not runtime["cuda_available"]:
            print("[gpu] FAILED - CUDA is not available, aborting (GPU-only setup).")
            return 1

        status["stage"] = "plan"
        status["plan"] = report_plan()

        import torch

        from acestep.handler import AceStepHandler
        from acestep.llm_inference import LLMHandler

        torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats()

        status["stage"] = "dit"
        print("-" * 72)
        print(f"[dit] loading {DIT_MODEL} ...")
        t0 = time.time()
        dit_handler = AceStepHandler()
        message, success = dit_handler.initialize_service(
            project_root=str(REPO_DIR),
            config_path=DIT_MODEL,
            device="auto",
            use_flash_attention=False,
            compile_model=False,
            offload_to_cpu=True,
            offload_dit_to_cpu=True,
            quantization=None,
        )
        status["dit_seconds"] = round(time.time() - t0, 1)
        status["dit_message"] = message
        if not success:
            print(f"[dit] FAILED: {message}")
            return 1
        params = sum(p.numel() for p in dit_handler.model.parameters())
        status["dit_params_b"] = round(params / 1e9, 3)
        status["sample_rate"] = dit_handler.sample_rate
        print(f"[dit] loaded in {status['dit_seconds']}s - dtype={dit_handler.dtype}")
        print(f"[dit] params           : {status['dit_params_b']} B")
        print(f"[dit] sample rate      : {dit_handler.sample_rate} Hz")
        print(f"[dit] vae device       : {next(dit_handler.vae.parameters()).device}")

        status["stage"] = "lm"
        print("-" * 72)
        print(f"[lm] loading {LM_MODEL} (backend=pt) ...")
        t0 = time.time()
        llm_handler = LLMHandler()
        message, success = llm_handler.initialize(
            checkpoint_dir=str(MODELS_DIR),
            lm_model_path=LM_MODEL,
            backend="pt",
            device="auto",
            offload_to_cpu=True,
            dtype=None,
        )
        status["lm_seconds"] = round(time.time() - t0, 1)
        status["lm_message"] = message
        if not success:
            print(f"[lm] FAILED: {message}")
            return 1
        status["lm_dtype"] = str(llm_handler.dtype)
        status["lm_backend"] = str(llm_handler.llm_backend)
        print(f"[lm] loaded in {status['lm_seconds']}s - backend={status['lm_backend']}")
        print(f"[lm] dtype/device      : {status['lm_dtype']} / {llm_handler.device}")

        peak = torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() / 1024**3
        reserved = torch.cuda.max_memory_reserved() / 1024**3
        status["peak_vram_gb"] = round(peak, 2)
        status["reserved_vram_gb"] = round(reserved, 2)
        print("-" * 72)
        print(f"[vram] peak allocated  : {peak:.2f} GB")
        print(f"[vram] peak reserved   : {reserved:.2f} GB")

        status["stage"] = "teardown"
        print("[teardown] releasing models and clearing the CUDA cache ...")
        del dit_handler, llm_handler
        gc.collect()
        if torch.cuda.is_available():
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
            torch.cuda.synchronize()
            free_bytes, total_bytes = torch.cuda.mem_get_info()
            status["free_vram_after_gb"] = round(free_bytes / 1024**3, 2)
            total_gb = round(total_bytes / 1024**3, 2)
            print(f"[teardown] VRAM free   : {status['free_vram_after_gb']} / {total_gb} GB")

        status["ok"] = True
        status["stage"] = "done"
        print("=" * 72)
        print("RESULT: model load OK (this script generates no audio)")
        print("=" * 72)
        return 0
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - report the exact blocking step
        status["errors"].append(f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
        print(f"[error] stage='{status['stage']}' {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
        traceback.print_exc()
        return 1
    finally:
        (LOG_DIR / "model_load_report.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(status, indent=2, default=str), encoding="utf-8"
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())


===== [58/66] AI_Music/scripts/write_setup_report.py  (6578 bytes) =====
"""Compose <workspace>\\AI_Music\\logs\\setup_report.txt from the run reports.

<workspace> = G:\\AIMUSIC (all paths are resolved from this
file's location, so the project folder can be moved without editing the script).

Reads logs/model_load_report.json + logs/generate_report.json (written by
test_model_load.py / generate_test.py) and the live environment, then writes a
single human-readable summary.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from _common import human, safe_stdio

# <workspace>\AI_Music\scripts\write_setup_report.py -> <workspace>\AI_Music
BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
MODELS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "models" / "Ace-Step1.5"
LOG_DIR = BASE_DIR / "logs"
TARGET_WAV = BASE_DIR / "generated" / "test_lofi_01.wav"
REPORT_PATH = LOG_DIR / "setup_report.txt"




def read_json(path: Path) -> dict:
    try:
        return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception:
        return {}


def dir_stats(path: Path) -> tuple[int, float]:
    files = [p for p in path.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and p.parent.name != ".cache"]
    return len(files), float(sum(p.stat().st_size for p in files))


WEIGHTS = (
    "acestep-v15-turbo/model.safetensors",
    "acestep-5Hz-lm-1.7B/model.safetensors",
    "Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B/model.safetensors",
    "vae/diffusion_pytorch_model.safetensors",
)


def sha_status(verified: dict) -> str:
    ok = 0
    for rel in WEIGHTS:
        path = MODELS_DIR / rel
        entry = verified.get(rel) if isinstance(verified, dict) else None
        if not path.is_file() or not isinstance(entry, dict):
            continue
        stat = path.stat()
        if (int(entry.get("size", -1)) == int(stat.st_size)
                and int(entry.get("mtime", -1)) == int(stat.st_mtime)
                and entry.get("sha256")):
            ok += 1
    if ok == len(WEIGHTS):
        return f"已驗證 ({ok}/{len(WEIGHTS)})"
    if ok:
        return f"部分驗證 ({ok}/{len(WEIGHTS)})"
    return "未驗證"


def main() -> int:
    safe_stdio()
    load = read_json(LOG_DIR / "model_load_report.json")
    gen = read_json(LOG_DIR / "generate_report.json")
    runtime = load.get("runtime", {})
    plan = load.get("plan", {})
    audio = gen.get("audio", {})
    verified = read_json(MODELS_DIR / ".verified.json").get("files", {})
    weights_sha = sha_status(verified)
    gpu_title = runtime.get("gpu_name") or "GPU"

    files, total = dir_stats(MODELS_DIR)
    lines: list[str] = []
    add = lines.append

    add("ACE-Step 1.5 — local offline setup report")
    add("=" * 68)
    add(f"generated by : {Path(__file__).name}")
    add("")
    add("[1] Model location and size")
    add(f"  model dir        : {MODELS_DIR}")
    add(f"  DiT model        : acestep-v15-turbo (official ACE-Step/Ace-Step1.5)")
    add(f"  LM model         : acestep-5Hz-lm-1.7B (official)")
    add(f"  text encoder     : Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B (official)")
    add(f"  VAE              : acestep VAE (official)")
    add(f"  files on disk    : {files}")
    add(f"  total size       : {human(total)}")
    add(f"  source           : huggingface.co/ACE-Step/Ace-Step1.5 (official, {weights_sha})")
    add("")
    add("[2] Python / PyTorch / CUDA")
    add(f"  python           : {runtime.get('python', sys.version.split()[0])}")
    add(f"  python exe       : {sys.executable}")
    add(f"  torch            : {runtime.get('torch', 'n/a')}")
    add(f"  torch CUDA build : {runtime.get('torch_cuda', 'n/a')}")
    add(f"  cuda available   : {runtime.get('cuda_available', 'n/a')}")
    add("")
    add(f"[3] {gpu_title} status")
    add(f"  gpu              : {runtime.get('gpu_name', 'n/a')}")
    add(f"  compute cap.     : {runtime.get('compute_capability', 'n/a')}")
    add(f"  total VRAM       : {runtime.get('total_vram_gb', 'n/a')} GB")
    add(f"  free VRAM at start: {runtime.get('free_vram_gb', 'n/a')} GB")
    add(f"  bf16 supported   : {runtime.get('bf16_supported', 'n/a')}")
    add(f"  gpu tier (auto)  : {plan.get('tier', 'n/a')} / max duration with LM "
        f"{plan.get('max_duration_with_lm', 'n/a')} s")
    add(f"  peak VRAM (load) : {load.get('peak_vram_gb', 'n/a')} GB "
        f"(reserved {load.get('reserved_vram_gb', 'n/a')} GB)")
    add("")
    add("[4] Model load test (test_model_load.py)")
    add(f"  result           : {'SUCCESS' if load.get('ok') else 'FAILED / not run'}")
    add(f"  blocking step    : {load.get('stage', 'n/a')}")
    add(f"  DiT load         : {load.get('dit_seconds', 'n/a')} s — {load.get('dit_message', '')}")
    add(f"  DiT params       : {load.get('dit_params_b', 'n/a')} B")
    add(f"  LM load          : {load.get('lm_seconds', 'n/a')} s — {load.get('lm_message', '')}")
    add(f"  sample rate      : {load.get('sample_rate', 'n/a')} Hz")
    if load.get("errors"):
        add(f"  errors           : {load['errors']}")
    add("")
    add("[5] Music generation test (generate_test.py)")
    add(f"  result           : {'SUCCESS' if gen.get('ok') else 'FAILED / not run'}")
    add(f"  blocking step    : {gen.get('stage', 'n/a')}")
    add(f"  generation time  : {gen.get('generate_seconds', 'n/a')} s")
    add(f"  prompt           : Lo-fi chill instrumental, 78 BPM, 180 s, WAV")
    add(f"  output wav       : {TARGET_WAV}")
    if TARGET_WAV.is_file():
        add(f"  wav exists       : yes ({human(float(TARGET_WAV.stat().st_size))})")
    else:
        add("  wav exists       : no")
    if audio:
        add(f"  wav properties   : {audio.get('sample_rate')} Hz, {audio.get('channels')} ch, "
            f"{audio.get('duration_sec')} s, peak {audio.get('peak')}, rms {audio.get('rms')}")
    if gen.get("errors"):
        add(f"  errors           : {gen['errors']}")
    add("")
    add("[6] Runtime mode")
    add("  offline          : HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1, TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1")
    add(f"  checkpoints env  : ACESTEP_CHECKPOINTS_DIR={MODELS_DIR}")
    add("  webui / api srv  : NOT used (no gradio, no fastapi, no localhost server)")
    add("  device policy    : CUDA (RTX 3060), CPU offload for VAE/text encoder, LM backend='pt'")
    add("")

    REPORT_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    REPORT_PATH.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print("\n".join(lines))
    print(f"[report] written to {REPORT_PATH}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())


===== [59/66] AI_Music/scripts/youtube_upload.py  (8306 bytes) =====
"""Upload a rendered video to YouTube as private (OAuth installed-app flow).

Prerequisites:
  %APPDATA%\\AIMusicGenerator\\youtube_client.json  (Google OAuth client, Desktop app)
  python scripts/youtube_upload.py --auth
  python scripts/youtube_upload.py --video out.mp4 --metadata out.youtube.json
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import secrets
import sys
import time
import urllib.parse
import webbrowser
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
from pathlib import Path

import requests

APP_DIR = Path(os.environ.get("APPDATA", str(Path.home()))) / "AIMusicGenerator"
CLIENT_FILE = APP_DIR / "youtube_client.json"
TOKEN_FILE = APP_DIR / "youtube_token.json"
SCOPES = ("https://www.googleapis.com/auth/youtube.upload "
          "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/youtube.force-ssl")
TOKEN_URL = "https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token"
AUTH_URL = "https://accounts.google.com/o/oauth2/v2/auth"
UPLOAD_URL = ("https://www.googleapis.com/upload/youtube/v3/videos"
              "?uploadType=resumable&part=snippet,status")


def load_client() -> dict:
    if not CLIENT_FILE.is_file():
        raise SystemExit(f"missing {CLIENT_FILE}")
    data = json.loads(CLIENT_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return data.get("installed") or data.get("web") or data


def load_token() -> dict | None:
    if not TOKEN_FILE.is_file():
        return None
    try:
        return json.loads(TOKEN_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return None


def save_token(token: dict) -> None:
    APP_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    TOKEN_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(token, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
                          encoding="utf-8")


def authorize() -> int:
    client = load_client()
    result = {}

    class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
        def do_GET(self):
            query = urllib.parse.urlparse(self.path).query
            params = urllib.parse.parse_qs(query)
            result["code"] = (params.get("code") or [""])[0]
            self.send_response(200)
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write("授權完成，可以關閉這個視窗。".encode("utf-8"))

        def log_message(self, fmt, *args):
            pass

    server = HTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)
    port = server.server_port
    redirect = f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/"
    state = secrets.token_urlsafe(16)
    query = urllib.parse.urlencode({
        "client_id": client["client_id"],
        "redirect_uri": redirect,
        "response_type": "code",
        "scope": SCOPES,
        "access_type": "offline",
        "prompt": "consent",
        "state": state,
    })
    print("Opening browser for Google authorization...")
    webbrowser.open(AUTH_URL + "?" + query)
    server.handle_request()
    code = result.get("code")
    if not code:
        print("[error] no authorization code")
        return 1
    response = requests.post(TOKEN_URL, data={
        "code": code,
        "client_id": client["client_id"],
        "client_secret": client["client_secret"],
        "redirect_uri": redirect,
        "grant_type": "authorization_code",
    }, timeout=60)
    if response.status_code != 200:
        print("[error] token exchange:", response.text)
        return 1
    token = response.json()
    token["expires_at"] = time.time() + int(token.get("expires_in", 3600))
    save_token(token)
    print(f"[ok] token saved to {TOKEN_FILE}")
    return 0


def access_token() -> str:
    client = load_client()
    token = load_token()
    if not token:
        raise SystemExit("not authorized; run --auth first")
    if token.get("expires_at", 0) > time.time() + 60:
        return token["access_token"]
    refresh = token.get("refresh_token")
    if not refresh:
        raise SystemExit("token expired and no refresh_token; run --auth again")
    response = requests.post(TOKEN_URL, data={
        "client_id": client["client_id"],
        "client_secret": client["client_secret"],
        "refresh_token": refresh,
        "grant_type": "refresh_token",
    }, timeout=60)
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise SystemExit("refresh failed: " + response.text)
    refreshed = response.json()
    token["access_token"] = refreshed["access_token"]
    token["expires_at"] = time.time() + int(refreshed.get("expires_in", 3600))
    save_token(token)
    return token["access_token"]


def load_metadata(video: Path, metadata_path: Path | None) -> dict:
    if metadata_path is None:
        metadata_path = video.with_suffix(".youtube.json")
    if not metadata_path.is_file():
        raise SystemExit(f"missing metadata: {metadata_path}")
    data = json.loads(metadata_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    title = str(data.get("title") or video.stem)[:100]
    description = str(data.get("description") or "")[:5000]
    tags = [str(tag) for tag in (data.get("tags") or [])][:30]
    return {
        "snippet": {
            "title": title,
            "description": description,
            "tags": tags,
            "categoryId": str(data.get("categoryId") or "10"),
        },
        "status": {
            "privacyStatus": str(data.get("privacyStatus") or "private"),
            "madeForKids": bool(data.get("madeForKids", False)),
        },
    }


def upload(video: Path, metadata: dict, thumbnail: Path | None) -> int:
    token = access_token()
    size = video.stat().st_size
    headers = {
        "Authorization": "Bearer " + token,
        "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=UTF-8",
        "X-Upload-Content-Type": "video/mp4",
        "X-Upload-Content-Length": str(size),
    }
    start = requests.post(UPLOAD_URL, headers=headers,
                          data=json.dumps(metadata), timeout=60)
    if start.status_code != 200:
        print("[error] upload init:", start.status_code, start.text)
        return 1
    location = start.headers.get("Location")
    if not location:
        print("[error] no resumable Location header")
        return 1
    with open(video, "rb") as handle:
        response = requests.put(location, data=handle, headers={
            "Content-Type": "video/mp4",
            "Content-Length": str(size),
        }, timeout=1800)
    if response.status_code not in (200, 201):
        print("[error] upload:", response.status_code, response.text)
        return 1
    result = response.json()
    video_id = result.get("id")
    print(f"[ok] uploaded https://youtu.be/{video_id}")
    if thumbnail is not None and thumbnail.is_file() and video_id:
        with open(thumbnail, "rb") as handle:
            response = requests.post(
                "https://www.googleapis.com/upload/youtube/v3/thumbnails/set",
                params={"videoId": video_id},
                headers={"Authorization": "Bearer " + token},
                files={"image": (thumbnail.name, handle, "image/png")},
                timeout=120)
        print("[thumb]", response.status_code)
    return 0


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--auth", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--video", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--metadata", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--thumbnail", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--privacy", choices=("private", "unlisted", "public"))
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.auth:
        return authorize()
    if args.video is None:
        parser.error("--video is required")
    if not args.video.is_file():
        raise SystemExit(f"missing video: {args.video}")
    metadata = load_metadata(args.video, args.metadata)
    if args.privacy:
        metadata["status"]["privacyStatus"] = args.privacy
    if args.dry_run:
        print(json.dumps(metadata, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
        print(f"[dry-run] video={args.video} bytes={args.video.stat().st_size}")
        return 0
    return upload(args.video, metadata, args.thumbnail)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

class NormalizeConfigTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.tmp.cleanup)
        self.base = {
            "caption": "test caption",
            "lyrics": "[Instrumental]",
            "instrumental": True,
            "thinking": False,
            "bpm": 78,
            "duration": 60,
            "inference_steps": 8,
            "guidance_scale": 1.0,
            "output_dir": self.tmp.name,
        }

    def _config(self, **changes):
        data = dict(self.base)
        data.update(changes)
        path = Path(self.tmp.name) / "cfg.json"
        path.write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")
        return backend.load_config(path)

    def test_bpm_boundaries(self):
        for value in (30, 300):
            self.assertEqual(self._config(bpm=value)["bpm"], value)
        for value in (29, 301):
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                self._config(bpm=value)
        self.assertIsNone(self._config(bpm=None)["bpm"])

    def test_duration_boundaries(self):
        for value in (10, 7200):
            self.assertEqual(self._config(duration=value)["duration"], float(value))
        for value in (9, 7201):
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                self._config(duration=value)

    def test_long_thinking_requires_opt_in(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            self._config(thinking=True, duration=481)
        cfg = self._config(thinking=True, duration=481, allow_long_thinking=True)
        self.assertEqual(cfg["duration"], 481.0)

    def test_caption_must_not_be_blank(self):
        for value in ("", "   "):
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                self._config(caption=value)

    def test_auto_music_fields(self):
        cfg = self._config(keyscale="", timesignature="")
        self.assertEqual(cfg["keyscale"], "")
        self.assertEqual(cfg["timesignature"], "")

    def test_audio_format_and_offload(self):
        self.assertEqual(self._config(audio_format="flac")["audio_format"], "flac")
        self.assertEqual(self._config(offload_mode="save_vram")["offload_mode"], "save_vram")
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            self._config(audio_format="ogg")
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            self._config(offload_mode="never")

                    mock.patch.object(backend, "load_handlers", return_value=(None, None)), \
                    mock.patch.object(backend, "release_models"), \
                    mock.patch.object(backend, "run_job", side_effect=fake_job):

                    mock.patch.object(backend, "load_handlers", return_value=(None, None)), \
                    mock.patch.object(backend, "release_models"), \
                    mock.patch.object(backend, "run_job", run_job):
                code = backend.run_album(path)

            self.assertEqual(meta["invalid"][0]["index"], 2)
            self.assertEqual(run_job.call_count, 1)

    def test_all_invalid_skips_model_load(self):
        from unittest import mock

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            out = Path(tmp)
            path = self._album_file(out, [{"title": "Bad", "duration": 7201}])
            load = mock.MagicMock()
            acquire = mock.MagicMock()

                    mock.patch.object(backend, "load_handlers", load):
                code = backend.run_album(path)
            self.assertEqual(code, 1)
            load.assert_not_called()
            acquire.assert_not_called()

            self.assertEqual(meta["invalid"][0]["index"], 1)

        from unittest import mock

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            out = Path(tmp)

                {"title": "One", "duration": 30},
                {"title": "Two", "duration": 30},

            def fake_job(dit, llm, cfg):
                calls.append(cfg["filename_prefix"])
                if len(calls) == 1:
                    target = out / "01_One.mp3"
                    target.write_bytes(b"audio")
                    return 0, target
                raise RuntimeError("boom")

            with mock.patch.object(backend, "acquire_gpu", return_value=mock.MagicMock()), \
                    mock.patch.object(backend, "load_handlers", return_value=(None, None)), \
                    mock.patch.object(backend, "release_models"), \
                    mock.patch.object(backend, "run_job", side_effect=fake_job):
                code = backend.run_album(path)
            self.assertEqual(code, 1)
            meta = json.loads((out / "album.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            self.assertEqual(len(meta["tracks"]), 1)
            self.assertEqual(meta["invalid"][0]["index"], 2)


class UniqueTargetTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_duplicate_names_increment(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            out = Path(tmp)
            first = backend.unique_target(out, "ace", 42, "wav")
            first.write_bytes(b"x")
            second = backend.unique_target(out, "ace", 42, "wav")
            self.assertNotEqual(first, second)
            second.write_bytes(b"x")
            third = backend.unique_target(out, "ace", 42, "wav")
            self.assertNotEqual(second, third)
            self.assertEqual(len({first, second, third}), 3)

    def test_extension_is_honoured(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            target = backend.unique_target(Path(tmp), "ace", 7, "flac")
            self.assertEqual(target.suffix, ".flac")

    unittest.main()


===== [61/66] AI_Music/tests/test_gpu_lock.py  (3585 bytes) =====
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest import mock

BASE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
APP = BASE / "app"
sys.path.insert(0, str(APP))

import generate_backend as backend  # noqa: E402
from gpu_lock import GpuBusy, GpuLockError, acquire  # noqa: E402

backend._stdio()


class GpuLockTests(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.lock_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="aimusic_gpu_lock_")
        cls.previous = os.environ.get("AIMUSIC_GPU_LOCK_DIR")
        os.environ["AIMUSIC_GPU_LOCK_DIR"] = cls.lock_dir

    @classmethod
    def tearDownClass(cls):
        if cls.previous is None:
            os.environ.pop("AIMUSIC_GPU_LOCK_DIR", None)
        else:
            os.environ["AIMUSIC_GPU_LOCK_DIR"] = cls.previous
        shutil.rmtree(cls.lock_dir, ignore_errors=True)

    def _hold_lock(self):
        script = (
            "import sys,time;"
            f"sys.path.insert(0, {str(APP)!r});"
            "from gpu_lock import acquire;"
            "lock=acquire('test-holder');"
            "print('locked', flush=True);"
            "time.sleep(30)"
        )
        process = subprocess.Popen(
            [sys.executable, "-c", script],
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
            stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
            text=True,
            encoding="utf-8",
            errors="replace",
            env=os.environ.copy(),
        )
        line = process.stdout.readline().strip()
        if line != "locked":
            self.fail("lock holder failed: " + process.stderr.read())
        return process

    def _stop(self, process):
        process.kill()
        try:
            process.wait(timeout=10)
        finally:
            for stream in (process.stdout, process.stderr):
                try:
                    stream.close()
                except Exception:
                    pass

    def test_filesystem_error_is_not_reported_as_busy(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="aimusic_lock_error_") as tmp:
            blocker = Path(tmp) / "not_a_directory"
            blocker.write_text("x", encoding="utf-8")
            previous = os.environ.get("AIMUSIC_GPU_LOCK_DIR")
            os.environ["AIMUSIC_GPU_LOCK_DIR"] = str(blocker / "child")
            try:
                with self.assertRaises(GpuLockError):
                    acquire("probe", 0)
            finally:
                if previous is None:
                    os.environ.pop("AIMUSIC_GPU_LOCK_DIR", None)
                else:
                    os.environ["AIMUSIC_GPU_LOCK_DIR"] = previous

    def test_lock_is_cross_process_and_released_on_death(self):
        process = self._hold_lock()
        try:
            with self.assertRaises(GpuBusy):
                acquire("main", wait_seconds=0)
        finally:
            self._stop(process)
        lock = acquire("after-death", wait_seconds=5)
        lock.release()

    def test_oneshot_returns_75_without_loading_handlers(self):
        process = self._hold_lock()
        load = mock.MagicMock()
        try:
            with mock.patch.object(backend, "load_handlers", load):
                code = backend.run_oneshot({"offload_mode": "auto"}, gpu_wait=0)
        finally:
            self._stop(process)
        self.assertEqual(code, 75)
        load.assert_not_called()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

import longform_generate as longform  # noqa: E402


def make_album(count: int, seed: int | None = 100) -> tuple[dict, int]:
    base = {"caption": "test longform caption", "duration": 60}
    if seed is not None:
        base["seed"] = seed
    return longform.build_album(base, count * 175.0, 180.0, 5.0,
                                Path("unused"))


class SplitBatchTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_splits_into_groups_of_three(self):
        album, count = make_album(7)
        self.assertEqual(count, 7)
        batches = longform.split_batches(album, 3)
        self.assertEqual([len(group) for group in batches], [3, 3, 1])

    def test_zero_keeps_single_batch(self):
        album, count = make_album(5)
        self.assertEqual([len(longform.split_batches(album, 0)[0])], [count])

    def test_large_size_keeps_single_batch(self):
        album, _ = make_album(5)
        self.assertEqual(len(longform.split_batches(album, 10)), 1)


class SeedTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_each_chunk_gets_next_seed(self):
        album, count = make_album(4, seed=500)
        seeds = [track["seed"] for track in album["tracks"]]
        self.assertEqual(seeds, [500, 501, 502, 503])
        self.assertEqual(len(seeds), count)

    def test_random_seed_is_not_forced(self):
        album, _ = make_album(3, seed=-1)
        self.assertTrue(all("seed" not in track for track in album["tracks"]))

    def test_prompt_stays_seamless(self):
        album, count = make_album(3)
        self.assertIn("intro", album["tracks"][0]["prompt"])
        self.assertIn("outro", album["tracks"][-1]["prompt"])
        self.assertIn("seamless continuation", album["tracks"][1]["prompt"])

class VideoStyleTests(unittest.TestCase):
