        graph = video.build_filter("cover", 1920, 1080, 24)
        self.assertIn("crop=1920:1080", graph)
        self.assertNotIn("zoompan", graph)

    def test_kenburns_uses_zoompan_with_fps(self):
        graph = video.build_filter("kenburns", 1280, 720, 24)
        self.assertIn("zoompan=", graph)
        self.assertIn("s=1280x720:fps=24", graph)
        self.assertNotIn("noise", graph)

    def test_ambient_adds_grain_and_vignette(self):
        graph = video.build_filter("ambient", 1280, 720, 24)
        self.assertIn("zoompan=", graph)
        self.assertIn("noise=alls=3", graph)
        self.assertIn("vignette", graph)

    def test_blur_and_waveform_unchanged(self):
        self.assertIn("gblur", video.build_filter("blur", 1920, 1080))
        waveform = video.build_filter("waveform", 1920, 1080)
        self.assertIn("[2:v]", waveform)
        self.assertIn("gblur", waveform)

===== [64/66] AI_Music/YOUTUBE_AUTO_UPLOAD_PLAN.md  (5981 bytes) =====
# YouTube 自動上傳流程與條件（規劃，尚未開發）

更新日期：2026-11-17

## 1. 目標與邊界

在「音樂生成 → 封面 → MP4」完成後，自動把影片上傳到指定 YouTube 頻道，並設定標題、說明、標籤、縮圖、播放清單與隱私狀態。

本階段只做流程與條件規劃，不建立 Google Cloud 專案、不寫 API 程式、不上傳任何影片。

## 2. 前置條件

- Google Cloud 專案已啟用 **YouTube Data API v3**。
- 建立 **OAuth 2.0 用戶端（Desktop app）**，使用 `https://www.googleapis.com/auth/youtube.upload` 權限。
- 首次授權需人工在瀏覽器完成；refresh token 以 Windows DPAPI 加密保存在本機，不進 Git。
- 目標頻道已完成 Google 帳號驗證；未驗證帳號有 15 分鐘影片長度限制。
- 頻道沒有版權警告、社群規範警告或上傳限制。
- OAuth 憑證、頻道 ID、播放清單 ID 由使用者設定，不寫死在程式。

## 3. 建議流程

1. 音樂生成成功（exit 0）且輸出檔存在。
2. 封面完成（SD 封面或使用者選定封面）。
3. 自動轉出 MP4，且通過影片驗證。
4. 產生 metadata：
   - 標題
   - 說明
   - 標籤
   - 分類（Music）
   - 語言
   - 是否為兒童內容
   - 預設隱私狀態
5. 產生 1280×720 縮圖（JPG/PNG，小於 2 MB）。
6. 上傳前檢查（見第 4 節）。
7. 以 resumable upload 上傳影片。
8. 上傳成功後設定縮圖、播放清單、字幕（可選）。
9. 記錄 video ID、狀態、時間、錯誤；必要時重試。
10. 依照設定：私人待審、不公開測試、或排程公開。

## 4. 上傳前必要條件（Gate）

全部通過才可上傳：

- 生成與轉檔流程 exit code 為 0。
- MP4 存在、大小大於 1 MB、可被 ffprobe 解析。
- 影片為 H.264 + AAC、yuv420p、1920×1080 或 1080×1920、fps ≥ 1（目前預設 5 fps 靜態畫面）。
- 音軌存在且長度與音檔誤差小於 2 秒。
- 標題 1–100 字元；說明不超過 5,000 字元；標籤合計不超過 500 字元。
- 縮圖存在、1280×720、檔案小於 2 MB。
- 沒有相同內容雜湊的重複上傳紀錄（可用 `video_id` 或 SHA-256 比對）。
- OAuth token 可用；必要時可自動 refresh。
- 當日 API quota 足夠。
- 使用者已勾選「自動上傳」並選定頻道與隱私狀態。
- 若設定為公開，需人工確認或符合排程規則。

## 5. Metadata 規則

- 標題格式建議：`歌名 | 風格 / 用途`，避免誇大與關鍵字堆疊。
- 說明：
  - 第一段說明音樂風格與情境。
  - 第二段列曲目或時間軸。
  - 第三段放版權與授權聲明。
  - 若有 AI 生成內容，依 YouTube 規範揭露。
- 標籤：風格、樂器、情境、用途；避免誤導與商標濫用。
- 分類：Music。
- 語言：依頻道主要語言設定。
- 兒童內容：預設「否」，不可預設為兒童導向。
- 隱私：預設「私人」或「不公開」，確認後再公開。

## 6. 縮圖

- 1280×720，JPG/PNG，小於 2 MB。
- 以封面為主視覺，加上可讀的大標題。
- 避免文字被裁切；重要元素置中。
- 每次上傳前檢查檔案格式與大小。

## 7. Quota 與頻率

- YouTube Data API 每日預設 quota：10,000 units。
- 每次影片上傳約 1,600 units。
- 實務上每天最多約 6 次上傳，还需保留其他 API 呼叫的額度。
- 建議：
  - 測試期每天 1 次（不公開）。
  - 穩定期每天 1–3 次。
  - 超過額度時排入隔日佇列，不重試到失敗為止。

## 8. 狀態與重試

建議狀態：

`pending → validated → uploading → uploaded → thumbnail_set → playlist_set → published / failed`

- 上傳採 resumable，網路中斷可續傳。
- 4xx（權限、配額、參數）不自動重試，記錄原因。
- 5xx 與網路錯誤最多重試 3 次，採指數退避。
- 每次重試都使用同一支影片與同一份 metadata，避免重複上傳。
- 已上傳但設定縮圖失敗時，只重試設定步驟，不重新上傳。

## 9. 建議程式結構（未來）

- `AI_Music/scripts/youtube_auth.py`：OAuth 授權與 token 更新。
- `AI_Music/scripts/youtube_upload.py`：驗證、上傳、設定縮圖與播放清單。
- `AI_Music/scripts/youtube_queue.py`：佇列與狀態保存（JSON 或 SQLite）。
- `AI_Music/app/src/YouTubeUploader.cs` 或呼叫 Python 的 Runner。
- App 設定新增：啟用自動上傳、頻道、預設隱私、播放清單、標題模板。
- 憑證位置：`%APPDATA%\AIMusicGenerator\youtube_token.bin`（DPAPI 加密）。

## 10. 安全與合規

- 不把 client secret、refresh token 寫進程式或 Git。
- 只要求 `youtube.upload` 權限，不要求完整帳號權限。
- 上傳前檢查音樂與封面沒有第三方版權素材。
- 遵守 YouTube 垃圾內容、重複內容、誤導 metadata 與 AI 內容揭露規範。
- 保留上傳紀錄與原始檔案，方便申訴或刪除。

## 11. 測試計畫

1. 使用測試頻道，上傳 10 秒測試影片為「私人」。
2. 驗證 video ID、標題、說明、標籤、縮圖、播放清單。
3. 測試 token 過期、quota 不足、網路中斷。
4. 測試重複檔案不重複上傳。
5. 測試 8 分鐘與長曲（若引擎支援）之上傳。
6. 確認刪除測試影片與清理紀錄。

## 12. 分階段建議

- 階段 1：手動上傳，工具只產生 metadata 與縮圖。
- 階段 2：半自動，App 按鈕上傳為私人，人工確認後公開。
- 階段 3：全自動，通過 Gate 後排程上傳與發布。

## 13. 待決定事項

- 預設隱私：私人、不公開或公開。
- 是否啟用排程發布與時區。
- 是否固定播放清單與頻道。
- 標題與說明的固定模板。
- AI 內容揭露文字。
- 測試頻道與正式頻道是否分開。


===== [65/66] migrate_venv_paths.ps1  (9523 bytes) =====
# Repair the two ACE-Step virtual environments after moving the project to a new
# absolute path.  uv builds Windows launchers that embed ABSOLUTE paths, so a
# moved venv cannot start until the launchers are rebuilt.
#
# What it does
#   1. re-creates both venvs in place with `uv venv --relocatable --allow-existing`
#      (keeps Lib\site-packages, replaces the path-bound python.exe launchers)
#   2. rewrites any remaining text file that still contains the old paths
#      (`Scripts\activate*`, `pyvenv.cfg`, ...)
#   3. re-installs the packages that own console scripts so their .exe wrappers
#      (hf.exe, torchrun.exe, ...) are regenerated with the new paths
#   4. verifies python + torch + CUDA and runs the .exe wrappers
#
# Usage (run right after the workspace has been moved to a new absolute path):
#   powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "G:\AIMUSIC\migrate_venv_paths.ps1"
#   ... -OldRoot 'D:\VS CODE\PROJECT\AIMUSIC\AI_Music'   # previous location, used for the text rewrite
#   ... -SkipReinstall              # only fix the interpreters, keep wrappers as-is
#
# Before this script, run retarget_workspace_paths.ps1: it rewrites the absolute
# paths in the freeze files, so the wrapper re-install below regenerates the
# console scripts straight from the new wheel location.

param(
    [string]$Root = 'G:\AIMUSIC',
    [string]$OldRoot = 'D:\VS CODE\PROJECT\AIMUSIC\AI_Music',
    [string]$OldVenv = 'D:\VS CODE\PROJECT\AIMUSIC\AI_Music.venv',
    [switch]$SkipReinstall
)

# NOTE: keep this on Continue.  uv writes its progress lines to stderr, and with
# $ErrorActionPreference='Stop' PowerShell turns those into NativeCommandError
# exceptions that abort the script even though uv exited 0.  Exit codes are
# checked explicitly below instead.
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'

$proj   = Join-Path $Root 'AI_Music'
$main   = Join-Path $Root 'AI_Music.venv'
$hf     = Join-Path $proj 'tools\hfvenv'
$base   = Join-Path $proj 'tools\python\cpython-3.12-windows-x86_64-none\python.exe'
$uv     = Join-Path $proj 'tools\uv\uv.exe'
$logDir = Join-Path $proj 'logs'
$stamp  = Get-Date -Format 'yyyyMMdd_HHmmss'
$log    = Join-Path $logDir "venv_repair_$stamp.log"
$newVenv = $main
$newRoot = $proj

New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $logDir | Out-Null
$env:UV_CACHE_DIR = Join-Path $proj '.uv-cache'

function Say($msg) {
    $line = "$msg"
    Write-Host $line
    Add-Content -LiteralPath $log -Value $line -Encoding UTF8
}

# Run a native exe with its output captured to temp files.  uv prints progress
# to stderr; PowerShell 5.1 would otherwise turn that into a mangled
# NativeCommandError block.  Returns the exit code.
function Run-Native($exe, [string[]]$argv) {
    $tmpO = [IO.Path]::GetTempFileName()
    $tmpE = [IO.Path]::GetTempFileName()
    $saved = $ErrorActionPreference
    # PowerShell 5.1 turns native stderr writes into NativeCommandError records;
    # silence that rendering, the text itself is captured in the temp files.
    $ErrorActionPreference = 'SilentlyContinue'
    & $exe @argv 1> $tmpO 2> $tmpE
    $code = $LASTEXITCODE
    $ErrorActionPreference = $saved
    foreach ($f in @($tmpO, $tmpE)) {
        Get-Content -LiteralPath $f -EA SilentlyContinue |
            Where-Object { $_ -ne '' } | ForEach-Object { Say ("    " + $_) }
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $f -Force -EA SilentlyContinue
    }
    return $code
}

function Find-OldPath([string]$text, [string]$needle) {
    if ([string]::IsNullOrEmpty($needle)) { return $false }
    if ($text.IndexOf($needle, [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase) -ge 0) { return $true }
    $slash = $needle.Replace('\', '/')
    return ($text.IndexOf($slash, [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase) -ge 0)
}

function Replace-OldPath([string]$text, [string]$old, [string]$new) {
    if ([string]::IsNullOrEmpty($old)) { return $text }
    $sb = New-Object System.Text.StringBuilder
    $pos = 0
    while ($true) {
        $i = $text.IndexOf($old, $pos, [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)
        if ($i -lt 0) { [void]$sb.Append($text.Substring($pos)); break }
        [void]$sb.Append($text.Substring($pos, $i - $pos)).Append($new)
        $pos = $i + $old.Length
    }
    return $sb.ToString()
}

Say "=== venv path repair - $stamp ==="
Say "root            : $Root"
Say "new venv path   : $newVenv"
Say "new project path: $newRoot"
Say "old venv path   : $OldVenv"
Say "old project path: $OldRoot"
Say "uv              : $uv ($(if (Test-Path $uv) { 'ok' } else { 'MISSING' }))"
Say "base interpreter: $base ($(if (Test-Path $base) { 'ok' } else { 'MISSING' }))"
if (-not (Test-Path $uv))   { throw "uv.exe not found at $uv" }
if (-not (Test-Path $base)) { throw "base python not found at $base" }
Say ""

# ---- 1. rebuild both venvs as relocatable (site-packages is preserved) ----
foreach ($venv in @($main, $hf)) {
    Say "--- uv venv --relocatable --allow-existing $(Split-Path -Leaf $venv) ---"
    $uvExit = Run-Native $uv @('venv', '--relocatable', '--allow-existing', '--python', $base, $venv)
    if ($uvExit -ne 0) { throw "uv venv failed for $venv (exit=$uvExit)" }
    $py = Join-Path $venv 'Scripts\python.exe'
    $probe = & $py -c "import sys;print(sys.executable);print(sys.prefix)" 2>&1 | Out-String
    Say ("    python.exe works: exit=$LASTEXITCODE " + ($probe.Trim() -replace "`r?`n", ' | '))
    Say ""
}

# ---- 2. text rewrite for anything still holding the old absolute paths ----
Say "--- text rewrite (old -> new) ---"
$old = @($OldVenv, $OldRoot)     # venv path first: it is the more specific one
$new = @($newVenv, $newRoot)
foreach ($venv in @($main, $hf)) {
    $files = @()
    $cfg = Join-Path $venv 'pyvenv.cfg'
    if (Test-Path $cfg) { $files += Get-Item $cfg }
    $files += Get-ChildItem (Join-Path $venv 'Scripts') -Force -File -EA SilentlyContinue |
        Where-Object { $_.Length -lt 262144 -and $_.Extension -notin '.exe', '.dll', '.pyd', '.lib' }
    foreach ($f in ($files | Sort-Object FullName -Unique)) {
        $t = [IO.File]::ReadAllText($f.FullName, [Text.Encoding]::UTF8)
        $stale = $false
        for ($i = 0; $i -lt $old.Count; $i++) {
            if (Find-OldPath $t $old[$i]) { $stale = $true; break }
        }
        if ($stale) {
            for ($i = 0; $i -lt $old.Count; $i++) { $t = Replace-OldPath $t $old[$i] $new[$i] }
            [IO.File]::WriteAllText($f.FullName, $t, (New-Object Text.UTF8Encoding($false)))
            Say ("    fixed " + $f.FullName)
        }
    }
}
Say ""

# ---- 3. regenerate console-script wrappers ----
if (-not $SkipReinstall) {
    # exact pins come from the freeze files, so versions cannot drift
    $freezeMain = Get-Content (Join-Path $Root 'env_freeze_main_venv.txt') -EA SilentlyContinue
    $freezeHf   = Get-Content (Join-Path $Root 'env_freeze_hfvenv.txt') -EA SilentlyContinue

    function Get-Pins($freeze, $names) {
        $out = @()
        foreach ($n in $names) {
            # match both `name==version` and `name @ file:///...`
            $hit = $freeze | Where-Object { $_ -match ('^' + [regex]::Escape($n) + '(\s*==|\s*@)') } | Select-Object -First 1
            if ($hit) { $out += $hit } else { Say "    ! no pin for $n (skipped)" }
        }
        return $out
    }

    $mainScriptPkgs = @(
        'accelerate', 'cffi', 'diffusers', 'numpy', 'fonttools', 'huggingface-hub',
        'httpx', 'httpx2', 'idna', 'sympy', 'charset-normalizer', 'tqdm',
        'transformers', 'numba', 'torch', 'torchaudio', 'torchvision'
    )
    $hfScriptPkgs = @('huggingface-hub', 'idna', 'charset-normalizer', 'tqdm')

    Say "--- regenerating console scripts (main venv) ---"
    $pins = Get-Pins $freezeMain $mainScriptPkgs
    Say ("    packages: " + ($pins -join ', '))
    $pipExit = Run-Native $uv (@('pip', 'install', '--python', (Join-Path $main 'Scripts\python.exe'),
        '--no-deps', '--reinstall', '--find-links', (Join-Path $proj 'wheels')) + $pins)
    if ($pipExit -ne 0) { Say "    ! uv pip install (main) exit=$pipExit" }
    Say ""

    Say "--- regenerating console scripts (hfvenv) ---"
    $pinsHf = Get-Pins $freezeHf $hfScriptPkgs
    Say ("    packages: " + ($pinsHf -join ', '))
    $pipExitHf = Run-Native $uv (@('pip', 'install', '--python', (Join-Path $hf 'Scripts\python.exe'),
        '--no-deps', '--reinstall') + $pinsHf)
    if ($pipExitHf -ne 0) { Say "    ! uv pip install (hfvenv) exit=$pipExitHf" }
    Say ""
}

# ---- 4. verification ----
Say "--- verification ---"
$mainPy = Join-Path $main 'Scripts\python.exe'
$check = & $mainPy -c "import sys, torch; print('exe     :', sys.executable); print('prefix  :', sys.prefix); print('python  :', sys.version.split()[0]); print('torch   :', torch.__version__); print('cuda    :', torch.cuda.is_available())" 2>&1
$check | ForEach-Object { Say ("    " + $_) }
Say ("    exit=$LASTEXITCODE")

$hfExe = Join-Path $main 'Scripts\hf.exe'
if (Test-Path $hfExe) {
    $hfOut = & $hfExe version 2>&1
    Say ("    hf.exe  : exit=$LASTEXITCODE " + (($hfOut | Select-Object -First 1) -join ''))
}
$torchrun = Join-Path $main 'Scripts\torchrun.exe'
if (Test-Path $torchrun) {
    $trOut = & $torchrun --version 2>&1
    Say ("    torchrun: exit=$LASTEXITCODE " + (($trOut | Select-Object -First 1) -join ''))
}
$hfPy = & (Join-Path $hf 'Scripts\python.exe') -c "import huggingface_hub;print('hfvenv hf_hub ' + huggingface_hub.__version__)" 2>&1
Say ("    hfvenv  : exit=$LASTEXITCODE " + (($hfPy -join ' | ')))

Say ""
Say "log: $log"


===== [66/66] verify_migration.ps1  (11223 bytes) =====
# Verify that the ACE-Step environment really works from its new location.
#
#   1. no binary/text file inside either venv still references the old paths
#   2. every console-script wrapper starts (trampolines are path bound on Windows)
#   3. python / torch / CUDA / the three CUDA wheels
#   4. model files are all present under the new path
#
# Usage: powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "G:\AIMUSIC\verify_migration.ps1"

param(
    [string]$Root = 'G:\AIMUSIC',
    [string[]]$OldPaths = @('D:\VS CODE\PROJECT\AIMUSIC\AI_Music.venv',
                            'D:\VS CODE\PROJECT\AIMUSIC\AI_Music',
                            'D:\AI_Music.venv', 'D:\AI_Music')
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
$proj = Join-Path $Root 'AI_Music'
$main = Join-Path $Root 'AI_Music.venv'
$hf   = Join-Path $proj 'tools\hfvenv'
$log  = Join-Path $proj 'logs\migration_verify.txt'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path (Join-Path $proj 'logs') | Out-Null

$r = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
function W($s) { $r.Add([string]$s); Write-Host $s }
function Find-OldPath([string]$text, [string]$needle) {
    if ([string]::IsNullOrEmpty($needle)) { return $false }
    if ($text.IndexOf($needle, [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase) -ge 0) { return $true }
    $slash = $needle.Replace('\', '/')
    return ($text.IndexOf($slash, [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase) -ge 0)
}

W "ACE-Step migration verification - $(Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss')"
W ("=" * 72)

# ---- 1. stale old paths ----
W "[1] files still referencing the old paths"
$hits = 0
$failures = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
function Add-Failure([string]$message) {
    $failures.Add($message)
    W ("    FAIL " + $message)
}
$scanExt = @('.exe', '.json', '.cfg', '.txt', '.pth', '.bat', '.cmd', '.ps1', '.sh', '.dat')
foreach ($venv in @($main, $hf)) {
    $cand = @()
    $cand += Get-ChildItem (Join-Path $venv 'Scripts') -Force -File -EA SilentlyContinue
    $cand += Get-ChildItem $venv -Recurse -Force -File -EA SilentlyContinue |
        Where-Object { $scanExt -contains $_.Extension.ToLower() -and $_.Length -lt 5MB }
    foreach ($f in ($cand | Sort-Object FullName -Unique)) {
        $bytes = [IO.File]::ReadAllBytes($f.FullName)
        $ascii = [Text.Encoding]::ASCII.GetString($bytes)
        foreach ($old in $OldPaths) {
            if ((Find-OldPath $ascii $old)) {
                W ("    STALE  " + $f.FullName)
                Add-Failure ("stale path: " + $f.FullName)
                $hits++
            }
        }
    }
}
if ($hits -eq 0) { W "    none - launchers, configs and metadata are clean" }
W ""

# ---- 1b. junctions (they store absolute targets, so a move breaks them) ----
W "[1b] junctions (absolute targets - these are what broke on the first move)"
$links = @(
    @{ Path = (Join-Path $proj '.venv');                                         Probe = 'Scripts\python.exe' },
    @{ Path = (Join-Path $proj 'tools\python\cpython-3.12-windows-x86_64-none'); Probe = 'python.exe' }
)
foreach ($l in $links) {
    $item = Get-Item $l.Path -Force -EA SilentlyContinue
    if (-not $item) { W ("    MISSING   " + $l.Path); Add-Failure ("missing junction: " + $l.Path); continue }
    if (-not ($item.Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint)) {
        W ("    NOT-LINK  " + $l.Path); Add-Failure ("not a junction: " + $l.Path); continue
    }
    $target = @($item.Target)[0]
    $resolves = Test-Path (Join-Path $l.Path $l.Probe)
    W ("    {0,-9} {1,-56} -> {2}" -f $(if ($resolves) { 'ok' } else { 'BROKEN' }),
        $l.Path.Replace($proj, '<proj>'), $target)
    if (-not $resolves) { Add-Failure ("broken junction: " + $l.Path) }
}
W ""

# ---- 2. console-script wrappers ----
W "[2] console-script wrappers (each one must load its package)"
$probeArgs = @{
    'hf.exe'              = @('version')
    'huggingface-cli.exe' = @('version')
    'torchrun.exe'        = @('--help')
    'torchfrtrace.exe'    = @('--help')
    'accelerate.exe'      = @('--help')
    'transformers.exe'    = @('--help')
    'diffusers-cli.exe'   = @('--help')
    'numpy-config.exe'    = @('--help')
    'fonttools.exe'       = @('--help')
    'isympy.exe'          = @('--version')
    'tqdm.exe'            = @('--help')
    'numba'               = @('--help')
    'idna.exe'            = @('decode', '--help')
    'httpx.exe'           = @('--help')
    'cffi-gen-src.exe'    = @('--help')
    'tiny-agents.exe'     = @('--help')
    'ttx.exe'             = @('--help')
    'pyftsubset.exe'      = @('--help')
    'pyftmerge.exe'       = @('--help')
    'normalizer.exe'      = @('--help')
    'httpx2.exe'          = @('--help')
    'f2py.exe'            = @('--help')
}
$okCount = 0; $badCount = 0
foreach ($venv in @($main, $hf)) {
    foreach ($name in ($probeArgs.Keys | Sort-Object)) {
        $exe = Join-Path $venv (Join-Path 'Scripts' $name)
        if (-not (Test-Path $exe)) {
            if ($venv -eq $main) { Add-Failure ("missing wrapper: " + $name) }
            continue
        }
        $tmpO = [IO.Path]::GetTempFileName(); $tmpE = [IO.Path]::GetTempFileName()
        $saved = $ErrorActionPreference; $ErrorActionPreference = 'SilentlyContinue'
        & $exe @($probeArgs[$name]) 1> $tmpO 2> $tmpE
        $code = $LASTEXITCODE
        $ErrorActionPreference = $saved
        $txt = ((Get-Content $tmpO -Raw -EA SilentlyContinue) + (Get-Content $tmpE -Raw -EA SilentlyContinue))
        Remove-Item $tmpO, $tmpE -Force -EA SilentlyContinue
        $tramp = $txt -match 'uv trampoline failed'
        if ($tramp) {
            $badCount++
            W ("    BROKEN " + (Split-Path -Leaf $venv) + "\$name  (trampoline error)")
            Add-Failure ("trampoline failure: " + (Split-Path -Leaf $venv) + "\$name")
        }
        elseif ($code -ne 0) {
            $badCount++
            W ("    FAILED " + (Split-Path -Leaf $venv) + "\$name  exit=$code")
            Add-Failure ("wrapper exit $code : " + (Split-Path -Leaf $venv) + "\$name")
        }
        else { $okCount++; W ("    ok     " + (Split-Path -Leaf $venv) + "\$name  exit=$code") }
    }
}
W "    -> $okCount working, $badCount broken"
W ""

# ---- 3. interpreter + GPU ----
W "[3] interpreter / torch / CUDA"
$py = Join-Path $main 'Scripts\python.exe'
$tmpO = [IO.Path]::GetTempFileName(); $tmpE = [IO.Path]::GetTempFileName()
$saved = $ErrorActionPreference; $ErrorActionPreference = 'SilentlyContinue'
& $py -c "import sys, torch, transformers, diffusers, soundfile, einops; print('exe      :', sys.executable); print('prefix   :', sys.prefix); print('python   :', sys.version.split()[0]); print('torch    :', torch.__version__, '(cuda', str(torch.version.cuda) + ')'); print('cuda ok  :', torch.cuda.is_available()); print('gpu      :', torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)); print('transformers:', transformers.__version__); print('diffusers:', diffusers.__version__); print('soundfile:', soundfile.__version__); print('einops   :', einops.__version__)" 1> $tmpO 2> $tmpE
$ErrorActionPreference = $saved
Get-Content $tmpO -EA SilentlyContinue | ForEach-Object { W ("    " + $_) }
(Get-Content $tmpE -EA SilentlyContinue) | Where-Object { $_ } | ForEach-Object { W ("    ERR " + $_) }
Remove-Item $tmpO, $tmpE -Force -EA SilentlyContinue
$tmpO = [IO.Path]::GetTempFileName(); $tmpE = [IO.Path]::GetTempFileName()
& $py -c "import torch,sys;sys.exit(0 if torch.cuda.is_available() else 3)" 1> $tmpO 2> $tmpE
$cudaExit = $LASTEXITCODE
if ($cudaExit -ne 0) { Add-Failure ("torch CUDA unavailable (exit=$cudaExit)") }
Remove-Item $tmpO, $tmpE -Force -EA SilentlyContinue
W ""

# ---- 4. CUDA wheel origin + model files ----
W "[4] installed CUDA wheels (must point at the new wheels dir)"
$durl = Get-Content (Join-Path $main 'Lib\site-packages\torch-2.7.1+cu128.dist-info\direct_url.json') -Raw -EA SilentlyContinue
W ("    torch direct_url: " + ($durl -replace '\s+', ' '))
W ""
W "[5] model files under the new path"
$models = Join-Path $proj 'models\Ace-Step1.5'
foreach ($rel in @('acestep-v15-turbo\model.safetensors', 'acestep-5Hz-lm-1.7B\model.safetensors',
                   'Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B\model.safetensors', 'vae\diffusion_pytorch_model.safetensors')) {
    $p = Join-Path $models $rel
    $state = if (Test-Path $p) { '{0:N1} MB' -f ((Get-Item $p).Length / 1MB) } else { 'MISSING' }
    W ("    {0,12}  {1}" -f $state, $rel)
    if (-not (Test-Path $p)) { Add-Failure ("missing model file: " + $rel) }
}
$all = Get-ChildItem $models -Recurse -Force -File -EA SilentlyContinue |
    Where-Object { $_.FullName -notlike '*\.cache\*' }
W ("    total: {0} files, {1:N1} MB" -f $all.Count, (($all | Measure-Object Length -Sum).Sum / 1MB))
W ""

# ---- 6. project sources: no stale paths + valid syntax ----
W "[6] project scripts"
$skip = '\\.venv\\|\\.uv-cache\\|\\.hf-cache\\|\\models\\|\\wheels\\|\\__pycache__\\|\\logs\\|\\AI_Music_code_bundle_[^\\]*\\.txt'
$srcFiles = Get-ChildItem $proj -Recurse -File -Force -EA SilentlyContinue |
    Where-Object { $_.FullName -notmatch $skip -and $_.Length -lt 3MB -and
                   $_.Extension -in '.py', '.ps1', '.md', '.txt', '.json', '.cfg', '.toml', '.bat', '.cmd' }
$stale = $srcFiles | Select-String -Pattern ([regex]::Escape($OldPaths[1])) -EA SilentlyContinue
if (($stale | Measure-Object).Count -eq 0) {
    W ("    {0} source files scanned - no stale absolute paths" -f $srcFiles.Count)
} else {
    Add-Failure ("stale old paths in " + (($stale | Group-Object Path).Count) + " source file(s)")
    foreach ($g in ($stale | Group-Object Path)) {
        W ("    STALE " + $g.Name.Replace($proj, '<proj>'))
        foreach ($m in $g.Group) { W ("        L{0}: {1}" -f $m.LineNumber, $m.Line.Trim()) }
    }
}
$pyFiles = Get-ChildItem (Join-Path $proj 'scripts') -Filter '*.py' -File -EA SilentlyContinue
$saved = $ErrorActionPreference; $ErrorActionPreference = 'SilentlyContinue'
& (Join-Path $main 'Scripts\python.exe') -m py_compile @($pyFiles.FullName) 2>&1 |
    ForEach-Object { W ("    PYERR " + $_) }
$pyExit = $LASTEXITCODE
$ErrorActionPreference = $saved
W ("    python syntax check: {0} file(s), exit={1}" -f $pyFiles.Count, $pyExit)
if ($pyExit -ne 0) { Add-Failure ("python syntax check failed (exit=$pyExit)") }
foreach ($ps in @((Join-Path $proj 'scripts\install_env.ps1'), (Join-Path $proj 'scripts\fetch_pytorch_wheels.ps1'),
                  (Join-Path $Root 'migrate_venv_paths.ps1'), (Join-Path $Root 'verify_migration.ps1'))) {
    if (-not (Test-Path $ps)) { continue }
    $err = $null
    [void][System.Management.Automation.Language.Parser]::ParseFile($ps, [ref]$null, [ref]$err)
    $parseErrors = ($err | Measure-Object).Count
    W ("    {0,-28} powershell parse errors = {1}" -f (Split-Path -Leaf $ps), $parseErrors)
    if ($parseErrors -gt 0) { Add-Failure ("PowerShell parse errors in " + (Split-Path -Leaf $ps)) }
}

if ($failures.Count -gt 0) {
    W ""
    W ("RESULT: FAILED (" + $failures.Count + " failure(s))")
    $r | Set-Content -LiteralPath $log -Encoding UTF8
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "log: $log"
    exit 1
}
W ""
W "RESULT: PASS"
$r | Set-Content -LiteralPath $log -Encoding UTF8
Write-Host ""
Write-Host "log: $log"
exit 0
